Last activity: 2026-08-10 — Completed quick task 260810-n3a (convo-list-kill-non-identity-sessions). Adds a **`Kill`** context-menu item to the pretty-conversations right-click / long-press menu that HARD-TERMINATES the underlying tmux session on the host via a new SSH-backed backend route. Scoped narrower than the existing `Deactivate` (which only removes the row from Ashley's active-set — the agent keeps running under the hood per Ashley's 2026-07-27 rule at `conversation-store.ts:1034`): Kill is only surfaced for rows where `!isRdp && !identity && row.targetTmuxSession != null && onKill` — i.e. NON-identity tmux sessions (throwaway agents where no `.md` identity file resolves for the row's targetTmuxSession) that aren't RDP. Identity rows are intentionally NOT killable from the context menu (they have real `/id save` state that shouldn't be nuked via a menu shortcut); RDP rows aren't tmux-backed. New endpoint `POST /host/:hostId/session/kill` (JSON body `{ tmuxSession }`) mounted in `src/backend/database/routes/host.ts` with `authenticateJWT + requireDataAccess` — NO `upload.single`/multipart middleware (identities-PUT silent-drop trap explicitly avoided; grep-verified). Handler flow mirrors `src/backend/database/routes/sessions.ts` verbatim for SSH plumbing: `connectOneShot(host, PER_HOST_KILL_TIMEOUT_MS=3000)` → try/finally `conn.end()` → `execCommand(conn, `tmux kill-session -t ${tmuxSession}`)`. **Security-critical gate:** `const TMUX_SESSION_ALLOWLIST = /^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$/` validated BEFORE any shell interpolation — rejects whitespace, quotes, `;`, `&`, `|`, `$`, backticks, path separators (test 3 explicitly asserts `foo; rm -rf /` payload → 400 with `connectOneShot` call count = 0). Missing-session on remote (matches /can't find session|session not found|no such session/i) treated as idempotent 204 (already-gone is the intended state). Errors NOT silently swallowed — every catch returns specific status + `{ error: <message> }` and logs at `sshLogger.error` with structured operation tag. `res.status(204).end()` on success; success also logs `sshLogger.info({ operation: "session_kill", hostId, tmuxSession, userId })`. Frontend: new `killTmuxSession(hostId, tmuxSession): Promise<void>` in `src/ui/api/sessions-api.ts` throws on non-2xx with `err.response?.data?.error` message. `AppShell.tsx:1508` new `onKillRow={async (row) => {...}}` closure — guards row.host + row.targetTmuxSession (defense-in-depth; row-side gate should prevent), `await killTmuxSession(parseInt(row.host.id,10), row.targetTmuxSession)` → `closeTab(row.id)` on success, `window.alert(err.message)` on failure (grep found no existing toast utility). `PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx` — new optional `onKillRow?: (row) => void|Promise<void>` prop (optional so tests can render panel without wiring) + `handleRowKill(row)` handler at ~L687 that calls `window.confirm(\`Kill tmux session "\${tmuxSession}" on \${hostName}? Last activity: 2026-08-08 — Completed quick task 260808-ohn (SessionHoldingOverlay flashes on session re-open — Layer 1 fast-path recycle detector refactor: swap `/exit` edge-triggered detection for `/id reset` tail-state-derived detection). Root cause: current Layer 1 (`claude-session-server.ts:1232-1255`) scanned each raw JSONL line for `line.includes('"content":"<command-name>/exit</command-name>')` gated by a per-connection `hasSeenExit` flag. Every WS reconnect (patch #344 hidden-pane WS pause reopens on visibility=true) restarts the tail with `-n +1` which replays every JSONL line from line 1. On replay `hasSeenExit` resets to false — any historical line containing the `/exit` marker bytes (Ashley's actual `/exit` user turns saved by the harness, or messages quoting the pattern) fires Layer 1 spuriously. Fix B (2026-07-30) then self-clears via same-file-active on the next 3s discovery-repoll tick — producing observed "instant appear + few-second flash + auto-disappear" symptom. Diagnosis proof: container log showed 14 arm+clear pairs on session `owGv_6oxMc7Sd5o8kzt3O` in ~1h; that session's JSONL grep-verified to contain 2 literal `/exit` marker byte matches (both were historical user `/exit` turns from Ashley in this very tanya conversation). Ashley's design (better than any of tanya's initial 3 fix shapes): (a) change detection target from `/exit` to `/id reset` — matches her actual recycle flow (`/exit` is unused; `/id reset` routes via the supervisor-sentinel signal-kill path, never puts `/exit` in the JSONL); (b) reframe as tail-state-derived — arm iff file's MOST RECENT user turn is `/id reset`. Historical `/id reset` lines superseded by a later user turn can never fire — kills the entire replay false-fire class by design. Handles her sometimes-freeform-explanation-after-reset case via prefix match on args (`<command-args>reset...`). Byte pattern: BOTH `<command-name>/id</command-name>` AND `<command-args>reset` on same raw JSONL line (`line.includes` — no JSON.parse). Extracted pure helpers to new sibling `layer1-detect.ts` (`isUserTurn`, `isIdResetUserTurn`, `applyLineToLayer1State` reducer + 28 unit tests). Rewired `onLine` at `claude-session-server.ts` to dispatch reducer actions to `transitionToHolding('id_reset')` / `transitionFromHoldingToActiveSameFile()`. Deleted `hasSeenExit` flag, the `/exit` include-check block, and the `"exit_marker"` branch of `transitionToHolding`'s reason union (now `"id_reset" | "discovery_diff"`). Layer 2 (discovery-repoll) byte-untouched — still the definitive recycle detector via new-UUID sessionFile appearance; `session_holding_cleared` + `session_changed` WS frames unchanged. Two atomic commits: `c9a485f` (Task 1: extract helpers + reducer + 28 unit tests, post-rebase from `3b723b3`) + `8a40b5a` (Task 2: rewire onLine + 10 integration tests + delete /exit path, post-rebase from `3ece6b3`). Byte gates verified: `hasSeenExit`/`"exit_marker"` code refs → 0 (only comments remain), `<command-name>/exit` → 0, `"id_reset"` → 5. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0, `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0, full `npx vitest run` = **1594 pass / 6 skip / 0 fail across 129 files** (+38 from this refactor; zero regressions). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. NOT pushed / NOT built / NOT deployed per code-work-doesn't-authorize-ship rule — awaiting ship greenlight (which Ashley subsequently gave). Bounty: `session-holding-layer1-detect-id-reset-not-exit`. Prior activity:

(4) Belt-and-suspenders: existing patch #345 first-live-frame frontend auto-dismiss stays as strict-safety fallback — marker approach is additive. (5) Natural-resume path preserved: when `wakeTriggerTs` is null (supervisor auto-woke via matrix_peek or schedule_peek, no user Wake click), skip the marker check entirely and fall through to patch #346's dismiss-on-sentinel-gone behavior. Last activity: 2026-08-08 — Completed + shipped quick task 260808-dmz (dormancy overlay hotfix — inactive-branch dormant probe). Patch #345 shipped earlier this session but never actually showed the overlay in practice — Ashley tested with Tiffany (dormant, `.dormant` sentinel present) and saw the "no active Claude session" fallback instead. Root cause: patch #345 piggybacked the dormancy stat check on the ACTIVE per-pane poll cycle (~claude-session-server.ts:3445 with `dormantInFlight` guard), but for actually-dormant panes `discoverClaudeSession` returns `{status:"inactive", reason:"not_claude"}` (no claude process in the pane_pid descendant walk), which triggers the short-circuit at lines 3484-3500 — emit one `{type:"inactive"}` frame, tear down SSH (`conn.end(); sshConn = null`), never start the poll cycle → dormancy check never runs → `{type:"dormant"}` never fires → DormancyOverlay never mounts. My mistake in #345: the design assumed dormancy layers on top of a live session poll, but dormancy IS the state of a session that isn't live. Fix moves the probe INTO the inactive branch and adds a dormant-poll loop. Verified via console-forward log at 2026-08-08T09:44:25Z showing `[SkynetLog] connection info tmux session attached: tiffany` fired fine (SSH tmux attach works for dormant panes) but the pretty-view pane emitted `messageCount:0`, `wsFramesSinceLast:0` — pretty-view mounted but backend never emitted anything after the initial inactive frame. Fix (`src/backend/claude-session/claude-session-server.ts`, atomic commit `acfdf55` + frontend belt-and-suspenders `b347bc1`): (1) extracted the "start active session tail + context-pct timer + all active-flow setup" block that ran after the inactive check into a `startActiveSessionFlow(conn, discovery)` helper — same behavior byte-for-byte, but callable from the dormant-poll transition. Bounty premise called this out explicitly: *"extract that block or call it as a helper — do NOT copy-paste"*. (2) In the inactive branch (line 3484), before teardown: identity-shape probe (`stat ~/.claude/identities/<tmuxSession>/`, cached per connection); if identity-shaped AND `.dormant` sentinel exists → emit `{type:"dormant", dormant:true}` instead of `{type:"inactive"}`, keep SSH connection alive (skip the `conn.end(); sshConn = null` teardown block), enter dormant-poll loop. (3) Dormant-poll loop: 3s cadence, `dormantPollInFlight` guard mirroring `contextPctInFlight`, reuses the kept-alive SSH conn (no new SSH open per tick); polls `.dormant` presence via a single `stat` exec each cycle; on sentinel-disappearance emits `{type:"dormant", dormant:false}` and retries `discoverClaudeSession`; if retry returns active → calls `startActiveSessionFlow(conn, activeResult)` which emits `{type:"session", pid, sessionFile}` and starts the tail (the `{type:"session"}` frame triggers PrettyView's onmessage-top auto-dismiss from patch #345, closing the overlay for free); if retry still returns inactive/not_claude → keeps polling (supervisor may not have reconciled yet, CHECK_INTERVAL is 30s default, wait for next cycle). (4) Wake handler already lives inside `ws.onmessage` and doesn't depend on the poll — verified reachable in dormant-poll state via new test coverage. (5) Kept patch #345's active-poll `dormantInFlight` piggyback as belt-and-suspenders per bounty constraint recommendation — defensive against a hypothetical supervisor race where `.dormant` reappears mid-active (shouldn't happen given supervisor invariants but cheap to keep). (6) Frontend belt-and-suspenders: `PrettyView.tsx:1575` inactive fallback now also gated on `!dormant` so it can't stomp the overlay even if backend somehow emits both (should be prevented structurally by fix #2 above, but explicit gate documents intent + catches future regressions). (7) Tag-along fix: diag registry pretty-view snapshot now reads `isVisible` from the mounted PrettyView instead of emitting `null` (patch #344 added the prop but the diag-registry snapshot wasn't updated; DIAG-REPORT logs pre-fix showed `isVisible: null` for pretty-view panes, `isVisible: true|false` for terminal panes). One-line change in the diag registry. Test coverage: extended existing `__applyDormantPollTickForTests` / `__applyWakeMessageForTests` seams from patch #345 with 5 new tests G-K covering (G) `.dormant` present + not_claude → emit dormant true, no teardown, poll loop starts; (H) sentinel disappears + retry finds claude → emit dormant false + transition to active flow via `startActiveSessionFlow`; (I) sentinel disappears + retry still not_claude → keeps polling; (J) wake message reachable in dormant-poll state → sentinel deleted via SSH exec, wake_result emitted; (K) SSH connection stays alive throughout dormant-poll cycle (no premature teardown). Full `npx vitest run` = **1585 pass / 6 skip / 0 fail** (baseline 1580+ from patch #345 maintained + 5 new). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. Shipped under 15-min deadman: rebased on origin, `docker compose build skynet`, `docker compose up -d --force-recreate skynet`, image `9364e84a8e67`, container healthy sustained, HTTPS 200 confirmed on `term.gigaashley.click`; new bundle `Terminal-96UG-f6O.js`; byte-verified `dormant`, `wake_result`, `"Session is asleep"` symbols present. Patch #346 entry appended to skynet-patches.md. Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `acfdf55` (backend restructure + tests) + `b347bc1` (frontend gate + diag tag-along). Bounty `dormancy-overlay-inactive-branch-fix` code+deploy complete pending Ashley UAT: open Tiffany's pretty-view pane in PWA → expect DormancyOverlay appears ("session is asleep" + Wake button) NOT the "no active Claude session" fallback → tap Wake → overlay transitions to "waking…" → within ~60s (supervisor CHECK_INTERVAL=30s + claude-launch + /id run) overlay auto-dismisses on first live Claude frame from the transition to active flow. Also expect DIAG-REPORT `isVisible: true` (not null) for pretty-view panes going forward. Prior activity:

Last activity: 2026-08-08 — Completed + shipped quick task 260808-cd6 (dormancy overlay + Wake button in PrettyView). agent-supervisor gained a DORMANCY mode 2026-08-07 (per session-dormancy-pilot-beelink); when idle > IDLE_THRESHOLD_MINUTES the supervisor sends /exit + drops sentinel at `~/.claude/identities/<name>/.dormant` + kills claude while keeping tmux alive at a bare shell prompt. Three wake paths exist in the supervisor: matrix_peek (its own /sync), schedule_peek (wakeup specs), and sentinel-delete (external actor rm's the file; supervisor's design calls this "FREE — no code, natural fallout"). This patch makes Skynet the external actor Ashley controls from her PWA — without it, once an identity goes dormant she has no way to talk to it. Shape (Ashley 2026-08-08 verbatim: *"we would need an overlay on pretty view like the existing session recycling overlay to say that the session went to sleep, and maybe button to wake it up, and composebox is disabled in the way lots of stuff already disables it, and that covers it, no display on convo list, PrettyView only"*): backend piggybacks a `stat ~/.claude/identities/<name>/.dormant` check on the existing per-pane 3s status poll in `src/backend/claude-session/claude-session-server.ts` (~line 3445, mirrors the `contextPctInFlight` guard exactly — new `dormantInFlight` guard + emit-only-on-change discipline matching the existing `harness_tasks` / `backgrounded_agents` frames). Identity-shape probe (`stat ~/.claude/identities/<name>/`) runs once per connection and caches the answer — non-identity panes skip dormancy forever on that connection, zero cost. New WS message types: `{type:"dormant", dormant:true|false}` from backend, `{type:"wake"}` from frontend → backend SSH exec `rm -f ~/.claude/identities/<name>/.dormant` on the target host → `{type:"wake_result", ok:true|false, error?:string}` back. Backend does NOT try to fast-poke the supervisor — CHECK_INTERVAL (default 30s) picks up the missing sentinel naturally on next reconcile. Wake handler security: ignores any hostId/tmuxSession in the payload, uses connection-scoped `currentTmuxSession` only (mirrors T-14-02-01 aside_arm pattern). Shell escape: single-quote wrapping (same pattern as `capture-pane` at line 3441); tmuxSession already frontend-validated to alphanumeric/dash/underscore. Visibility gating: inherited FREE from patch #344 (hidden pane → WS closed → no poll → no emit — dovetails with the just-shipped iteration 1 of hidden-pane cost mitigation). New frontend: `src/ui/features/pretty-view/DormancyOverlay.tsx` (mirrors SessionHoldingOverlay geometry, Moon glyph, "Session is asleep" copy, Wake button → transitions to "waking…" state → elapsed-seconds hint after 15s so it doesn't feel hung; warm-red error variant on `wake_result.ok=false` with retryable Wake button). ⚠️ Static glyph — never `animate-spin` — grep-gated in the test (motion-channel discipline; PWA has no fleet-wide animation contract yet). PrettyView.tsx wires: consumes `dormant` + `wake_result` WS messages, `dormantRef` mirror for stale-closure safety, live-frame auto-dismiss at TOP of onmessage handler before the switch (any incoming message/image/relay_*/context_pct/harness_tasks/session frame dismisses the overlay — that's the definitive signal recover-path succeeded + /id ran, no timing heuristic needed), ticker useEffect for elapsed-seconds hint, `handleWake` callback, DormancyOverlay mount when dormant, `dormantActive={dormant || waking}` prop on ComposeBox. ComposeBox.tsx: new `dormantActive: boolean` prop; wired at ALL ~15 disable sites 1:1 with existing `reconnectingActive` (Send/reset/ThumbsUp/Recap/queued-row-Send disabled; textarea/mic/attach live so Ashley can pre-draft while waking); Task 4 verified via `grep -c dormantActive == grep -c reconnectingActive` count-parity gate in the test suite. New test files: `DormancyOverlay.test.tsx` (renders, Wake button click fires callback, "waking…" state after click, elapsed hint after 15s, error variant, glyph-not-animated grep-gate) + `ComposeBox.dormant-disable.test.tsx` (parallel to the three existing `*-disable.test.tsx` files — recycle, reconnecting, plan-pending). Backend test: `dormant-poll.test.ts` covers the new poll path. **agent-supervisor UNTOUCHED** — sentinel-delete is path 3 (FREE) per its own 2026-08-07 design; Skynet integrates against the contract without modifying the supervisor. Last activity: 2026-08-08 — Completed quick task 260808-11l (context-% meter source-swap from tmux-pane scrape to JSONL session file). Motivation: Ashley reported the tmux pane occasionally collapses to ~2 chars wide on her mobile PWA — the statusline's leading pct chars remain but the surrounding bar glyphs (`█`/`░`) that `parseContextPct` AND-anchors against get truncated, so the parser returns null and the pretty-view context meter freezes at its last known value. Empirical validation across 8 live sessions (all Opus 4.7, 1M window) showed JSONL reconstruction from the last `type:"assistant"` turn's `message.usage.{input_tokens + cache_creation_input_tokens + cache_read_input_tokens}` sum, divided by 1_000_000, normalized by the same 16.5% autocompact buffer the statusline uses, matches CC's reported number within ~0.5% every time; displayed integer matches 5/8 exact + 3/8 off by ±1 (rounding-order artifact, not a math disagreement — CC rounds `remaining_percentage` to integer before normalizing, my reconstruction normalizes floats then rounds only at display). Sub-1% precision is well within the 80% context-watch nudge threshold's practical window. Mapping mechanic (already existed in Skynet — no new discovery needed): `src/backend/claude-session/session-file-discovery.ts` runs at pane connect via `discoverClaudeSession()`, reads `~/.claude/sessions/<claudePid>.json` (Claude Code v2.1.150+ writes this file with `sessionId + cwd + status`), constructs `~/.claude/projects/<slugified-cwd>/<sessionId>.jsonl`. Result tracked in `currentSessionFile` closure-local in claude-session-server.ts:1097 (plan referenced `state.currentSessionFile` — executor correctly used the actual scope, functionally identical). PID-keyed → cwd-collision-safe (bounty `pretty-view-shows-wrong-session-jsonl` already solved this class of bug). Fix: new helper `src/backend/claude-session/context-pct-from-jsonl.ts` (156 lines) exporting `readContextPctFromJsonl(conn, sessionFile) → Promise<number | null>`: single SSH exec `tail -c 10000 '<sessionFile>'`, reverse-scan lines for first `type:"assistant"` with `message.usage`, compute + normalize + round + clamp to [0,100]. Returns null on exec error / no valid turn found / JSON parse failure of every candidate. Constants: `AUTO_COMPACT_BUFFER_PCT = 16.5` (mirrors `~/.claude/hooks/gsd-statusline.js:314`), `MODEL_CONTEXT_WINDOW = 1_000_000` (Ashley 2026-08-08 verbatim: *"every model available in the harness is a million tokens window"*), 3000ms timeout (matches session-file-discovery). Poll callback swap in `claude-session-server.ts` around line 3435-3465: wrapped the 3s poller body as an async IIFE that tries JSONL FIRST when `currentSessionFile` is non-null, falls back to the existing `tmux capture-pane` + `parseContextPct(output)` path when JSONL returns null. All existing guards preserved (`stopped` / `ws.readyState` / `!sshConn` / `contextPctInFlight` / `.finally(() => { contextPctInFlight = false })`). `context-pct-parser.ts` and its tests preserved byte-identical (125 lines) as the fallback path — fresh sessions with no assistant turn yet, or JSONL read failures, still get a meter number. Frontend untouched — same `{type:"context_pct", pct}` WS message shape at 3s cadence. Tests: new `context-pct-from-jsonl.test.ts` (224 lines, 8 cases): (a) happy path with the empirical fixture (usage sum 198542 → normalized displayed 24, matches my 2026-08-08 live measurement for session e222905c); (b) no assistant turn in tail → null; (c) assistant turn missing `message.usage` → null; (d) execCommand throws → null; (e) boundary: remaining_pct exactly 16.5 → displayed 100; (f) over-full (>1M tokens) → clamped to 100; (g) multiple assistant turns → picks the LAST one; (h) malformed JSON line between valid turns doesn't break the reverse scan. Last activity: 2026-08-05 — Shipped inline fix (no GSD): `fix(conversation-store): clear sessionStorage-inherited activeSet when only=1 in URL hash — stops Move-to-new-window from bringing along origin-window active rows` (commit `63bf0a8` on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`). Fifth atomic ship this session; fourth in the global-files-modal chain (70q→7rq→53a36a6→ea0098c→**63bf0a8**). Ashley UAT: after ea0098c dropped `noopener` from Move-to-new-window's `window.open` so the popup-blocker null-check would work, new windows opened with the ORIGIN window's whole activeSet in tow (e.g. Tina + Nelly active in origin → right-click Nelly → Move-to-new-window → new window has BOTH Tina and Nelly active instead of just Nelly). Root cause: browsers copy sessionStorage from opener to child on same-origin `window.open()` when `noopener` is absent (spec-guaranteed "session cloning"). The conversation-store's `ACTIVE_SET_STORAGE_KEY = "pv-conv-active-set"` (patch #137 per-window session persistence) was inherited on the child's first module-import — `hydrateActiveSetFromStorage()` read a set that belonged to a different window. The URL's `only=1` flag (patch #34/#35) was already gating persisted-tabs restore at `AppShell.tsx:808` but NOT the sessionStorage-inherited activeSet. Pre-ea0098c that worked because `noopener` isolated the sessionStorage; post-ea0098c the guard had to move into the hydrator itself. Fix: `hydrateActiveSetFromStorage()` now parses `location.hash` at the top with `URLSearchParams` (strict param match, NOT substring — regression-guarded), and if `only=1` is present it removes the storage key AND returns an empty Set. AppShell's URL-restore path (patch #230) still enrolls URL-supplied tabs into the active set via its normal enroll logic — new window ends up with only the URL's tabs active, matching what `only=1` was designed to promise. Rejected alternatives: (a) re-adding `noopener` would prevent inheritance but revert ea0098c's deactivate mechanism (window.open w/ noopener always returns null); (b) broader sessionStorage-wide clear on only=1 would nuke the `tab-url.ts` pending-workspace stash which INTENTIONALLY relies on the same sessionStorage-inheritance for its own long-URL fallback — targeted key-specific clear leaves that mechanism intact. Tests: 4 new cases in `conversation-store.test.ts` each using `vi.resetModules()` + dynamic re-import to force a fresh module-scope hydrate (mirrors the patch #137 module-init test pattern) — (1) only=1 + pre-seeded storage → empty Set + storage cleared (fresh-slate assertion), (2) hash without only param + pre-seeded storage → stored values hydrate unchanged (patch #137 regression guard), (3) empty hash + pre-seeded storage → stored values hydrate unchanged (edge-case regression), (4) hash containing substring "only" as URL VALUE (`tab=lonely`) but no `only` param → stored values unchanged (strict-parse regression guard catches naive substring refactor). Verification: `npx vitest run conversation-store.test.ts` = **78/78 pass**; full suite `npx vitest run` = **1424 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 118 files** (+4 vs uo4-noopener-fix's 1420/6/118, zero regressions). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. Shipped: image `10f9397ed4a0` built + pushed to origin + `docker compose up -d --force-recreate skynet` → healthy in 10s. Byte gates: AppShell bundle rotated `AppShell-DsHNkVUq.js` → `AppShell-CvtgaK_j.js` (new frontend in-container); `grep "pv-conv-active-set" /app/html/assets/AppShell-CvtgaK_j.js` = 1 hit (guard's removeItem path compiled). Deploy queue: 3371233 (uo4-noopener-fix docs) → +1 code commit (`63bf0a8`) → image `10f9397ed4a0` NOW LIVE. Full session fix-chain arc: quick 70q (unhide filter button + fix read tilde) → quick 7rq (fix modal load race) → inline 53a36a6 (fix write handler tilde) → inline ea0098c (drop noopener) → inline 63bf0a8 (guard sessionStorage bleed). Global-files editing feature end-to-end WORKING as of this ship; Move-to-new-window / Open-in-new-window end-to-end WORKING as of this ship.

Last activity: 2026-08-05 — Shipped inline fix (no GSD, Ashley greenlit "let's go" on the diagnose-then-inline pattern): `fix(pretty-conv-row-uo4): drop 'noopener' from Move-to-new-window window.open call — noopener always returns null, defeating the popup-blocker null-check` (commit `ea0098c` on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`). Ashley UAT of quick-260804-uo4 2026-08-05 caught the bug: "Move to new window" from the sidebar context menu opened the new window correctly but left the origin tab active (the deactivate never fired). Root cause: `PrettyConversationRow.tsx:619` had `const w = window.open("#" + payload, "_blank", "noopener");` followed by `if (w !== null && inActiveSet) onDeactivate?.();`. Per HTML spec, `window.open()` with the `noopener` feature ALWAYS returns null (so the new window can't access `window.opener`) — meaning `w !== null` was always false and `onDeactivate` never fired regardless of whether the popup opened or was blocked. The original 260804-uo4 commit's stated intent for the null-check was popup-blocker safety ("if the window was blocked, the original tab survives") but `noopener` defeated exactly that mechanism. Slipped past UO1-UO6 because their `window.open` mock returned a truthy Window regardless of the features arg. Fix: drop `"noopener"` from the features string. Same-origin Skynet↔Skynet, both windows are our own trusted code — the threat model `noopener` guards against (untrusted popup mutating opener via `window.opener.location`) doesn't apply here. Post-fix, `w` is a real Window handle on success and `null` on block — null-check regains intended meaning. Comment block updated to document the trap + tell future maintainers explicitly NOT to re-add `"noopener"` here. Test: new **UO4b regression guard** in `PrettyConversationRow.test.tsx` — mocks `window.open` to return truthy Window, clicks Move-to-new-window, asserts (a) `window.open` features arg is either absent or does NOT contain `"noopener"` (the load-bearing assertion — anything else and every real-browser click leaves origin tab active regardless of what the null-check logic says), (b) `onDeactivate` DID fire (mirrors UO1's contract to prove the regression this test protects would silently break UO1's real-browser behavior without failing UO1 in vitest which doesn't enforce the noopener→null spec). UO1-UO6 unchanged. Verification: `npx vitest run PrettyConversationRow.test.tsx` = **41/41 pass**; full suite `npx vitest run` = **1420 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 118 files** (+1 vs write-fix's 1419/6/118, zero regressions). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. Shipped: image `6f8f41626bb2` built + pushed to origin + `docker compose up -d --force-recreate skynet` → healthy in 10s. Byte gates: AppShell bundle rotated `AppShell-CFWDHV7h.js` → `AppShell-DsHNkVUq.js` (new frontend in-container); `grep noopener /app/html/assets/AppShell-*.js` = **0 hits** (confirms the argument was removed from the shipped bundle). Deploy queue: 064fe3c (write-fix docs) → +1 code commit (`ea0098c`) → image `6f8f41626bb2` NOW LIVE. Fourth atomic ship this session (70q → 7rq → 53a36a6 write-fix → ea0098c uo4-noopener-fix). No `.planning/quick/` — inline no-GSD ship matching write-fix pattern.

Last activity: 2026-08-05 — Shipped inline fix (no GSD, Ashley "skip GSD this time"): `fix(global-files-write): move tilde-expansion above mtime pre-check to stop false 409 on every save` (commit `53a36a6` on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`). Same class of bug quick-260805-70q fixed on POST /read, but on PUT /write. The write handler had its own tilde-expansion block at step 6 that correctly resolved `~/foo` → `$HOME/foo` before the SFTP atomic write, BUT three surrounding shell commands used `shellEscape(filePath)` on the un-expanded path: (1) step 5 mtime pre-check `stat -c '%Y' '~/…'` → single-quoted `~` doesn't expand → returns 0 → `0 !== expectedMtime` → 409 mismatch on every save; (2) step 5 currentContent cat for the 409 payload `cat '~/…'` → returns empty → modal's reload-and-retry UX overwrites tab state with `{content:"", mtime:0}` → GlobalFileTab hits `!content && mtime===0` empty-branch → renders "No content in this file yet" (exact symptom Ashley hit during 7rq UAT); (3) step 8 post-write re-stat → same → newMtime=0 → even if #1 fixed, next save would false-409 with expectedMtime=0. Fix: relocate the existing tilde-expansion block up to right after SSH connect (now step 5, before the mtime pre-check), declare `escapedPath = shellEscape(absPath)` once — all three broken sites use escapedPath so one relocation + one arg change repairs them together; SFTP write already used absPath. Also fixed misleading step-8 comment that claimed `stat` works with either tilde or absolute form (single-quoted `~` doesn't expand — same trap the read handler already learned). Tests: extended `src/backend/database/routes/global-files-read-write.test.ts` with 4 write-handler cases mirroring the read-handler shape — W1 tilde save w/ matching expectedMtime asserts echo $HOME fires ONCE + every stat/cat uses resolved absolute path + SFTP write called w/ absolute + 200 returns REAL new mtime (regression guard on step-8 poisoning next save); W2 absolute path save skips echo $HOME + uses original path; W3 unresolvable HOME → 502 BEFORE mtime pre-check (regression guard on the false-409 root cause); W4 real mtime mismatch on tilde path → 409 with currentContent read from resolved absolute path (real content flows, NOT "" that would land in the modal's dead-end textarea replacement). Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0; targeted `npx vitest run global-files-read-write.test.ts` = **8/8 pass** (4 read + 4 write); full suite `npx vitest run` = **1419 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 118 files** (+4 vs 7rq's 1415/6/118, zero regressions). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. Shipped: image `24af668741f6` built + pushed to origin + `docker compose up -d --force-recreate skynet` → healthy in 10s. Live-smoke: read thenasty `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` (3382 bytes, mtime 1785803393) → write same content w/ expectedMtime=1785803393 → 200 with real new mtime 1785910125 (NOT 0); re-read post-write returns 3382 bytes byte-identical (no corruption from the smoke's round-trip; only mtime bumped as expected). Deploy queue extends from 9bf76eb (7rq docs) → +1 commit (`53a36a6`) → image `24af668741f6` NOW LIVE. Explicit non-goals still deferred: item 2 default-to-one-host picker tweak (pending a-or-b decision), empty-branch fallthrough in GlobalFileTab.tsx (still shows "No content in this file yet" for legitimately-empty files — separate change), skynet-patches.md bump (orchestrator handles). No `.planning/quick/` directory this ship — Ashley explicitly skipped GSD.

Last activity: 2026-08-05 — Completed quick task 260805-7rq: fix pre-existing Phase 23 React useEffect race in `src/ui/features/pretty-view/GlobalFilesModal.tsx:143`. Ashley's UAT of the 70q ship (2 commits shipped in image `bb99f307bcb4`) surfaced a symptom: modal spins forever on thenasty read even though nginx access log showed `POST /global-files/read` returning 200 with 3507 bytes in ~1s. Root cause: the lazy-load useEffect had `tabData` in its deps array — sequence was (1) effect fires on activeTab change → `setTabData({loading})` mutates tabData reference → (2) React re-runs effect (tabData in deps) → cleanup from (1) sets `cancelled = true` → (3) new effect body sees `tabData.has(activeTab)` truthy (from step 1's loading placeholder) → early-returns without new cleanup → (4) original `readGlobalFile` promise resolves ~700ms later → sees `cancelled = true` → never calls `setTabData(ready)` → spinner forever. Pre-70q-tilde-fix, the pre-fix backend returned empty content near-instantaneously so the promise resolved in the same microtask batch as `setTabData(loading)` and React collapsed them into one commit — dodging the race. My 70q tilde-expansion fix made the SSH round-trip a real ~700ms, exposing the pre-existing bug. TDD fix: RED regression test in new `src/ui/features/pretty-view/GlobalFilesModal.test.tsx` mocks `readGlobalFile` with `setTimeout(50)` macrotask delay + asserts `screen.getByRole("textbox").value === "MOCKED FILE CONTENT"` (positive READY assertion, not just "loading gone"); test failed against unpatched code with `Expected: true, Received: null` (textarea never rendered, spinner stayed indefinitely) — race reproduction confirmed. GREEN fix: dropped `tabData` from the deps array (`[selectedHostId, activeTab, tabData]` → `[selectedHostId, activeTab]`), added `// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps` with WHY comment explaining the stale-closure intent. The `tabData.has(activeTab)` gate inside the effect is intentionally a "have we started/finished loading this tab" check meaningful at effect-run time (host/tab change); stale-closure read is desired semantics. Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `5c88555 test(quick-260805-7rq-01)` (RED regression) + `4a2310e fix(quick-260805-7rq-01)` (GREEN dep drop). Verification: `npm run build` EXIT 0 in 4.54s; full `npx vitest run` = **1415 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 118 files** (+1 new race test vs 70q ship's 1414/6/117, zero regressions). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. Backend-only build skipped (frontend-only change per plan). Shipped: image `f6d1c760cff1` built + pushed to origin + `docker compose up -d --force-recreate skynet` → healthy in 30s; AppShell bundle hash changed `AppShell-LN847MEC.js` → `AppShell-CFWDHV7h.js` confirming new frontend in-container. **Also this session (data change, no code commit): updated `/var/lib/docker/volumes/skynet_skynet-data/_data/global-files.json`** to cover every non-RDP fleet host per Ashley 2026-08-05: *"I should be able to edit the user wide clod file from every host that is currently in this instance. And that represents the set of global files that I want to have editable in this instance."* Added termix, GIGAASHLEYPC, ashley-laptop, laura-mac, lisa-mac, aither-cloud2-prod (6 new); removed stale entries whose hostname keys no longer match any fleet host (skynet-ec2, aither-cloud, aither-cloud2, aither-sftp — those 4 were dead entries the fleet had renamed away from). Deploy queue extends from `3b5e076` (docs) → +2 commits (`5c88555` + `4a2310e`) → image `f6d1c760cff1` NOW LIVE. Explicit non-goals still deferred: item 2 default-to-one-host picker tweak in GlobalFilesModal.tsx (pending Ashley's a-or-b decision on picker restriction semantics), empty-branch fallthrough in GlobalFileTab.tsx, skynet-patches.md bump (orchestrator handles).

Prior activity: 2026-08-02 — Completed quick task 260802-rps: replace patch #63 JSONL scan for `ExitPlanMode` with a tmux pane screen-scrape (new `plan-pending-parser.ts` pure helper + wire-in on the same setInterval that already runs `parseContextPct`) so `PlanPendingBubble` renders live while a plan is pending. Root cause (proven live on Moxie/workstation 2026-08-02): Claude Code 2.1.150's `ExitPlanModeV2Tool` buffers the `ExitPlanMode` tool_use in Ink UI memory and only flushes to the parent JSONL when the user resolves the approval prompt — 57-minute gap directly observed between model call and JSONL write on Moxie (Ashley approved, ExitPlanMode tool_use then appeared with its original 18:56:52 timestamp on line 593). The fleet-wide 2.1.150 pin didn't guard against this because the change was an Anthropic-side model-behavior shift (deferred-tool loading via `ToolSearch(select:ExitPlanMode)` → `ExitPlanMode(...)`) coupled with Claude Code choosing V2's buffered-persistence path for that flow — neither is version-gated on a `npm install`-pinnable dimension. Fingerprint (mirrors `context-pct-parser.ts`'s bottom-anchor rationale): bottom-30-lines slice MUST contain the load-bearing `No, keep planning` reject-option marker (present in every plan-approval variant regardless of `--dangerously-skip-permissions` — that flag only changes options 1/2, never the last "keep planning" option), AND anywhere in the pane one of `Here is Claude's plan:` or `Ready to code?` (the two header variants — bypass-permissions and default) must appear. Both required — either alone false-positives on prose/transcript quotes. Bottom-anchoring is what keeps prose-mentions of "No, keep planning" elsewhere in the scrollback from tripping the detector. WS message shape (`{type:"plan_pending", pending}`) UNCHANGED; `planFilePath: ""` because pane text doesn't carry it reliably and `PlanPendingBubble.tsx` doesn't consume that field for rendering. Frontend handler + component untouched — pure backend detection swap. Legacy patch #63 JSONL scan (~L1200-1270 in `claude-session-server.ts`) LEFT IN PLACE with a deprecation comment block documenting the V2 discovery; scan is now harmless belt-and-suspenders (V2 flush re-emits pending:null at resolution edge, coalesces cleanly with the pane-scrape's own null-emit) and preserves backward-compat with any older Claude Code sessions still writing ExitPlanMode eagerly. Prior activity: 2026-08-02 — Completed quick task 260802-qrw: fix identity-modal save failure — swap `sftp.rename` → `sftp.ext_openssh_rename` in `writeMarkdownFileAtomic`. Closes bounty `identity-modal-save-sftp-rename-eexist` (high-priority, inbound from Stacy's ceo-skynet handoff at `~/pretty-view-uploads/2026-08-02/190204-TINA-HANDOFF.md`). Root cause identified by @stacy with full OpenSSH-source trace on ceo-skynet, confirmed identical on skynet-ec2 (same OpenSSH 9.6p1 Ubuntu-3ubuntu13.18, same ssh2 1.17.0, same env/mount config, same buggy code — my HEAD was 26 commits ahead of Stacy's `cd95a61` tip but zero commits touched `identity-artifact-reader.ts`). Bug: SFTPv3 `SSH_FXP_RENAME` cannot atomically overwrite — OpenSSH's `process_rename` tries `link(old, new)` first, `link()` returns `EEXIST` when target exists, and `errno_to_portable()` has no case for `EEXIST` so it falls through to `SSH2_FX_FAILURE` (empty error string on the wire, client sees generic `Error: Failure` with `code: 4`). Ashley confirmed all her modal-save attempts have been on EXISTING identities (`/id` creates the file before you can talk to that identity, so first-write can't happen via the modal) — meaning per the bug analysis every save should be failing, yet she reported "sometimes it works and sometimes it doesn't." That "sometimes works" pattern remains unexplained by any theory tested this session; fix supersedes the mystery (all saves should succeed post-swap). Fix: `sftp.ext_openssh_rename` uses the `posix-rename@openssh.com` extension with POSIX `rename(2)` semantics (atomic overwrite allowed); every OpenSSH ≥5.1 (2008+) supports it by default. ONE helper swap fixes ALL four consumers — `writeIdentityFile` + `writeIdentityHistory` + `writeIdentityHandoff` + `writeIdentityBountyFields` all route through `writeMarkdownFileAtomic`. Scope-excluded: `src/backend/ssh/pretty-view-upload.ts:239` also uses `sftp.rename` but renames onto collision-resolved non-existent paths only (via `resolveNonCollidingFinal` + `reservedFinalPaths` batch guard) — EEXIST can't hit it in practice, so left alone. Executor TDD: RED regression test written FIRST with a throwing `rename` spy that fires the diagnostic `must not call sftp.rename — use ext_openssh_rename` on regression; ran RED (test fails against unmodified code, confirming trap is load-bearing), then GREEN (swap call site + rewrite JSDoc prologue with root-cause paragraph), then all gates green. Deviations: none — executed exactly as planned. One atomic commit on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `042235e`. Verification: `npm run build:backend` PASS (backend patch — Tina's learned rule: `tsc --noEmit` alone doesn't catch backend TS errors, `build:backend` is the strictest local check); `npm run build` PASS; `npx vitest run src/backend/claude-session/identity-artifact-reader` = **6 files / 26 tests / 0 failures** (existing 23 + 3 new regression tests in `identity-artifact-reader.remote-writes.test.ts`); grep gate `grep -c "sftp\.rename(" src/backend/claude-session/identity-artifact-reader.ts` = 0 executable call sites (JSDoc references to the old API preserved for pedagogical value). NOT pushed, NOT built (image), NOT deployed per code-work-doesn't-authorize-ship rule (Ashley 2026-07-27). Ships as patch #268 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. Deploy queue extends to `2318460` (patch #267 row+tokens+CSS) + `8c9ea5e` (patch #267 panel+tests) + `042235e` (patch #268); all HELD until Ashley's ship word. Downstream: Stacy will pull upstream on the next normal fork-pull cycle (she also has a separate ceo-skynet compose fix planned — IDENTITIES_LOCAL_HOST_IDS env + bind-mount — but that's her side, not mine). OPEN QUESTION carried in bounty timeline (not blocking): the "sometimes works" pattern for existing-file overwrites remains unexplained; fix will hide the symptom, revisit only if Ashley cares about the mechanism. Rebase risk: nil — `identity-artifact-reader.ts` is fork-authored (Phase 18).

Prior activity: 2026-08-02 — Completed quick task 260802-pq2: replace mobile swipe-to-reveal with long-press context menu on conversation rows. Closes pinned bounty `swipe-actions-visible-through-translucent-rows` — Ashley's original ask was to fix the visual bleed-through where the swipe-reveal strip painted Pin/Hide/Deactivate buttons behind translucent ambient/hidden rows. Discussion pivoted to the more consistent long-press → context menu model (mirrors desktop right-click), which eliminates the bleed class entirely by not painting anything behind rows. Completes an intent already spec'd in the code (`PrettyConversationRow.tsx:429` comment: "to hide, long-press → menu"). Reuses the existing `PrettyConversationContextMenu` component (portal-mounted, hue-aware, viewport-clamped) with the same `items[]` builder desktop uses — mobile and desktop now share a single Pin/Unpin, Hide/Unhide, Deactivate-if-active-set menu (no item-set divergence). Prior activity: 2026-07-31 — Completed quick task 260731-r81: fix "Server connection lost, recovering…" toast that fired when the pretty-view speak button was used on a long assistant message. Ashley bounty-driven (`speak-button-long-message-triggers-connection-lost-popup` — pinned, verbatim parked earlier this session via `/bounty`: "'Server connection lost, recovering…' popup happens when i try to use speak button on a long message"). Root cause diagnosed: both the frontend axios call in `postSpeak` (bound to `authApi`'s default 30s timeout at `main-axios.ts:306`) and the backend `handleSpeak` AbortController (`src/backend/database/routes/voice.ts:147`, `setTimeout(..., 30_000)`) timed out before Chatterbox TTS finished synthesizing long text — one path throws `code=ECONNABORTED` "timeout of 30000ms exceeded" (client trips first), the other returns 504 `{error: "TTS timeout"}` (backend trips first). Either path lands in `main-axios.ts:530`'s response interceptor which calls `dbHealthMonitor.reportDatabaseError()`; the `isBackendUnreachable` predicate matches BOTH `errorCode === "ECONNABORTED"` and `lowerMessage.includes("timeout")`, so it emits `database-connection-degraded` → `AppShell.tsx:646` renders toast `common.connectionDegraded` = "Server connection lost, recovering…" (`locales/en.json:115`), which sticks until any other request succeeds. The 30s cap was inherited unthinkingly from `handleTranscribe`'s T-16-02 STT timeout when patch #223 added TTS support last session — STT clips are small + fast, TTS synthesis scales with text length, and `SPEAK_TEXT_MAX = 25000` chars can take minutes at Chatterbox's rate. Vehicle sizing per ROADMAP check: no `speak-button-connection-lost` phase in ROADMAP.md, ~10 lines of code across two files with no design decisions → `/gsd:quick` (not plan-phase, not ad-hoc — the two-file cross-tree scope + typecheck + full-suite value justify the ceremony). Fix (two atomic code commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`): `d7ab821` bumps `handleSpeak`'s AbortController from `30_000` → `300_000` ms (5 min) with an inline comment explaining the 10× asymmetry vs `handleTranscribe`; `c8f75ae` adds explicit `timeout: 300_000` to `postSpeak`'s axios config object overriding `authApi`'s 30s default, with a matching inline comment. Deliberately NOT touched (per plan leave-alone list, verified via `git diff --name-only HEAD~2 HEAD`): `handleTranscribe` (still 30s — STT scope preserved), `handleListVoices` (still 30s), `getVoices` (still uses authApi 30s default), `main-axios.ts` global default, `db-health-monitor.ts::isBackendUnreachable` predicate, all locale files, all AppShell toast wiring, `ChatMessage.speak.test.tsx` + `IdentityModal.voice.test.tsx` (both mock `postSpeak` at the module boundary so the timeout value isn't observed — remained green untouched). Sanity greps corroborate: `setTimeout(..., 30_000)` count in `voice.ts` = 2 (handleTranscribe + handleListVoices only), `setTimeout(..., 300_000)` count = 1 (handleSpeak), `timeout: 300_000` in `voice-api.ts` = 1 (postSpeak only), zero timeout key added to `getVoices`. Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 per patch #154 mandatory-for-backend-touching rule; `npm run build` EXIT 0 (frontend bundle 4.28s); full `npx vitest run --reporter=verbose` = **947 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 82 files** — matches the `8e01c93` baseline exactly (no regressions); independent grep of `/tmp/r81-vitest.log` for `FAIL|failed|✗` per tina.md L508 preference (#209→#211 arc) — 17 hits all benign (application-level `[INFO]` log lines from tests that intentionally exercise failure paths + `--reporter=verbose` printing test names containing the word "failed" + expected `Network Error` lines from voice-api error-handling suites); zero `Unhandled|Rejection` matches outside expected test scopes. Two atomic commits scope-clean: `git show --name-only d7ab821` = 1 file (`src/backend/database/routes/voice.ts`), `git show --name-only c8f75ae` = 1 file (`src/ui/api/voice-api.ts`) — no identity-side files touched (per h8f-vs-hn8 executor-scope split, Tina updates skynet-patches.md + bounty archive AS ORCHESTRATOR after this row); no git worktrees used (fleet rule 2026-07-31 verified via `workflow.use_worktrees=false` in gsd config); no push, no `docker build`, no `docker compose up --force-recreate` — stopped at commit boundary per fleet rule (Ashley 2026-07-27). Ships as patch #231 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. Closes pinned bounty `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/speak-button-long-message-triggers-connection-lost-popup/` — status stays `in_progress` + `pinned` per through-line rule until it archives on batch deploy (observable fix requires the container to carry the new bundle; #231's fix only takes effect post-recreate). Last activity (prior): 2026-07-30 — Completed quick task 260730-sjf: fixed the session-recycling overlay firing spuriously on host-reachability trouble. Ashley bounty-driven (`recycling-overlay-fires-when-host-unreachable` — parked this session via `/bounty`, greenlit as next after diagnosis). Two categorical fixes shipped in one arc, motivated by root cause that the Layer-2 discovery repoll's `.then()` callback in `claude-session-server.ts` conflated "we couldn't reach the host to ask" with "we asked and Claude isn't there" — both funneled into `transitionToHolding("discovery_diff")`. **Fix A (commit `4d3ef07`) — narrow the arm:** `queryPanePid` (`src/backend/ssh/tmux-helper.ts`) split its single catch into a two-case contract — rethrow on `execCommand` SSH failure so callers can classify, return null only on unparseable/empty pane_pid output. `discoverClaudeSession` (`src/backend/claude-session/session-file-discovery.ts`) wraps `queryPanePid` in try/catch and returns `{status:"inactive", reason:"exec_error"}` on throw (matching the three existing SSH-throw catches at the walk/PID-file/JSONL-test sites). `claude-session-server.ts` repoll callback hoists `isExecErrorTick` flag: `exec_error` inactive branch is now silent-tick (no `transitionToHolding`), and the `holdingTicks++` block is guarded on `!isExecErrorTick` so SSH failures don't burn the 5-min holding budget. Two tests added to `session-file-discovery.test.ts` (Cases 13+14: queryPanePid rethrow classified as exec_error; unparseable-pid null still classified as no_tmux_session). **Fix B (commit `90e1d85`) — self-clear on same-file recovery:** new `SessionHoldingClearedEvent` variant `{type:"session_holding_cleared"}` added to `ClaudeSessionServerEvent` union in `src/ui/api/claude-session-api.ts`. New backend helper `transitionFromHoldingToActiveSameFile()` (modeled on `transitionToHolding` with matching idempotency guard) flips `changeoverState="active"`, resets `holdingTicks=0`, emits the new WS frame, logs `claude_session_holding_cleared`. Call site: repoll active-branch's same-file path — `if (changeoverState === "holding" && result.sessionFile === currentSessionFile)` — invokes the new helper. Real SIGTERM-fallback recycles unaffected (they take the different-file branch first). Frontend: new `case "session_holding_cleared"` in `PrettyView.tsx`'s onmessage switch calls `setIsHolding(false) + setHoldingTimeoutError(false)` ONLY — deliberately does NOT wipe messages / contextPct / harnessTasks / backgroundedAgents / plan_pending / asideText (the point is the session did NOT change; message stream survives verbatim). Distinct from case `session_changed` which is heavy-reset. Executor auto-fix: rather than mock full WS server + SSH pair (7+ dep mocks to reach `connectToPane`), exported a module-scope `__applyRepollResultForTests` pure test seam mirroring the existing `__handleIdentityCountBountiesForTests` pattern — takes state + helpers as injectable parameters. New file `src/backend/claude-session/claude-session-server.repoll.test.ts` (9 tests): 5 branch cases from spec (a-e) plus 4 sub-cases. 3 new tests in `PrettyView.test.tsx` (F1: overlay unmounts on session_holding_cleared; F2: messages preserved; F3: contextPct preserved via aria-valuenow — auto-fix from initial textContent assertion because contextPct renders through aria-valuenow, not visible text). Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 (zero errors — critical because tsc --noEmit alone doesn't catch backend TS errors per patch #154 rule); `npm run build` EXIT 0 (4.39s); full `npm test` = 76 files / 861 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed. Log grep-gate for `FAIL|failed|✗` returned 0 real failures (verified per the just-promoted L508 learned preference from #211 arc — don't trust summary lines). Note: prior 5 unrelated failures in ComposeBox.voice.test.tsx (from patch #209's jsdom .catch bug) already resolved by patch #211's `9213a5b` (this branch's tip). Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `4d3ef07` (Fix A) + `90e1d85` (Fix B). NO push, NO docker build, NO deploy — stopped at commit boundary per fleet rule (Ashley 2026-07-27). Last activity (prior): 2026-07-30 — Completed quick task 260730-ptd: wired 4 Google Material Design audio-feedback sounds (start/stop/cancel/error) into the `useVoiceRecording` hook at `src/ui/features/pretty-view/useVoiceRecording.ts`. Task 1 (`be3c573`, assets): copied 4 unmodified Material Design MP3s (state-change_confirm-down → start.mp3; state-change_confirm-up → stop.mp3; navigation-cancel → cancel.mp3; alert_error-02 → error.mp3) into new `src/ui/assets/sounds/mic/` directory + wrote `CREDITS.md` with CC-BY-SA 4.0 attribution + source URL + license URL + verbatim filename mapping. Task 2 (`5c59743`, wire-in + tests): 4 Vite `?url` imports at hook top; 4 `useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null)` slots with inline lazy-init guards (hook mount = only construction site, survives re-renders); `playSound(audio)` helper centralizes `currentTime = 0` reset + `.play().catch(() => {})` silent-failure — audio failure is UX polish and MUST NOT disrupt recording. Placements: start.mp3 in the `start()` `.then()` callback AFTER `recorder.start()` + `setState("recording")` (success path only — permission-denied never plays, per plan spec); stop.mp3 as the very FIRST statement in `endAppend()` + `endSend()` after their state guards (BEFORE any `await`, so user hears feedback before STT network latency); cancel.mp3 as first statement in `cancel()` after guard (BEFORE `stopRecording()` teardown); error.mp3 in all 3 failure branches of `transcribeBlob()` (fetch throw, !res.ok, invalid JSON) + the empty-transcript guard in endAppend/endSend. Test suite `useVoiceRecording.test.ts` grew 8→14 tests: Audio constructor mocked as function-declaration (NOT `vi.fn().mockImplementation(arrow)` — vitest requires real constructor shape for `new` calls); each new test uses `invocationCallOrder` to prove ordering (start.mp3 AFTER recorder.start(), stop.mp3 BEFORE fetch, cancel.mp3 BEFORE recorder.stop()); regression tests cover getUserMedia rejection (start.mp3 NOT played), HTTP 500 + network error (error.mp3 plays), and rejected-play() promise (silently swallowed, state still transitions). No iOS silent-switch code — HTML5 `<audio>` on iOS Safari uses the ambient audio session which honors the silent switch by default (zero code needed). No mute toggle, no transcript-ready chime, no UI-visible attribution surface — all deferred per plan scope. ### Roadmap Evolution

- 2026-08-13: Phase 35 added — pretty-view-owns-compose-send-migrate-off-terminal-ws-borrow. Root cause (diagnosed 2026-08-13 live from `console-forward.log` at 03:19:33.265 fail → 03:19:47.285 success on retry): pretty-view compose-send routes through the TERMINAL's SSH WebSocket (`Terminal.tsx:3261-3299 onSend` reads `webSocketRef.current`), NOT PrettyView's own JSONL-watching WS. Terminal SSH WS silently dies from network-middleware timeouts (Caddy idle-timeout / TCP keepalive gap / mobile-tower NAT rebind); client-side `webSocketRef.current` still points at the dead socket; `if (!ws || ws.readyState !== 1) return false` trips instantly → `submit-failed err="not-connected"`. Ashley 2026-08-13 corrected the initial voice-specific frame: *"this isn't specific to voice or microphone in any way. Like, that's not even what I tried to send at the time that the issue popped up."* Design LOCKED with Ashley 2026-08-13: unify by giving pretty-view its own compose-send path instead of borrowing terminal's. Pretty-view WS (`PrettyView.tsx:639 wsRef` → `claude-session-server.ts:1382 wss on 30011`) already has aggressive lifecycle (iOS-PWA visibilitychange reopen at `:1493`, auto-reconnect per patch #148, pane-hide close+reopen) and already writes into the pane in production via `raw_keystrokes` (backend at `:4015-4053` uses `tmux send-keys -l`) — so this is EXTENDING an existing channel, not inventing a new one. Ashley 2026-08-13 verbatim on the framing: *"can't the PrettyView WebSocket just interact the same way as the Terminal WebSocket did for the Compose send?"* — yes, exactly. Backend approach: add `type:"input"` + `type:"interrupt"` handlers on claude-session WS mirroring `terminal.ts:499`'s split-send semantics, executed via the pretty-view WS's existing sshConn as `tmux send-keys -l -t <session> <body>` → 250ms wait (matches terminal.ts:842's validated timing — patch #111 raised the original 50ms after real-world submit failures) → `tmux send-keys -t <session> Enter` (two calls preserve the split-send timing patch #44 established because Ink races body-and-\r otherwise). Trust boundary mirrors raw_keystrokes: connection-scoped `currentTmuxSession`, ignore any client-supplied hostId/tmuxSession. Frontend cutover: FOUR call sites migrate atomically from `webSocketRef.current` → PrettyView's `wsRef.current` — `onSend` (Terminal.tsx:3261), `onInterrupt` (Terminal.tsx:3300), `onInjectedTurnReady` (Terminal.tsx:3208), and MessageQueueDrawer's `onSend` at Terminal.tsx:3331 (do NOT skip MessageQueueDrawer — leaving it on webSocketRef moves the bug rather than fixes it). MessageQueueDrawer stays mounted where it is; only its wsRef source flips (planner picks between drilling wsRef down as a prop vs. passing a callback that closes over it). Ashley 2026-08-13 verbatim on empirical verification: *"I don't even think we need to verify anything empirically … as long as we're using the same method as we used on the old WebSocket in this sense, then that's good enough for me."* → verification collapses to `npx vitest run` green + `npm run build:backend` green; no separate byte-stream comparison plan. Two plans: (1) additive backend `input` + `interrupt` handlers on claude-session WS (shippable independently — no cutover yet); (2) atomic frontend cutover of all four call sites. Rebase risk LOW — additive backend handler + frontend swap on fork-local files (Terminal.tsx, PrettyView.tsx, MessageQueueDrawer wiring, claude-session-server.ts); no upstream Skynet surfaces touched. SHIPS FIRST — Phase 34 (voice-slash-server-side-skill-catalog) + quick 260813-0qx (deactivate-reactivate-during-reset-latches-inactive) are held pending this ship per Ashley 2026-08-13: *"why don't you just save shipping the slash command stuff for after we do this new stuff next session."* Bounty tracker: `~/.claude/roles/box-maintainer/bounties/terminal-ws-silent-death-on-session-return/`.
- 2026-08-13: Phase 34 added — voice-slash-server-side-skill-catalog. Retire the client-side double-word intent-transform (`composeIntentTransform.ts` + `INTENT_REGISTRY = { bounty }` at `src/ui/features/pretty-view/composeIntentTransform.ts:61`) in favor of a server-side STT-endpoint-scoped transform that lets Ashley say "slash <skill-name> <args>" (voice-first path only) to invoke ANY skill on the target box's `~/.claude/skills/` with no maintained registry. Ashley 2026-08-13 (verbatim, on server-side location): *"the best time to be doing it would be when we do the STT, because that's the only time this pattern is used ... whether I'm intending to hit the send button where the voice gets transcribed and sent right away, or I'm hitting the append button so that it ends up in the compose box, i feel like the place that this change should happen is like coming back from the stt transcription server."* Ashley 2026-08-13 (verbatim, on TTL vs no-cache): *"I don't send messages more than once every 60 seconds usually anyways, so [TTL] would basically do nothing anyways ... if it's not a big deal, then we probably don't even need any TTL."* Ashley 2026-08-13 (verbatim, on timeout): *"instead of a 500 millisecond hard timeout, I think we could increase the timeout to something way overboard, and as long as that call is only happening when the message begins with the word slash, then I feel like that's a good tradeoff ... I only invoke skills that way, you know, maybe a handful of times out of every dozens of messages."* Design LOCKED: wake-word gate (`^\s*slash[\s.,;:!?\-]+`) runs unconditionally on every STT return; on HIT, server SSH-fetches skill catalog (`ls ~/.claude/skills/*/`) with generous 10s timeout + fail-open, greedy longest-prefix matcher against words after "slash" with kebab-case normalization ("gsd quick" → `gsd-quick`), returns transformed transcript (`/skill-name <rest>`) or unchanged on no-match; on MISS, zero server-side work. Transform applies to BOTH `endSend` (:465) and `endAppend` (:412) paths in `useVoiceRecording.ts`. Client passes `{hostId, tmuxSession}` alongside audio blob to STT endpoint. Typed messages unaffected (voice-only path — user types `/foo` directly for typed slash-commands, already works). User-wide skills only (`~/.claude/skills/*`); project-scoped `.claude/skills/` deferred to v2. NO TTL cache at v1 (wake-word gate + ~1-msg-per-minute cadence + SSH cost dominated by STT means add-latency is negligible; per-(hostId, tmuxSession) TTL cache is a small follow-up if latency becomes visible). Retire in same phase: `composeIntentTransform.ts` + `INTENT_REGISTRY` + doubled-word regex + associated tests. Bounty tracker: create at plan time.
- 2026-08-12: Phase 32 added — Redesign pretty-view auto-scroll (three-case sticky-bottom hook: session-first-load / follow-on-new-when-at-bottom / user-send-forces-bottom). Rip-and-replace the disabled `use-auto-scroll.ts` (225 lines, TEMP-disabled 2026-08-10 by quick 260810-299) with a fresh ~80-line hook. Design settled with Ashley 2026-08-12 (verbatim: "keep it simple, the old auto-scroll got complicated because I asked for a bunch of different cases; if you have to rip out the old stuff and start fresh that's fine — done right is priority"). Shape: one `stickyRef` + one `programmaticRef` + one exported `scrollToBottomAndFollow` used by pill AND all send paths (Enter/click/queued-fire/voice/aside-resume). ResizeObserver on OUTER scroll container closes H1 accessory-mount blind spot (WipBubble/PlanPendingBubble/AsideBubble live as in-flow siblings post-Phase-27 Step-B). Single scroll listener replaces old wheel+keydown+touchmove trifecta — `programmaticRef` distinguishes our writes from user. Full detail in bounty `pv-auto-scroll-redesign` timeline entry 2026-08-12T18:05:00Z.
- 2026-08-11: Phase 31 added — Whole-app structured-logging backfill. Two symptoms hit Ashley on cellular today that surfaced BOTH as new failure modes AND as under-instrumented: (a) `Waiting for connection logs...` — new-session-open hangs indefinitely after 2-3 opens within one PWA lifecycle, force-quit+reopen fixes; Ashley verbatim: "I've seen that connection log hundreds of times overusing the app and it's never failed to load in the first place like that"; (b) `Connection rejected by server` full-screen fatal-looking overlay on tabs left ~3 min idle then reconnected, auth actually succeeded and something's flipping wasConnectedRef back to false before a wasClean close hits the guard; (c) speak button on message bubbles produces no audio — press button, pause button appears (frontend thinks playback started), silent. Console-forward log 08:03-08:07 UTC on 2026-08-11 shows 116 WS error events in a 5-min window (iPhone Safari 26.6) — WS establishes then closes ~3-10s later in perpetual cycles, `Reconnecting... (1/8)` counter resetting each cycle, but the log lines are USELESS: `[WebSocket] Error: {"isTrusted":true}` is JSON.stringify on a DOM Event (strips everything), Abnormal-closure log carries no code/reason/wasClean/hostId/sessionId, no logs at the pause-gate boundary, no logs at wasConnectedRef transitions. Direction — this phase is FOUNDATIONAL for diagnosing the two symptom bounties (`ws-pause-gate-stuck-connect-cycling`, `speak-button-broken-on-cellular`) which are DEFERRED behind it. Do a pass across the whole app instrumenting interaction/lifecycle/effect boundaries: WS lifecycle (open/close/error with `event.code`/`reason`/`wasClean` explicitly extracted, hostId, sessionId, wasConnectedRef transitions), pause-gate boundary (patches #367-#369), all 4 reopen paths (setup-effect deps / onclose retry scheduler / visibilitychange handler / direct connectToHost callers) with which-path-fired attribution, session-open flow, TTS/speak pipeline (fetch, blob decode, `audio.play()` promise resolve/reject with error, media events canplay/playing/error/stalled/suspend/ended), voice-recording lifecycle. Rule for future patches: proactively instrument at decision points and edge transitions with enough context to be actionable in isolation. Ashley greenlit the "logging is cheap and we already batch-post" philosophy 2026-08-11; banked as § Standing directives in `~/.claude/roles/box-maintainer/box-maintainer.md`. Bounty trackers: `ws-pause-gate-stuck-connect-cycling` and `speak-button-broken-on-cellular` (both in_progress, both parked pending Phase 31 data).
- 2026-08-10: Phase 30 added — pane-state-backend-authoritative-no-client-inference. Phase 29 consolidated ~5 racing client-side overlay states into one machine with a truth table — real architectural improvement. But the machine's INPUTS are still mostly client-observed (isVisible-edge triggers, PWA-foreground handler forcing resolving, backendFirstFrame written by ~10 different frame types including the D-11 "any live message = active" swap, my patch #381 client-hint hack). Ashley 2026-08-10 verbatim after tina's #381 shipped and produced overlay-flash: *"Whether or not the session is recycling should be based on '/id reset' being found in the session file. That was supposed to be the whole point of the phase that redid the logic that decides which overlays show and stuff. Everything is supposed to be based on real things, not client state."* Direction: reduce state-machine inputs to exactly TWO — (1) `pane_state` frame (authoritative phase verdict emitted by backend from real observations: session-file parser sees `/id reset` → holding, /exit scan + PID death → holding fallback, new session file inode → active, agent-supervisor dormancy → dormant, backend classification → inactive) emitted on WS attach + every state change; (2) `ws_transport_state` (unavoidably client-observed since only the browser knows its own socket state) — gates "can we trust the last pane_state" vs "show reconnecting." Deletes from frontend: three entry-trigger effects in usePaneResolvingMachine, rearmSnapshotRef machinery, D-11 message→active swap, all ~10 captureFirstFrame call sites including patch #381 (which becomes redundant), backendFirstFrame concept entirely, local isHolding/dormant/waking/holdingTimeoutError state slots. Adds to backend: new pane_state emitter, session-file-parser /id reset detection (suppress user-turn AND emit holding). Tests: parser test for /id reset skip+emit, pane_state emission tests, PrettyView state-machine simplified. Phase-29 test suite (usePaneResolvingMachine entry-trigger tests, resolve-phase truth table) rewrites or deletes as trigger machinery goes away. Bounty tracker: `~/.claude/roles/box-maintainer/bounties/reset-button-holding-overlay-regression-phase-29/` (broadening scope to this full-fix — patch #381 is the interim hack this phase deletes).
- 2026-08-10: Phase 29 added — Unified session-entry state machine — single 'resolving' spinner fronts every overlay until deterministic verdict. Ashley 2026-08-10 (verbatim): entering a session from the conversation list produces multiple racing overlays (screen flickers black with "Connecting…", "Connection lost" box covers half-screen, "Waking up" shows even though session was awake) — "no unified piece of logic governing … what state it's actually in so i know what to display and instead there's just a patchwork of bullshit fighting for what should be displayed." Also verbatim on determinism: "I really don't think we want a deadline here … if we're doing this right, then we should be able to go look at all the factors that matter and decide what to display … it's not like anything in here is stochastic … there's no reason for this not to be deterministic." Direction (tiffany diagnosis Ashley bought): unified pane-entry state machine with explicit `resolving` phase — on any transition into a pane (activeSet mount, tab-select, PWA foreground), enter `resolving`; single spinner is the ONLY thing shown; each substate input reports its deterministic verdict (WS open/error, backend first frame — active/inactive/session_holding/dormant); when ALL inputs have reported, transition to exactly one of {active, dormant, holding, error, inactive} and reveal corresponding UI. NO deadline/timeout — inputs have deterministic resolve signals; if something is slow the spinner stays up honestly. Testable end-to-end: for any (inputs) → exactly one displayed state. **Phase-number note:** originally added as Phase 28 in an earlier session, but Tina in parallel claimed Phase 28 for PrettyView virtualization correctness cluster and shipped it as patch #374; mine renumbered to 29 on 2026-08-10 rebase. Root cause is gsd-sdk `phase.add` with no cross-tree lock; Tina filing a bounty on that. Bounty tracker: `~/.claude/roles/box-maintainer/bounties/unified-session-entry-state-machine/`.

Scope: src/ui/features/pretty-view/PrettyView.tsx message list only (WipBubble/PlanPendingBubble/AsideBubble in slot below stay unvirtualized). Preserve existing scroll anchor (auto-scroll-to-bottom-when-pinned vs. don't-yank-when-scrolled-up) and initial-slice-from-bottom hydration. Image bubble grow handled via ResizeObserver + re-measure. ⌘F/find-in-page regression on long conversations ACCEPTED (Ashley 2026-08-09 verbatim: "I don't really care about losing that functionality"). New dep: TanStack Virtual. Rebase risk LOW — additive integration on fork-local PrettyView; no upstream Skynet surfaces touched. Bounty tracker: `~/.claude/roles/box-maintainer/bounties/pretty-view-message-list-virtualization/`.

- 2026-07-28: Phase 17 added — Pretty-view relay bubbles, Skynet integration. Lifts the validated detectors from bounty `pretty-view-relay-bubble-prototype` (prototype.html at 8899, 6/6 acceptance battery passed 2026-07-28) into `src/ui/features/pretty-view/` as a message-turn extension. Renders outbound fleet Matrix relay PUTs as right-aligned blue bubbles + inbound task-notification receives as left-aligned orange bubbles, so fleet coordination shows up in-flow instead of hiding inside opaque tool-use blobs. OUTBOUND detector: `curl` + `-X PUT` + URL shape `rooms/X/send/m.room.message/Y` (three-way conjunction, kills false positives from prose/comments). INBOUND detector: `type=user` + `origin.kind=task-notification` + recv.sh line regex — non-relay wakes correctly render as neutral. Bubble sender mxid resolved to local identity where possible so hue chain matches. Long-inbound file-pointer support fetches recv.sh's pointer file and renders full body inline. Extraction best-effort with ⚠ fallback; detection bulletproof. Depends on Phase 2. Other harness-synthesized `"type":"user"` lines passed the predicate — (a) skill-body injections `{content:[{type:"text",text:"Base directory for this skill: ..."}]}` the harness emits right after a slash-command turn to inject the SKILL.md into model context, (b) `<local-command-caveat>` and `<local-command-stdout>` string-content wrappers Claude Code synthesizes for `!bash` command output. When any of these lands while `changeoverState === "holding"` (right after `/id reset`), the reducer at `layer1-detect.ts:164-185` flips `mostRecentUserTurnIsIdReset=false` and returns `clear_holding` → backend calls `transitionFromHoldingToActiveSameFile()` → emits `pane_state:active reason:same_file_recovery` → overlay unmounts before the actual recycle completes. Like, it really doesn't need to be more complicated than that."* Weighed against tighten-isUserTurn: any-`/id reset`-seen model reintroduces reconnect-flash class (tail `-n +1` replay on every WS reconnect would arm on historical resets); "most recent user turn" invariant is what quietens reconnect. | 260810-ia4 | Reduce PrettyView virtualizer jitter with three coordinated cheap fixes (partial down-payment on bounty `pv-auto-scroll-redesign` — jitter class only, no library change). Ashley 2026-08-10 symptoms: "jumpy sometimes" + "sometimes even snapping back to bottom as I try to scroll up." Pre-work grep confirmed no rogue caller of `scrollTop=scrollHeight` on message arrival — `useAutoScroll` is genuinely disabled per patch #373, `forceStickAndJumpRef` current is a no-op, `scrollToBottomAndFollow` fires only from two user-clicked buttons (jump-to-bottom pill + "let's go" quick-send). Root cause is variable-height re-measure churn: rows enter viewport at the constant estimate 80, real `measureElement` corrects upward (real bubbles land at 200-600px for code blocks/images), `getTotalSize()` grows, `virtualRow.start` for items below the correction increases, and same DOM node visibly slides down out of viewport while her scrollTop stays put — reads visually as "the layout snapped me down." Three fixes: (1) **Type-aware `estimatePvBubbleSize(m: StreamEvent): number`** exported at module scope in `PrettyView.tsx` (line 209) and wired into `useVirtualizer` at line 756 via `estimateSize: (i) => estimatePvBubbleSize(messages[i])`. Rules: `m.type === "image"` → 400; text with fenced code block → `Math.max(120, lineCount * 22 + 40)` (line-height 22 + 40px padding); plain text → `Math.max(80, Math.min(400, textLength * 0.4))`; fallback (MalformedLineEvent, no-text paths) → 80. Estimates go from ~30% correct (constant 80) to ~90% correct → correction deltas shrink → visible content displacement shrinks. Phase 27/28 invariants (`overscan: 5`, `scrollMargin: 12`, `getItemKey`, `initialRect: {width:1024, height:600}`, `observeElementRect` fallback 600) all untouched. Nine-assertion unit test file `src/ui/features/pretty-view/PrettyView.estimateSize.test.tsx` covers image=400, short-text floor, long-text scaling, code-block formula, very-long-text cap, distinct-values sanity, RelayOutbound, RelayInbound, MalformedLine. (2) **Aspect-ratio 4/3 reservation on ImageBubble** (`ImageBubble.tsx`) — pre-flight check on `ImageBlock` shape at `src/ui/api/claude-session-api.ts:44` confirmed only `{ data, mediaType, toolUseId? }` — no natural width/height metadata, so 4/3 fallback is the only path. Wrapped each `<img>` in `<div style={{ aspectRatio: "4 / 3" }} className="max-w-full max-h-[480px]">` with the img becoming `w-full h-full object-contain rounded` inside (line 90). Reserves the final layout box BEFORE decode so `measureElement` never observes a 0→N grow after mount — this was the biggest post-mount re-measure delta per Phase 27 review H2, and now it's structurally gone. Idempotent for non-image bubbles (not touched). (3) **`[overflow-anchor:none]` on outer scroll container** — Tailwind arbitrary variant added to the `composeScrollRefs` div at `PrettyView.tsx` ~L2006. Browser's native scroll-anchoring was competing with the virtualizer's re-measure adjustments for scroll-position authority; removing the browser feature makes the virtualizer sole authority through measurement changes (this is the primitive react-virtuoso does automatically). Bounded-DOM Test 1 upper bound (`<= 30`) NOT retuned — type-aware estimates with the 120-msg `type: "message"` fixture didn't shift the JSDOM visible-slice size past the cap. Verification: `npm run build:backend` exit 0 + `npm run build` exit 0 (built in 6.38s) + `npx vitest run` = **143 test files pass, 1822 tests pass / 7 skip / 1 todo / 0 fail** (the two `EnvironmentTeardownError` entries in vitest output are a pre-existing timing artifact in `IdentityModal.test.tsx`; vitest exit 0). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. NO worktrees (project config `workflow.use_worktrees=false` honored, matches Ashley fleet rule). Three atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`, post-rebase over Tanya's #384 (`fc0c072`, phase-30-restore-resolving-overlay-paint-delay, fast-path ship — no conflicts): `90b3dea` (Task 1: helper + wire + 9 unit tests) + `9eec4ba` (Task 2: ImageBubble aspect-ratio) + `fcf6485` (Task 3: overflow-anchor className). **NOT pushed / NOT image-built / NOT deployed** per code-work-doesn't-authorize-ship rule (Ashley 2026-07-27) — orchestrator (tina) handles deploy motion (coord-room BEFORE announce + rebase check + docker build + verify + push + `skynet-patches.md` #385 entry + coord-room AFTER) after Ashley greenlight. Explicitly parked (not in this quick task, wait for full `pv-auto-scroll-redesign` phase): H1 accessory-mount pin blind spot, H2 image-decode racy re-pin residuals not covered by aspect-ratio, M5 `scrollToIndex` audit, M7 paneKey-change scroll-to-bottom race, full auto-scroll redesign, library swap consideration (react-virtuoso / virta). | 260809-cnx | DormancyOverlay flow — two paired fixes. **Fix A (CNX-A):** ComposeBox never mounted on dormant panes because the gate at `PrettyView.tsx:~1834` was `status === "streaming" || status === "error"` and dormant panes have `status="connecting"` (no session frame ever fires). DormancyOverlay was geometrically confined to the chat-region wrapper already (line 1615-1829), and ComposeBox already had `dormantActive={dormant || waking}` prop wired (line 1871, from patch #345) — the reduced-state wiring was in place, ComposeBox just never mounted to consume it. Fix: extended the mount gate to `status === "streaming" || status === "error" || dormant`. Existing prop wiring handles the reduced state — Send/reset/queue/thumbsUp/paperclip disabled via `dormantActive={true}`; textarea + mic typeable so Ashley can pre-draft during the wake window. `canSend = status === "streaming"` naturally false on dormant panes, providing belt-and-suspenders with dormantActive. **Fix B (CNX-B):** Ashley's scenario: click Wake → navigate away (patch #344 hidden-pane WS pause closes WS on visibility flip) → phone sleeps → session re-dormants (supervisor's IDLE_THRESHOLD hit again) → come back → WS reopens → backend re-emits `{type:"dormant", dormant:true}` — but frontend local `waking=true` state is stale. `case "dormant"` handler at line ~826 sets `dormant=true` when backend says so but never resets waking (it only clears waking on the `dormant:false` path — natural resume). Result: overlay stuck on "Waking up…" with no working Wake button until page reload. Fix: added `prevIsVisibleRef = useRef<boolean>(isVisible)` (initialized to current value so initial mount is a no-op — critical so we don't false-fire mid-wake if Ashley stays on the pane) + a `useEffect([isVisible])` that fires on the false→true visibility edge to reset `waking + wakingStartTs + elapsedSeconds + wakeError`. WS is closed while pane hidden per patch #344, so any pre-hidden waking state is unreliable on re-visibility — resetting is safe; backend's fresh dormant frame within one 3s poll cycle paints the truth ("Session is asleep" + working Wake button). No changes to `case "dormant"` handler, `case "wake_result"` handler, live-frame auto-dismiss, or DormancyOverlay component itself. Backend byte-untouched. Files: `src/ui/features/pretty-view/PrettyView.tsx` (Fix A: 1-word gate extension; Fix B: new ref + useEffect ~10 lines), `src/ui/features/pretty-view/PrettyView.test.tsx` (new `describe("quick 260809-cnx dormant flow refinements")` block with 2 tests: CNX-A asserts ComposeBox mounts with typeable textarea + disabled Send on dormant frame; CNX-B asserts visibility cycle clears "Waking up…").
