| 260808-ohn | SessionHoldingOverlay flashes on session re-open — Layer 1 fast-path recycle detector refactor: swap `/exit` edge-triggered detection for `/id reset` tail-state-derived detection. Root cause: current Layer 1 (`claude-session-server.ts:1232-1255`) scanned each raw JSONL line for `line.includes('"content":"<command-name>/exit</command-name>')` gated by a per-connection `hasSeenExit` flag. Every WS reconnect (patch #344 hidden-pane WS pause reopens on visibility=true) restarts the tail with `-n +1` which replays every JSONL line from line 1 — on replay `hasSeenExit` resets to false, so any historical line containing the `/exit` marker bytes (Ashley's actual `/exit` user turns saved by the harness, or messages quoting the pattern) fires Layer 1 spuriously. Fix B (2026-07-30) then self-clears via same-file-active on the next 3s discovery-repoll tick — producing observed "instant appear + few-second flash + auto-disappear" symptom. Diagnosis proof: container log showed 14 arm+clear pairs on session `owGv_6oxMc7Sd5o8kzt3O` in ~1h; that session's JSONL grep-verified to contain 2 literal `/exit` marker byte matches (both historical user `/exit` turns from Ashley in this tanya conversation). Ashley's design (better than tanya's initial 3 fix shapes): (a) change detection target from `/exit` to `/id reset` — matches her actual recycle flow (`/exit` is unused; `/id reset` routes via supervisor-sentinel signal-kill path, never puts `/exit` in the JSONL); (b) reframe as tail-state-derived — arm iff file's MOST RECENT user turn is `/id reset`. Historical `/id reset` lines superseded by a later user turn can never fire — kills the entire replay false-fire class by design. Handles her sometimes-freeform-explanation-after-reset case via prefix match on args (`<command-args>reset...`). Byte pattern: BOTH `<command-name>/id</command-name>` AND `<command-args>reset` on same raw JSONL line (`line.includes` — no JSON.parse). Extracted pure helpers to new sibling `layer1-detect.ts` (`isUserTurn`, `isIdResetUserTurn`, `applyLineToLayer1State` reducer + 28 unit tests). Rewired `onLine` at `claude-session-server.ts` to dispatch reducer actions to `transitionToHolding('id_reset')` / `transitionFromHoldingToActiveSameFile()`. Deleted `hasSeenExit` flag, the `/exit` include-check block, and the `"exit_marker"` branch of `transitionToHolding`'s reason union (now `"id_reset" | "discovery_diff"`). Layer 2 (discovery-repoll) byte-untouched — still the definitive recycle detector via new-UUID sessionFile appearance; `session_holding_cleared` + `session_changed` WS frames unchanged. Two atomic commits: `c9a485f` (Task 1, post-rebase from `3b723b3`) + `8a40b5a` (Task 2, post-rebase from `3ece6b3`). Byte gates verified: `hasSeenExit`/`"exit_marker"` code refs → 0 (only comments remain), `<command-name>/exit` → 0, `"id_reset"` → 5. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0, `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0, full `npx vitest run` = **1594 pass / 6 skip / 0 fail across 129 files** (+38 from this refactor; zero regressions). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. WipBubble owns motion for TASK work; this overlay owns motion for SURFACE work; the two never co-render (loading overlay is only up before any bubbles render). Deviation documented explicitly in the file header of `PrettyViewLoadingOverlay.tsx` and locked in place by regression test 5 in `PrettyViewLoadingOverlay.test.tsx` (asserts `.animate-spin` IS present — inverse of the sibling static-glyph regression tests) so a future refactor cannot silently "fix" it back to static without first re-reading the header rationale. iOS Safari backdrop-filter hardening: scrim carries `isolate [transform:translateZ(0)]` verbatim from sibling overlays per patch #333 lesson (non-negotiable for any new backdrop-filter surface in this fork). | 260808-dmz | Dormancy overlay never showed for dormant panes — fix backend inactive-branch short-circuit (hotfix to patch #345 shipped same session). Ashley tested with Tiffany (`.dormant` sentinel present, tmux alive) and saw "no active Claude session" fallback instead of the DormancyOverlay. Root cause: patch #345 piggybacked the dormancy stat on the ACTIVE poll cycle (~claude-session-server.ts:3445), but `discoverClaudeSession` returns `{status:"inactive", reason:"not_claude"}` for dormant panes (no claude process in pane_pid descendant walk), which triggers the short-circuit at lines 3484-3500 — emit one `{type:"inactive"}` frame, tear down SSH, never start poll cycle → dormancy check never runs. Design mistake in #345: assumed dormancy layers on top of a live poll, but dormancy IS the state of a not-live session. Fix (`src/backend/claude-session/claude-session-server.ts`, atomic commit `acfdf55` + frontend `b347bc1`): (1) Extracted the "start active session tail + all setup" block after the inactive check into a `startActiveSessionFlow(conn, discovery)` helper — byte-identical behavior but callable from the dormant-poll transition (bounty explicitly required extract, not copy-paste). (2) In the inactive branch, BEFORE teardown: identity-shape probe (cached per connection); if identity-shaped + `.dormant` present → emit `{type:"dormant", dormant:true}` instead of `{type:"inactive"}`, keep SSH alive (skip the `conn.end(); sshConn=null` block), enter dormant-poll loop. (3) Dormant-poll loop: 3s cadence, new `dormantPollInFlight` guard mirroring `contextPctInFlight`, reuses kept-alive SSH conn (no new opens per tick); polls `.dormant` via single `stat` exec; on sentinel-disappearance emits `{type:"dormant", dormant:false}` + retries `discoverClaudeSession`; if retry returns active → calls `startActiveSessionFlow(conn, activeResult)` which emits `{type:"session"}` + starts tail (the `session` frame triggers PrettyView's onmessage-top auto-dismiss from patch #345, closing the overlay for free); if still inactive → keeps polling (supervisor's next CHECK_INTERVAL tick). (4) Wake handler already in `ws.onmessage` and doesn't depend on poll — verified reachable in dormant-poll state via new tests. (5) Kept patch #345's active-poll `dormantInFlight` piggyback as belt-and-suspenders per bounty constraint — defensive against a hypothetical supervisor race, cheap to keep. (6) Frontend belt-and-suspenders: `PrettyView.tsx:1575` inactive fallback now also gated on `!dormant` so it can't stomp the overlay if backend somehow emits both (structurally prevented by fix #2 but explicit gate documents intent). (7) Tag-along fix: diag registry pretty-view snapshot now reads `isVisible` instead of emitting `null` (patch #344 added the prop but diag-registry snapshot wasn't updated; DIAG-REPORT logs pre-fix showed `isVisible: null` for pretty-view panes). Test coverage: extended `__applyDormantPollTickForTests` / `__applyWakeMessageForTests` seams from patch #345 with 5 new tests G-K covering (G) `.dormant` + not_claude → dormant true + no teardown; (H) sentinel disappears + retry finds claude → dormant false + `startActiveSessionFlow` transition; (I) sentinel disappears + still not_claude → keeps polling; (J) wake reachable in dormant-poll state; (K) SSH stays alive throughout cycle. Full `npx vitest run` = **1585 pass / 6 skip / 0 fail** (baseline 1580+ from patch #345 maintained + 5 new). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. Ashley's iPhone 16 Pro Max PWA chugs with 4-5 active-set sessions carried since page load; baseline diag (patch #343) at 2026-08-08T06:07Z showed 3 hidden PrettyViews contributing 10+13+10 = 33 Claude WS frames/30s combined plus ongoing React re-render churn (every idle pane ticks 10-13 status polls per 30s even when nothing is happening). Fix (`src/ui/features/pretty-view/PrettyView.tsx` + `src/ui/features/terminal/Terminal.tsx`, single atomic commit `a55f14a`): (1) added `isVisible: boolean` prop to `PrettyViewProps`; (2) `Terminal.tsx:3044` wires `isVisible={isVisible}` at the mount site (one JSX line — `isVisible` was already threaded into Terminal at props line 84, destructured 114, refed at 321/580/649); (3) new `isVisibleRef` inside PrettyView (parallel to existing `statusRef`) mirrors the prop via useEffect so WS callbacks can read the current value without stale-closure hazards; (4) new WS-lifecycle useEffect keyed on `isVisible`: on false-flip while WS is open, calls `wsRef.current.close()` — existing `onclose` at line 878 sets `status="error"` which patch #339's render gates handle gracefully (scroll region stays mounted per the extended gate at ~1323 that includes `status==="error" && messages.length>0`; ComposeBox stays mounted per the `streaming || error` gate; `reconnectingActive={status === "error"}` prop disables Send/reset/ThumbsUp/Recap/queued-row-Send but keeps textarea + mic + attach live); on true-flip, resets reconnect budget + calls `setRetryKey(k=>k+1)` to trigger the existing WS-setup effect (no new connect path invented). (5) Two short guards prevent patch #148's auto-reconnect loop from fighting the pause: the `onclose` retry scheduler checks `isVisibleRef.current` before scheduling, and the iOS PWA `visibilitychange` handler treats offscreen-in-tab the same as backgrounded-tab (no wake-up reconnect when the pane itself is hidden). | 260808-fkg | Row-swipe combined pin+activate / unpin+deactivate on pretty-conversations (mobile PWA only, non-RDP). Ashley bounty: pinning + activating (and unpinning + deactivating) is a two-tap sequence in the context menu today — collapsing to a one-swipe motion per direction is the ask. Fix: added a swipe-to-ACT gesture layer to `PrettyConversationRow.tsx` alongside the existing long-press → context-menu layer. Both machines share the same onTouchStart/Move/End/Cancel handlers on the row body and coexist by cancelling each other on their own movement gates (long-press cancels on `hypot > 10`; swipe arms on `|dx| >= 8 && |dx| > |dy|`, which also clears the long-press timer). Swipe-RIGHT past threshold fires composite pin+activate (`onTogglePin` if `!pinned` + `onSelect` if `!inActiveSet`); swipe-LEFT past threshold fires composite unpin+deactivate (`onTogglePin` if `pinned` + `onDeactivate?()` if `inActiveSet`). Both directions guarded by `wouldChangeState` so already-both-set rows are silent no-ops (bounce would falsely imply action fired). Threshold: `Math.max(90, rowWidth * 0.35)` (35% for typical mobile column widths, floored at 90px for narrow rows). rowWidth measured once per gesture via `getBoundingClientRect().width`. Below-threshold release snaps back with 180ms cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1) transition applied inline only during the snap-back window (not during drag, which would fight the raw translate). Visual feedback: `box-shadow inset` glow INSIDE the row body (hue-tinted for right using `--pv-hue`, muted-cream for left using `hsla(35, 20%, 60%, 0.30)`) — NOT painted behind the row. This is the critical design constraint that makes this NOT a re-introduction of the retired quick-260802-pq2 `swipe-actions-visible-through-translucent-rows` bleed-through class of bug: no element painted behind the row, no persistent reveal strip, no PinAction/DeactivateAction imports re-added. tokens.ts stays header-only (single call site → no PC_SWIPE_* tokens per naming rule at lines 12-16). RDP rows exempt: every swipe touch handler early-returns on `isRdp === true`, mirroring the panel-level `rdpNoopTogglePin` exemption at `PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx:1050`. Desktop untouched via `!isMobile` gate. Tap-to-activate + long-press → context menu + desktop right-click context menu + context menu Pin/Unpin + Deactivate items all UNTOUCHED (split cases — pin-without-activate, activate-without-pin — still work through the menu at their existing one-tap cost). Panel-side (`PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx`) BYTES-IDENTICAL — row uses props panel already wires at lines 942/997/1051/1098/1157. Tests: 7 new TS1-TS7 tests in `PrettyConversationRow.test.tsx` (fake timers, `fireEvent.touchStart/touchMove/touchEnd` with `Touch` casts): TS1 ambient-unpinned → both composites fire, TS2 already-both-set → silent no-op, TS3 active-pinned + swipe-left → both composites fire, TS4 below-threshold → neither fires (snap-back only), TS5 vertical drag → never arms, TS6 small horizontal jitter during a tap → onSelect still fires via existing tap path (swipe never armed), TS7 RDP row → no action fires (isRdp early-return). Fixture note: rowWidth defaults to 0 in jsdom (no layout engine), so threshold `Math.max(90, rowWidth * 0.35)` collapses to 90 — dx values chosen with the 90 constant in mind (past = 100+, below = 40 or less). Plus 1 integration test TS-P1 in `PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx` renders full mobile-variant panel + dispatches swipe-right sequence on a fixture row + asserts on `pinConversationSpy` (called with canonical shadow-fleet id `fleet::1::swipetgt`) + `addToActiveSetSpy` (row.id) + `selectConversationSpy` (row.id) — proves the swipe composite flows through panel-level `handleTogglePin` + `handleRowSelect`, NOT through any store bypass on the row side. Files: `PrettyConversationRow.tsx` (+289 / -13, adds swipe machine + inline transform + class toggles + shared touch handlers), `pretty-conversations.css` (+29, adds swipe-past-threshold-right/left inset-glow rules in a "Swipe-to-act visual feedback (quick-260808-fkg)" block at the bottom), `PrettyConversationRow.test.tsx` (+267, TS1-TS7 describe block), `PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx` (+79, TS-P1 test + `act` import + `afterEach` import). Deviation from plan: TS-P1 uses host id `"1"` (not `"h1"`) because panel's `handleTogglePin` runs `parseInt(row.host.id, 10)` — `"h1"` resolves to NaN and produces `fleet::NaN::swipetgt`; plan already directed asserting against `fleet::1::swipetgt` so the fix was fixture-side only, not semantic. Verification: `npx vitest run` = **125 test files, 1550 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed** (+8 net-new tests vs baseline, zero regressions); `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. | 260808-1pa | Guard `stopRecording()` against non-recording state + 8s onstop watchdog (mic-dead-buttons). Ashley reproduced the "no matter what button I hit to end recording, they make noises but do nothing" bug in the live PWA at 2026-08-08T01:01:36Z. Durable console-forward log at `/opt/skynet/console-forward-logs/console-forward.log` (from patch #326, survives container recreate) showed exactly one failing sequence: first press fired `MediaRecorder.stop()` on a `recording` recorder but the browser's `onstop` never fired (iOS Safari MediaRecorder quirk — cause is browser-side, out of our control); every subsequent press then saw `recorder.state === "inactive"`, silently reassigned `recorder.onstop`, and called `.stop()` on the inactive recorder — a no-op that also doesn't fire onstop, so every `await stopRecording()` after the first hang hung too. React state stuck at `recording` forever until page reload. Fix (`src/ui/features/pretty-view/useVoiceRecording.ts` `stopRecording()`, function at lines 120-143): (1) added `recorder.state !== "recording"` guard at the top of the returned Promise — resolves null immediately without touching `onstop`, killing the reassign-onstop-on-inactive-recorder path; recovery to `state: "idle"` happens via the existing `if (!blob) { setState("idle"); return null; }` branches in `endAppend` (lines 294-298) / `endSend` (lines 335-338); (2) added 8000ms setTimeout watchdog racing with `onstop` — shared `resolved` flag + `clearTimeout(handle)` inside onstop ensures whichever fires first wins and the loser no-ops; watchdog path force-stops stream tracks, nulls `recorderRef.current` + `streamRef.current`, clears chunks, resolves null. Recovers even the FIRST hang (not just cases 2+). Tests: 2 new regression tests in `useVoiceRecording.test.ts` (Test G: guard-bails-without-touching-onstop; Test H: watchdog-cleanup-and-null-resolve-after-8s). Deviation from plan: adding `recorder.state` as a read target in production required aligning **4** `MockMediaRecorder` stubs (mine + 3 pre-existing in `ComposeBox.voice.test.tsx` / `ComposeBox.recycle-disable.test.tsx` / `ComposeBox.plan-pending-disable.test.tsx`) to expose a `state: "inactive" | "recording"` field flipped by mock `start()` / `stop()` — without alignment, 10+ pre-existing voice tests regressed because `undefined !== "recording"` short-circuited the happy path. Fix committed inline alongside the prod change (5-file commit instead of planned 2). Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; full `npx vitest run` = **1526 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 123 files** (+2 net-new tests vs baseline, zero regressions). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. NOT pushed / NOT built / NOT deployed per code-work-doesn't-authorize-ship rule (Ashley 2026-07-27) — deploy is a separate ask. Ashley greenlit the vehicle picks ("let's go") for #328's follow-up mic bug + a paired 1-line inline color-randomization fix on `NewSessionDialog.tsx` (blue-default → random hue on modal open, both mount + reset paths; landing as a separate atomic commit). Also durable proof that the console-forward log ships across container recreates: this bug wouldn't have been diagnosable without patch #326's `/opt/skynet/console-forward-logs/` durable path (tina's canonicalizing build had wiped `/tmp/console-forward.log` post-recycle before the durable-path fix landed). | 2026-08-08 | `a4a5962` | [260808-1pa-guard-stoprecording-against-non-recordin](./quick/260808-1pa-guard-stoprecording-against-non-recordin/) |
| 260808-11l | Context-% meter source-swap from tmux-pane scrape to JSONL session file. Ashley reported the mobile PWA occasionally collapses the pane to ~2 chars wide — the statusline's leading pct chars remain but the bar glyphs (`█`/`░`) `parseContextPct` AND-anchors against get truncated, freezing the pretty-view meter. Empirical validation on 8 live sessions (all Opus 4.7, 1M window): JSONL reconstruction from last `type:"assistant"` turn's `message.usage.{input_tokens + cache_creation_input_tokens + cache_read_input_tokens}` / 1_000_000, normalized by the same 16.5% autocompact buffer the statusline uses, matches CC's number within ~0.5% every time (displayed integer matches 5/8 exact + 3/8 off by ±1 — rounding-order artifact, sub-1% precision well within the 80% context-watch nudge threshold's practical window). Mapping mechanic already existed: `session-file-discovery.ts` reads `~/.claude/sessions/<claudePid>.json` (Claude Code v2.1.150+ writes this) → sessionId + cwd → JSONL path tracked in `currentSessionFile` closure-local (claude-session-server.ts:1097). PID-keyed → cwd-collision-safe. New helper `context-pct-from-jsonl.ts` (156 lines) exports `readContextPctFromJsonl(conn, sessionFile) → Promise<number \| null>`: single SSH exec `tail -c 10000`, reverse-scan for first valid `type:"assistant"` + `message.usage`, compute + normalize + round + clamp to [0,100]. Constants `AUTO_COMPACT_BUFFER_PCT = 16.5` + `MODEL_CONTEXT_WINDOW = 1_000_000` (Ashley: *"every model available in the harness is a million tokens window"*), 3000ms timeout (matches session-file-discovery). Poll callback in `claude-session-server.ts:~3435` wrapped as async IIFE: try JSONL FIRST when `currentSessionFile` non-null, fall back to legacy `tmux capture-pane` + `parseContextPct` when JSONL returns null (fresh session with no assistant turn yet, or read failure). All existing guards preserved (`stopped` / `ws.readyState` / `!sshConn` / `contextPctInFlight` + `.finally` clear). `context-pct-parser.ts` and its tests preserved byte-identical (125 lines) as the fallback path. Frontend untouched — same `{type:"context_pct", pct}` WS message at 3s cadence. Tests: new `context-pct-from-jsonl.test.ts` (224 lines, 8 cases): happy path with empirical fixture (session e222905c, sum=198542 → displayed=24), no assistant turn → null, missing usage → null, exec throws → null, buffer boundary (remaining=16.5 → displayed=100), over-full (>1M tokens → clamped to 100), multiple assistant turns picks last, malformed JSON line doesn't break reverse scan. | 260807-igo | Context-menu tap-feedback: flash on click. On mobile PWA, tapping an item in the conversation-row context menu (`PrettyConversationContextMenu.tsx`) had zero visual acknowledgment — the portal just vanished. Root cause: menu items used inline `onMouseEnter`/`onMouseLeave` handlers for hover (mouse-only, never fires on touch), and the click handler fired `item.onClick()` then `onClose()` synchronously, tearing down the portal before any `:active` state could flash. Fix (single atomic commit `6beecda` on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`): (1) added `.pv-context-menu-item` class in `pretty-conversations.css` with `:hover` (`rgba(255,240,215,0.08)`) + `:active` (`rgba(255,240,215,0.18)` — 2x louder for a distinct tap-down), 90ms bg transition, `-webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent` matching sibling components; (2) removed inline mouse-only handlers and applied the class alongside the existing mobile hit-target padding (`py-[8px] px-[12px] max-md:py-[18px] max-md:px-[14px]` — concatenated, not replaced); (3) added `FLASH_DISMISS_MS = 120` with `setTimeout` in the item-button onClick to delay `onClose()`; guarded with `mountedRef` (checked before the timer fires so unmounting the menu mid-flash does NOT call `onClose` on a torn-down parent) + `pendingTimeoutRef` cleanup on the mount/unmount effect. Hue-token border (`--pv-id-hue`) and `danger` variant color (`#ff9a8a`) untouched — inline styles remain authoritative. Tests: `PrettyConversationContextMenu.test.tsx` item-click describe wrapped in `vi.useFakeTimers()`, both existing item-click tests assert delayed `onClose` after `vi.advanceTimersByTime(120)`; new Test 11 proves unmount-during-flash-delay does NOT invoke `onClose` (14/14 pass). Verification: file-scoped 14/14 + full `npx vitest run` = **1520 pass / 6 skip / 0 fail across 123 files** (234s wall-clock, +1 test vs 1519 pre-quick baseline, zero regressions). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. Same fix improves desktop click-feedback for free — no mobile-only branching, no user-agent sniffing. NOT pushed / NOT image-built / NOT deployed per code-work-doesn't-authorize-ship rule (Ashley 2026-07-27) — awaiting Ashley's ship greenlight. NO worktrees (`workflow.use_worktrees=false` honored; executor ran directly on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`). | 260806-ixl | Unify "is this session working" logic between the sidebar conversation-list dot (`PrettyConversationsPanel`) and PrettyView's WipBubble by extending `session-working-store` into a single-source-of-truth composite. Motivation: sidebar dot rendered iff `inActiveSet && isWorking === false` where `isWorking = !isIdle` (Terminal.tsx publish from PTY idle patch #13), while WipBubble mounted iff `wipActive || backgroundedAgents.length > 0 || backgroundedShells.length > 0` (PrettyView local OR-composition). On a session with idle PTY + non-empty backgroundedShells the two surfaces disagreed (dot ON + WipBubble mounted simultaneously — Ashley's observed complaint). Investigation confirmed tier-2 pane-scrape / `/sessions/list` extension NOT needed: `AppShell.tsx:1832-1863`'s `tabs.map` renders every open tab with `shouldAttach = inPane || activeInline || isInActiveSet`, so Terminal WS-attaches for every active-set tab, and active-set tabs in pretty-mode also mount PrettyView — its claude-session WS is receiving `backgrounded_agents`/`backgrounded_shells` frames continuously for all active-set panes, matching the coverage the sidebar dot already had. Tier-1 client-only unify therefore gives full parity. Design: store schema widened from `Map<key, boolean|null>` to `Map<key, { ttyBusy: boolean|null; hasBgWork: boolean }>` with two independent publishers (`publishSessionTtyBusy`, `publishSessionHasBackgroundedWork`) and one derived hook `useSessionIsWorking(key): boolean` returning `state.ttyBusy === true || state.hasBgWork`. Null ttyBusy counts as not-working (preserves today's suppress-dot-until-first-emit semantics). Per-field no-op notify guard: publishing an unchanged ttyBusy does NOT re-notify even after an intervening hasBgWork publish (and vice versa) — prevents redundant re-renders across the sidebar row set. PrettyView publishes on both `case "backgrounded_agents"` / `case "backgrounded_shells"` WS branches + both reset paths (fresh-pane mount + session_changed); the sibling-array read inside the WS closure uses the functional-setter-as-reader trick (`setBackgroundedShells(cur => { publish(...); return cur; })`) to read the other array's latest value without adding refs. WipBubble mount + sidebar dot both consume the single hook — one place to change the composition rule, both surfaces move in lockstep. `wipActive` const removed (dead code — only reference was the old WipBubble mount). Four atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `a85606d refactor(session-working-store)` (composite shape + 11 tests A–K covering per-field independence + composite OR + edge cases) + `3661cca refactor(terminal)` (pure symbol rename publishSessionWorking → publishSessionTtyBusy) + `be63a68 feat(pretty-view)` (imports + key derivation + 2 WS-case publishes + 2 reset-path publishes + WipBubble rewire + dead-const removal) + `06fac7c refactor(pretty-conversations)` (Panel hook swap + 4 test-mock updates: Panel/chain/new-role-button/clone-dialog). `PrettyConversationRow`'s `isWorking?: boolean\|null` prop preserved (strict-equals gates accept plain boolean fine); narrowing happens naturally at the Panel boundary. Deviations from plan: (a) Task 1 committed as a single atomic commit (store + tests together) — consumers break at type level anyway pending Tasks 2–4 across the sequence, splitting store code from store tests produces the same red intermediate state either way; commit body documents intentional temporary app-wide typecheck red across Tasks 1–3 recovered at Task 4. (b) PrettyView WS-mock tests (Wip A–D) dropped per plan's escape hatch — the 37 existing PrettyView tests use complex WS stub infrastructure that would have required substantial rework to add backgrounded_agents/shells emission without breaking siblings; Task 1's store tests A–F already prove composite OR at the store level. (c) Intermittent `EnvironmentTeardownError` in `IdentityModal.test.tsx` is a pre-existing Vitest teardown race (reproducible on baseline `aa38697`, unrelated to this change). Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0; full `npx vitest run` = **1491 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 122 files** (+7 net-new vs baseline 1484/6/0, zero regressions). | 260806-gig | Phase 25 role-clustering hotfix — fold role frontmatter read into the per-host session enumeration path so remote identities' roles actually resolve. Phase 25 (shipped 2026-08-05) added a sort tuple (host, role, label) so conversation rows of the same role cluster within their host tier, but the clustering silently no-ops in production because role resolution used the LOCAL branch of `resolveRoleForIdentity` at `identities.ts:82` which reads role frontmatter from skynet-ec2's own filesystem — only tina's identity file lives on skynet-ec2, so every other fleet identity came back with `role: null` and the sort fell back to plain label-alpha. Ashley confirmed 2026-08-06 via `/identities` as tina admin: poppy, patricia, pixie, nicole, vicky all null. | 260805-7rq | Fix pre-existing Phase 23 React useEffect race in `src/ui/features/pretty-view/GlobalFilesModal.tsx:143` — surfaced during Ashley's UAT of 70q ship. Symptom: modal spins forever on thenasty read even though nginx access log showed `POST /global-files/read` returning 200 with 3507 bytes in ~1s. Root cause: the lazy-load useEffect had `tabData` in its deps array — sequence (1) effect fires on activeTab change → `setTabData({loading})` mutates tabData reference → (2) React re-runs effect (tabData in deps) → cleanup from (1) sets `cancelled = true` → (3) new effect body sees `tabData.has(activeTab)` truthy (loading placeholder) → early-returns without new cleanup → (4) original `readGlobalFile` promise resolves ~700ms later → sees `cancelled = true` → never calls `setTabData(ready)` → spinner forever. Pre-70q-tilde-fix, pre-fix backend returned empty content near-instantaneously so the promise resolved in the same microtask batch as `setTabData(loading)` and React collapsed them into one commit — dodging the race; the 70q tilde-expansion fix made the SSH round-trip a real ~700ms, exposing the pre-existing bug. TDD fix: RED regression test in new `src/ui/features/pretty-view/GlobalFilesModal.test.tsx` mocks `readGlobalFile` with `setTimeout(50)` macrotask delay + asserts `screen.getByRole("textbox").value === "MOCKED FILE CONTENT"` (positive READY assertion); failed against unpatched code with `Expected: true, Received: null` (textarea never rendered, spinner stayed indefinitely) — race reproduction confirmed. GREEN: dropped `tabData` from deps array (`[selectedHostId, activeTab, tabData]` → `[selectedHostId, activeTab]`), added `// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps` with WHY comment referencing the race and the intentional stale-closure semantics of the `tabData.has(activeTab)` gate. Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `5c88555 test(quick-260805-7rq-01)` + `4a2310e fix(quick-260805-7rq-01)`. Verification: `npm run build` EXIT 0 in 4.54s; full `npx vitest run` = **1415 pass / 6 skip / 0 fail across 118 files** (+1 new race test vs 70q's 1414/6/117, zero regressions). Fleet rule "never leave tests failing" honored. Shipped: image `f6d1c760cff1` built + pushed to origin + `docker compose up -d --force-recreate skynet` → healthy in 30s; AppShell bundle hash rotated `AppShell-LN847MEC.js` → `AppShell-CFWDHV7h.js` confirming new frontend in-container. Also this session (data change, no code commit): updated `/var/lib/docker/volumes/skynet_skynet-data/_data/global-files.json` to cover every non-RDP fleet host per Ashley: added termix, GIGAASHLEYPC, ashley-laptop, laura-mac, lisa-mac, aither-cloud2-prod (6 new); removed 4 stale entries whose hostname keys no longer matched any fleet host (skynet-ec2, aither-cloud, aither-cloud2, aither-sftp). Current config: 10 hosts × `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`. | 260803-1xw | PV paste post-send idle-watchdog + auto-retry Enter — closes pinned bounty `pv-paste-to-terminal-lands-as-unsent-bracket-paste`. Ashley's outcome-layer design fixing the class of bug that patches #100/#111/#118 kept losing at the byte layer. Tina attempted local reproduction across 300B/3KB/22KB/100KB/135KB with raw + bracketed-paste-wrapped + chunked injection — could NOT reproduce Ashley's failure in local tmux (all submitted), meaning the failure is specific to Skynet's real SSH pipeline. Rather than deploy instrumentation to capture the next reproduction, Ashley chose the watchdog approach: *"if you submit a message and the session is still detected as idle x seconds later or something, then it tries sending an enter or something,"* with the safety argument *"hitting enter on an otherwise empty box does nothing. So this isn't really a dangerous addition."* Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `2c7367f` (patch #287 — backend `src/backend/ssh/terminal.ts` watchdog on `isPrettyViewSubmit` path: snapshot `session.lastActivityAt` at send-fire time, arm setTimeout at T+2500ms — if `lastActivityAt` advanced → send worked, no-op; if stagnant → retry Enter via `tmux send-keys` matching #118 mechanism verbatim + re-snapshot; arm second setTimeout at T+5000ms — same check → recovery-succeeded log OR emit `{type:"paste_send_failed", mqid, reason:"no_activity_after_2_retries"}` to `attachedWs`. Watchdog handles tracked on new `TerminalSession.pvSubmitWatchdogs: Set<NodeJS.Timeout>` cancelled in both `destroySession` + `detachWs`. Arming logic mechanically extracted to sibling `terminal-pv-watchdog.ts` module for unit-testability without mounting the 3000-line WS dispatcher. Non-tmux fallback path arms nothing per plan guard T-6. 7 backend tests under `vi.useFakeTimers`) + `319499f` (patch #288 — frontend `Terminal.tsx` WS handler branch for `paste_send_failed` fires `toast.error(t("terminal.pasteSendFailed"), { duration: 8000 })` + `addLog`, reuses existing sonner + i18next imports, new i18n key `"Send didn't take effect after retry — try a shorter message or paste in chunks."`, 3 structural wiring tests). Design signal is `lastActivityAt` because it's updated on ANY pane stdout burst (existing mechanism), so if Claude Code prints even one character in response to the submit the watchdog knows it worked; and empty-box Enter is a no-op so the false-positive case (fast Claude reply, back to idle before watchdog check) is harmless. | 260802-uow | Composebox cluster — 4 pinned bounties, one coherent set of changes to the compose box send/mic/send-when-idle button trio + textarea layout. Closes pinned bounties `composebox-recording-doesnt-nuke-sibling-buttons` + `stt-loading-button-on-correct-textarea` + `composebox-right-padding-for-three-buttons` + `composebox-vertical-spacing-polish` (all four resolved this session). Root causes: (a) recording state ISN'T global across ComposeBoxes — `useVoiceRecording()` is called ONCE per ComposeBox (L333), but the visibility predicates for mic/arm-idle buttons gated on `voice.state === "idle"` instead of `micTarget !== <this textarea>`, so ANY textarea recording nuked ALL sibling mic + arm-idle buttons within the same ComposeBox; (b) same misgate meant the STT transcribing spinner (`Loader2` in the send-button slot) always landed on the primary regardless of which textarea recorded — slot send-button render had no Loader2 branch at all; (c) `pr-10` right padding sized for the always-present send button was too narrow when 3 buttons rendered (send @ right-1 + mic @ right-11 + arm-idle @ right-21 — arm-idle right edge lands ~124px from container edge); (d) two vertical-spacing measurements Ashley pulled from Chrome DevTools were off — Row 1 aux container had no bottom margin, queue-slots wrapper had no bottom margin so gap to primary was just outer `gap-1` (4px) rather than matching queued↔queued `gap-2` (8px). Fix — 3 atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`, ONE file (`src/ui/features/pretty-view/ComposeBox.tsx`): `754484f` scopes recording visibility per-textarea (Task 1 = bounties 1+2 — introduced `isPrimaryRecording`/`isPrimaryTranscribing`/`isSlotTranscribing`/`isSlotActiveMic` locals; rewrote `showMicButton`/`showSlotMic` to gate on `!<this-target's-active-mic>` instead of `voice.state === "idle"`; removed `voice.state === "idle"` gate from `showPrimaryArmButton`/`showSlotArmButton` entirely — arm-idle is orthogonal to recording elsewhere per Ashley's ask; added `showSlotTranscribingSend` + Loader2 render branch on slot send button mirroring primary's L2162; both MicButtons receive `disabled={voice.state !== "idle"}` — visible-but-disabled on siblings during any recording); `be67dea` conditional pr-32 (Task 2 = bounty 3 — `primaryThreeButtonState = showMicButton && showPrimaryArmButton`, `slotThreeButtonState = showSlotMic && showSlotArmButton`, append `... && "pr-32"` to base `pr-10` in both Textarea classNames; tailwind-merge later-wins dedupes cleanly); `9dec204` spacing polish (Task 3 = bounty 4 — `mb-[3px]` on Row 1 aux container using arbitrary bracket to preserve Ashley's DevTools-measured 3px exactly + `mb-1` on queue-slots wrapper so last-queued→primary gap = 4px `mb-1` + 4px outer `gap-1` = 8px matching internal `gap-2`). **What was explicitly not done**: NO refactor into per-textarea `useVoiceRecording()` hook instances — D-16-02 iOS Safari getUserMedia gesture lock forbids proliferating hook instances; `useVoiceRecording.ts` untouched; `MicButton.tsx` untouched (already accepted `disabled?: boolean` prop); button absolute positions (right-1 / right-11 / right-21) untouched. **Plan deviation**: Task 3 in plan was `checkpoint:human-verify` gate — pause for Ashley to visually verify all 4 bounties. Overridden at invocation time per fleet "no UAT check-ins, silence is success" rule; both spacing edits committed atomically like Tasks 1 + 2. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; `npx eslint` clean (5 pre-existing warnings, none new); full `npx vitest run` = **88 test files pass, 1064 tests pass, 6 skipped, 0 failed** (`ComposeBox.voice.test.tsx` 12/12 + `ComposeBox.test.tsx` 33/33 no updates needed — no existing test asserted "mic hidden while recording elsewhere"). Grep gates all green: new predicates present at expected line ranges; `const isSlotIdle` removed; both `disabled={voice.state !== "idle"}` MicButton sites confirmed; `Loader2` renders in both primary + slot; both `pr-32` sites present; `mb-[3px]` + `mb-1` present. | 260802-tzx | Add `attach: boolean` prop to Terminal — decouple WebSocket lifecycle from pane visibility so URL-restored active-set tabs actually connect (not just glow). Closes pinned bounty `url-restore-loads-only-selected-session-not-full-active-set`. Patch #230 (2026-07-31) enrolled URL-restored ambient tabs into activeSet so they rendered with active-set chrome, but their WS never opened — `isWorking` in `session-working-store` stayed null and the ready-dot in `PrettyConversationRow.tsx:507` lit up as "idle and ready for attention" on tabs that weren't actually reachable, violating Ashley's locked dot-semantics (must mean idle AND connected). Design settled with Ashley 2026-08-02 verbatim: *"we probably just want to actually load in the other sessions. If we fake it now, then there's just going to be other features that break at some point... clearly they are capable of handling that many sessions running at the same time."* → full attach, no side-channel poll. Root cause chain: `Terminal.tsx` L2813-2844 WS-open effect was gated on `isVisible` (passed from `AppShell.tsx` L1794 as `inPane || activeInline` where `activeInline = tab.id === effectiveSelectedTabId`); every tab is already portal-mounted (L1773-1801 `createPortal` per tab, no remount on selection change), but only the currently-selected tab's WS opened; `session-working-store.ts` L70-81 `publishSessionWorking` is only fed by `Terminal.tsx` L246-258 which needs WS-driven `isIdle`, so the whole `isWorking` publish chain was gated on `isVisible=true`. Fix: decouple "should I open a WebSocket" from "am I the currently-visible pane." Add `attach: boolean` prop to Terminal that gates WS lifecycle; keep `isVisible` for its existing meaning (render focus, fit, pointer-events, isVisibleRef mirror). Terminal.tsx L2814 predicate + L2844 deps swapped from `isVisible` → `attach`; **5 pane-visibility uses of `isVisible` untouched** (isVisibleRef at L562-563, performFit at L603, resize-observer at L2430, fit-on-visible at L2849, autocomplete pointer-events at L2940). Threading: `TerminalTabContent` (`tabUtils.tsx:90-136`) accepts + forwards `attach`; `renderTabContent` (L138-190) accepts new `shouldAttach: boolean = false` as 5th positional param after `isVisible`; AppShell adds `useActiveSet` to the existing `@/state/conversation-store` import block, calls `const activeSet = useActiveSet()` alongside `useSelectedConversationId`, computes `const isInActiveSet = activeSet.has(tab.id)` + `const shouldAttach = inPane || activeInline || isInActiveSet` inside the L1773 `tabs.map`, threads `shouldAttach` as 6th positional arg to `renderTabContent`. Verified: no WS-teardown-on-!isVisible currently exists so once opened WS stays alive across visibility changes until unmount/close — same as today for selected tab when switching away. Executor deviations: none material — Task 1 tsc gate expected type-errors mid-refactor but `tsconfig.app.json` has `strict:false` so no missing-required-prop JSX errors surfaced (wiring in Tasks 2-3 completes the picture by design); grep-count invariant on raw `grep -c "isVisible"` went 12→11 rather than 12→10 because the mandated explanatory comment contains the string `isVisible` as prose (invariant that matters — all 5 pane-visibility uses intact — holds). **TESTING-GAP**: neither `Terminal.test.tsx` nor `AppShell.test.tsx` exists in the fork; the two behavioral guarantees needing direct coverage — (a) WS opens when `attach=true` even if `isVisible=false`, and (b) URL-restore of N tabs produces N WS opens (all N tabs receive `attach=true`, not just the selected one) — have no automated coverage in this commit. Follow-up should stand up both test files with a WS-constructor spy + mock `TerminalTabContent` recording `attach` per tab id. Ashley explicitly forbade fabricating brittle test scaffolding here. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; `npm test` = **88 test files pass, 1064 tests pass, 6 skipped, 0 failures** (~150s). One atomic commit `714e238` on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`. NOT pushed, NOT image-built, NOT deployed per code-work-doesn't-authorize-ship rule (Ashley 2026-07-27). Ships as patch #273 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. Deploy queue extends from patches #267→#272 → +#273; still HELD until Ashley's ship word ends the pinned-rotation ship-together. | 260802-rps | Replace patch #63 JSONL scan for `ExitPlanMode` with a tmux pane screen-scrape so `PlanPendingBubble` renders live while a plan is pending. Closes bounty `plan-pending-via-tmux-scrape` (self-discovered mid-session diagnosing Moxie/workstation — Ashley reported PlanPendingBubble not showing during her Moxie plan-approval). Root cause proven live: Claude Code 2.1.150's `ExitPlanModeV2Tool` BUFFERS the `ExitPlanMode` tool_use in Ink UI memory and only flushes to the parent JSONL when the user resolves the approval prompt (57-minute gap observed on Moxie between model call and JSONL write — ExitPlanMode tool_use finally appeared at line 593 with its original 18:56:52 timestamp at the exact moment Ashley approved at 19:53:42). Fleet-wide 2.1.150 pin didn't and couldn't guard against this — the change was Anthropic-side model behavior (deferred-tool loading via `ToolSearch(select:ExitPlanMode)` → `ExitPlanMode(...)`) coupled with Claude Code choosing V2's buffered-persistence path. Neither is version-gated. Fix: new pure `isPlanPending(paneText: string): boolean` helper mirroring `context-pct-parser.ts`'s shape (bottom-30-lines slice + header-anywhere), fingerprint = load-bearing `No, keep planning` reject-option marker in bottom slice (present in every plan-approval variant regardless of `--dangerously-skip-permissions` — that flag only changes options 1/2, never the last "keep planning" option) AND one of `Here is Claude's plan:` OR `Ready to code?` anywhere in pane (both header variants — bypass-permissions and default). Both conditions required — either alone false-positives on prose/transcript quotes. Wire-in piggybacks on the same setInterval that already calls `parseContextPct(output)`, so zero extra SSH round-trip. WS message shape (`{type:"plan_pending", pending}`) UNCHANGED; `planFilePath: ""` because pane text doesn't carry it reliably and `PlanPendingBubble.tsx` doesn't consume it for rendering. Frontend handler + component untouched. Legacy patch #63 JSONL scan (~L1200-1270 in `claude-session-server.ts`) LEFT IN PLACE with 5-line deprecation comment block documenting the V2 discovery — retained as harmless belt-and-suspenders (V2 flush re-emits pending:null at resolution edge, coalesces cleanly with pane-scrape's own null-emit) and preserves backward-compat with any older Claude Code sessions still writing ExitPlanMode eagerly. | 260802-qrw | Fix identity-modal save failure — swap `sftp.rename` → `sftp.ext_openssh_rename` in `writeMarkdownFileAtomic`. Closes bounty `identity-modal-save-sftp-rename-eexist` (high-priority, inbound from Stacy's ceo-skynet handoff at `~/pretty-view-uploads/2026-08-02/190204-TINA-HANDOFF.md`). Root cause: SFTPv3 `SSH_FXP_RENAME` cannot atomically overwrite — OpenSSH's `process_rename` tries `link(old, new)` first, `link()` returns `EEXIST` on existing target, and `errno_to_portable()` falls through to `SSH2_FX_FAILURE` (client sees generic `Error: Failure` code 4). Fix: `sftp.ext_openssh_rename` uses `posix-rename@openssh.com` (POSIX `rename(2)` semantics — atomic overwrite; supported by every OpenSSH ≥5.1). ONE helper swap in `writeMarkdownFileAtomic` fixes all four consumers (`writeIdentityFile`, `writeIdentityHistory`, `writeIdentityHandoff`, `writeIdentityBountyFields`). My box-side investigation confirmed identical config with ceo-skynet on all Stacy's dimensions (env unset, no bind-mount, ssh2 1.17.0, OpenSSH 9.6p1, same code — HEAD 26 commits ahead of `cd95a61` but zero touched this file). Ashley confirmed all modal-save attempts have been on EXISTING identities (`/id` creates the file before you can talk to that identity, so first-write can't happen via modal) — so per the bug every save should fail, yet Ashley reports "sometimes works and sometimes doesn't"; that pattern remains unexplained but fix supersedes the mystery. Scope-excluded: `src/backend/ssh/pretty-view-upload.ts:239` also uses `sftp.rename` but renames onto collision-resolved non-existent paths only (`resolveNonCollidingFinal` + `reservedFinalPaths`), so EEXIST can't hit it — left alone. TDD execution: RED regression test written FIRST with throwing `rename` spy (diagnostic `must not call sftp.rename — use ext_openssh_rename` fires on any future revert); RED passed (test fails against unmodified code, confirming trap is load-bearing); GREEN swap + JSDoc rewrite; all gates green. One atomic commit: `042235e`. Verification: `npm run build:backend` PASS (backend patch — Tina's learned rule: `tsc --noEmit` doesn't catch backend TS errors); `npm run build` PASS; `npx vitest run src/backend/claude-session/identity-artifact-reader` = **6 files / 26 tests / 0 failures** (23 existing + 3 new regression tests in new file `identity-artifact-reader.remote-writes.test.ts`); grep gate `grep -c "sftp\.rename(" src/backend/claude-session/identity-artifact-reader.ts` = 0 executable call sites (JSDoc references to old API preserved for pedagogical value). No deviations from plan. NOT pushed, NOT image-built, NOT deployed per code-work-doesn't-authorize-ship rule (Ashley 2026-07-27). Ships as patch #268 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. Deploy queue: `2318460`+`8c9ea5e` (patch #267) + `042235e` (patch #268); all HELD. Downstream: Stacy pulls upstream on next fork-pull cycle. OPEN QUESTION carried in bounty: unexplained "sometimes works" pattern for overwrites — fix hides the symptom; revisit only if Ashley cares about mechanism. Rebase risk: nil (Phase 18 fork-authored). | 2026-08-02 | `042235e` | [260802-qrw-fix-identity-modal-save-failure-swap-sft](./quick/260802-qrw-fix-identity-modal-save-failure-swap-sft/) |

Tests: 4 row swipe tests + 4 panel swipe-strip content tests deleted; 6 new row long-press tests added (TL1-TL5a/b covering happy-path, movement-cancel, short-tap-still-selects, RDP-guard, vibrate-with-and-without-API). | 260801-75z | Vehicle C — refactor ComposeBox single-slot armed queue (patch #84 `queuedText: string \| null`) into per-source FIFO (`queue: Array<{source, text}>`) with per-textarea arm-idle buttons. Ashley design-driven (multi-round design conversation this session, following Vehicle A patch #241 bounty-bounty voice trigger + Vehicle B patch #242 aux-row cleanup). Design origin: pinned bounty `audit-composebox-button-icons-and-implementation` Ashley verbatim from the design conversation: *"we should move the send when idle button to become a per text area button, because you could enact that on any text area, not just the main one"* + *"if they went in order, because then you kind of unlock a functionality that you don't have otherwise, where you can queue up multiple messages and, you know, be feeding messages into the session while you're not even at the keyboard over multiple turns."* State machine changes: (a) state: `queuedText: string \| null` → `queue: QueueEntry[]` where `QueueEntry = {source: "primary" \| slotId, text: string}`; (b) per-source helpers `armSourceForIdle(source, text)` (updates in-place if source already queued — preserves FIFO position on re-arm) + `cancelSourceArmed(source)` (source-scoped) + `isSourceArmed(source)`; (c) `fireQueuedDispatch` → `fireNextQueued` — dispatches HEAD of queue, on success clears that source's textarea (primary → `setText("")`; slot → filter out of `queueSlots` + reschedule autosave); (d) idle-watchdog gate `queuedText !== null` → `queue.length > 0` — after each fire session goes working → idle again → next head fires (natural sequential cadence, no explicit chaining); (e) `handleQueue` (aux-row single-slot toggle) DELETED; (f) immediate-action cancels source-scoped: `handleSend` dequeues primary only, `handleQueueSlotSend` dequeues that slot only, `handleResetSend` dequeues primary only, `handleQuickSend` (thumbs-up/recap) doesn't touch queue at all (textarea-independent); (g) keydown gate `queuedText !== null` → `isSourceArmed("primary")`; (h) `queueArmed` old derived block deleted (still exists in new state block as `queue.length > 0` for hasty-anything-queued checks); (i) `showMicButton` Vehicle C v2 derivation gates on `!primaryArmed` but NOT text emptiness (260729-3y1 lock: mic always reachable regardless of text content); (j) new `showPrimaryArmButton` gates on `!primaryArmed && text.trim() !== "" && !recycleActive`. UI changes: (a) aux-row Hourglass Queue button REMOVED (replaced by short explanatory comment); (b) per-textarea arm-idle button added at right-21 bottom-0.5 on primary AND each queueSlot — one slot LEFT of mic (right-11), coexisting rather than mutex; RotateCwFadingClock icon size-6 with warm-neutral treatment; (c) primary + each slot get click-to-cancel armed overlay ("Queued — waiting for idle" scrim, whole scrim is now a `<button>` with pointer-events-auto so clicking anywhere cancels that source's armed entry); (d) queueSlot `<Textarea>` gets `disabled={slotArmed}` prop; slot Send button gets `|| slotArmed` in disabled predicate. UAT bug caught + fixed: v1 shipped with mic hiding on non-empty text (mic and arm-idle mutex sharing right-11), Ashley immediate response: *"Why in the world would we get rid of the mic button if there is text in the box already?"* — violated the 260729-3y1 "mic must stay tappable even with dirty text" lock. v2 moved arm-idle to right-21 (one slot LEFT), Ashley UAT'd v2: *"good to go"*. Design origin: typing `/` is Ashley's one remaining keyboard-reach in an otherwise voice-native workflow; the doubled word is a redundancy STT cannot lose, no natural English sentence starts with a word repeated back-to-back at the front, so false-positive rate is effectively zero. Registry-gated (ships with a single entry `bounty` → `/bounty`) so common English doublings ("no no", "bye bye", "very very") don't fire even if the regex matches. Regex `/^\s*([a-z]+)[\s.,;:!?\-]+\1[\s.,;:!?\-]+(\S.*)$/is`: `[a-z]+` letters-only for keyword precision, `i` flag case-insensitive on both first-word and backref, `s` flag makes `.` cross newlines, punctuation-tolerant on BOTH sides of the second word (faster-whisper habitually inserts commas/periods there — empirically validated 2026-08-01 via scratch/UAT with Ashley's voice), requires at least one whitespace-or-punct char between the doubled words (so `bountybounty` glued form does NOT match) and requires non-empty content after (so bare "bounty bounty" does NOT match — probably an accidental send). Vehicle sizing per ROADMAP check: no phase in ROADMAP.md, ~55-line pure-function module + ~180-line 25-case vitest suite + 4 tiny inline wraps in ComposeBox = one cohesive unit of work with locked design (multi-round scratch/UAT iteration already completed) → `/gsd:quick` (not plan-phase). Scratch path per patch #221 lesson: built in working tree WITHOUT committing → `docker cp dist/assets/. skynet:/app/html/assets/` + `docker cp dist/index.html skynet:/app/html/index.html` → Ashley UAT with real voice → she confirmed "worked!" → `git checkout --` scratch → redo via `/gsd:quick` for the clean shipping commits. Three atomic TDD commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `58bd386` (test, RED — 25-case suite: 10 passthrough cases including glued `bountybounty`, mid-sentence doubled `foo bounty bounty add`, `bounty bounty` alone with no content, common English `no no do not do that`, already-slash-prefixed `/bounty add a thing`; 15 transform cases including all 4 Whisper-shape UAT captures verbatim: "Bounty, bounty. Buzz, buzz, I'm a bee." → "/bounty Buzz, buzz, I'm a bee.", "Bounty bounty, bananas are delicious." → "/bounty bananas are delicious.", "bounty bounty. add a thing", "bounty bounty, add a thing"), `0455e76` (feat, GREEN — module implementation with JSDoc header explaining voice-first origin + front-only anchoring + registry gate + requires-content contract + punctuation-tolerance-both-sides + empty-match-returns-passthrough), `85ff865` (feat, integration — wraps exactly 4 `onSend(payload)` call sites in ComposeBox.tsx with `applyIntentTransform(collapseNewlinesForSend(...)).transformed`: `fireQueuedDispatch` L763, `handleQueueSlotSend` L903, `handleSend` primary path L959, `handleVoiceSend` queue-slot branch L1039; plus `import { applyIntentTransform } from "./composeIntentTransform";` at L15 beside other pretty-view sibling imports). Deliberately NOT wrapped (grep-verified): attachment-caption path (`onSendWithAttachments` around L947 — attachments have their own flow, `/bounty` skill can't consume an attachment), reset-send path (`handleResetSend` around L1122 — wraps with `/id reset (...)` prefix, no user-authored front for intent transform to fire on), quick-reply path (`handleQuickSend` around L1153 — sends canned strings like "thumbs up", no user-authored input). Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0 (clean); scoped `npx vitest run src/ui/features/pretty-view/composeIntentTransform.test.ts` = 25/25 passed; regression `npx vitest run src/ui/features/pretty-view/ComposeBox` = 78/78 passed (exact baseline from scratch iteration preserved); capture-and-grep gate on both vitest logs for `FAIL|failed|✗|Unhandled` per tina.md L508 preference (#209→#211 arc — runner's "0 failed" claim isn't authoritative if unhandled rejections tank tests silently) = 0 real failure markers. Backend not touched → `npm run build:backend` skipped per patch #154 mandatory-for-backend-touching rule (letter check: `grep -l "src/backend" ...` = 0 matches on staged files). | 260801-29v | Fix pretty-view file-upload race — chunk arriving before `activeBatches.set()` emitted `upload_failed reason=unknown_temp_id message="no active batch"`. Ashley bounty-driven (`file-upload-unknown-temp-id-no-active-batch` — pinned, verbatim parked earlier session via `/bounty`: "figure out file upload issues (unknown_temp_id: no active batc..)"). Root cause: `src/backend/ssh/terminal.ts:885` dispatched `void handleUploadStart(...)` fire-and-forget. `handleUploadStart` is async and performs real SFTP I/O over the SSH tunnel (`sftp.realpath(".")` + `ensureLandingDir` stat/mkdir + per-file `resolveNonCollidingFinal` stat calls + `createWriteStream`) before it calls `activeBatches.set(mqid, batch)` at pretty-view-upload.ts:460. Client-side pump (`src/ui/features/pretty-view/use-pretty-view-uploads.ts:414`) fires `void pumpBatch(batchId)` immediately after the `upload_start` WS send, so 64KB chunks arrive back-to-back on the same tick. Synchronous `case "upload_chunk"` (terminal.ts:892) then runs `handleUploadChunk` which does `activeBatches.get(mqid)` → undefined → emits the failure at pretty-view-upload.ts:558. Intermittent — hits on cold SFTP session (first upload) or slower I/O; misses on warm sessions. Ashley reproduced with a 162.7 KB text file. Vehicle sizing per ROADMAP check: no phase in ROADMAP.md, one focused unit of work with locked design → `/gsd:quick` (not plan-phase). Fix: added per-WS `pendingStarts: Map<string, Promise<void>>` in terminal.ts's WS closure adjacent to `ownedUploadBatches`; `case "upload_start"` captures the `handleUploadStart` promise, stores it in `pendingStarts` keyed by `messageQueueItemId`, self-cleans with `.finally(() => pendingStarts.delete(mqid))` (still non-blocking at dispatch level — other WS traffic like input/resize is not gated on SFTP setup); `case "upload_chunk"` looks up `pendingStarts.get(mqid)` and if a promise is present chains `handleUploadChunk` via `.then(...)` (deferring the chunk until the batch is registered), else calls `handleUploadChunk` synchronously as today (warm-session fast path preserved verbatim — no spurious microtask on the common case). JS microtask FIFO ordering preserves chunk-arrival order across multiple deferred chunks. No client-side changes, no new WS event, no added round-trip. Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `49cba54` (fix — +48/-9 in terminal.ts) then `d46e1bd` (regression test — +173 in pretty-view-upload.test.ts, two new `describe` blocks reusing existing mocks). Test A (race): fires `upload_start` and `upload_chunk` on the same tick with SFTP realpath/mkdir mocks delayed via `setTimeout(r, 0)`, asserts no `upload_failed reason=unknown_temp_id message="no active batch"` emitted AND chunk lands (`upload_progress` fires). Test B (fast path): after start settles, chunk runs synchronously with no `.then()` deferral. Race-capture proof: executor temporarily reverted the test-local dispatcher to the pre-fix fire-and-forget shape → Test A failed with exactly the expected error string → restored fixed helpers → Test A passes; confirms the test is sensitive to the race the terminal.ts change fixes. Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 per tina.md L508 mandatory-for-backend-touching rule; `npm run build` EXIT 0 (4.69s); `npx vitest run src/backend/ssh/pretty-view-upload.test.ts` = 17/17 passed. Scope check per commit: `git show --name-only 49cba54` = 1 file (`src/backend/ssh/terminal.ts`), `git show --name-only d46e1bd` = 1 file (`src/backend/ssh/pretty-view-upload.test.ts`) — no changes to `pretty-view-upload.ts` orchestrator, no client-side edits, no identity-side files touched (per h8f-vs-hn8 executor-scope split — Tina updates skynet-patches.md + bounty archive AS ORCHESTRATOR after this row); no git worktrees used (fleet rule 2026-07-31 verified via `workflow.use_worktrees=false`); no push, no `docker build`, no `docker compose up`, no deploy at commit time. Ships as patch #239 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. Closes pinned bounty `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/file-upload-unknown-temp-id-no-active-batch/`. Eleventh atomic entry in the pinned-bounties multi-session work-through-all-pinned-bounties arc. Rebase risk: nil — terminal.ts + pretty-view-upload.test.ts are fork-authored (Phase 05 upload orchestrator). | 2026-08-01 | `d46e1bd` | [260801-29v-fix-file-upload-race-chunk-arrives-befor](./quick/260801-29v-fix-file-upload-race-chunk-arrives-befor/) |

| 260731-r81 | Fix "Server connection lost, recovering…" toast that fired when the pretty-view speak button was used on a long assistant message. Ashley bounty-driven (`speak-button-long-message-triggers-connection-lost-popup` — pinned, verbatim parked earlier session via `/bounty`). Root cause: both the frontend axios `postSpeak` (bound to `authApi`'s default 30s at `main-axios.ts:306`) and the backend `handleSpeak` AbortController (`voice.ts:147`, `setTimeout(..., 30_000)`) timed out before Chatterbox TTS finished synthesizing long text — one path throws `ECONNABORTED`, the other returns 504 `{error: "TTS timeout"}`; both land in `main-axios.ts:530`'s response interceptor which calls `dbHealthMonitor.reportDatabaseError()`, `isBackendUnreachable` matches on `ECONNABORTED` and on the `"timeout"` substring rule, and the toast fires. 30s cap was inherited unthinkingly from `handleTranscribe`'s T-16-02 STT timeout when patch #223 added TTS last session — STT clips are small + fast, TTS synthesis scales with text length, and `SPEAK_TEXT_MAX = 25000` can take minutes at Chatterbox's rate. Vehicle sizing: no phase in ROADMAP.md, ~10 lines across two files, no design decisions → `/gsd:quick` (not plan-phase). Two atomic code commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `d7ab821` bumps `handleSpeak`'s AbortController to `300_000` ms (5 min) with an inline comment explaining the 10× asymmetry vs `handleTranscribe`; `c8f75ae` adds explicit `timeout: 300_000` to `postSpeak`'s axios config object overriding the 30s default, with matching inline comment. Deliberately NOT touched (verified via `git diff --name-only HEAD~2 HEAD`): `handleTranscribe` (still 30s), `handleListVoices` (still 30s), `getVoices` (still authApi 30s default), `main-axios.ts` global default, `db-health-monitor.ts::isBackendUnreachable` predicate, all locale + AppShell toast wiring, `ChatMessage.speak.test.tsx` + `IdentityModal.voice.test.tsx` (both mock `postSpeak` at the module boundary so timeout value isn't observed — remained green untouched). Sanity greps corroborate: `30_000` count in `voice.ts` = 2, `300_000` count = 1, `timeout: 300_000` in `voice-api.ts` = 1, zero timeout key in `getVoices`. Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 per patch #154 mandatory-for-backend-touching rule; `npm run build` EXIT 0 (4.28s); full `npx vitest run --reporter=verbose` = **947 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 82 files** — matches `8e01c93` baseline exactly; independent grep of `/tmp/r81-vitest.log` for `FAIL|failed|✗` per tina.md L508 preference (#209→#211 arc) — 17 hits all benign (application-level failure-path log lines + verbose reporter printing test names containing "failed" + expected voice-api Network Error tests); zero `Unhandled|Rejection` matches outside expected scopes. Scope check per commit: `git show --name-only d7ab821` = 1 file (`voice.ts`), `git show --name-only c8f75ae` = 1 file (`voice-api.ts`) — no identity-side files touched (per h8f-vs-hn8 executor-scope split, Tina updates identity-side AS ORCHESTRATOR after this row); no git worktrees (fleet rule verified via `workflow.use_worktrees=false`); no push, no `docker build`, no recreate — stopped at commit boundary per fleet rule (Ashley 2026-07-27). | 260731-2pa | Replace the raw JSON schedule textarea in `WakeupsTab.tsx` (patch #154's raw editor) with a form-based schedule editor. Ashley bounty-driven (`identity-modal-wakeup-form-editor` — pinned, verbatim: "a proper form UI for editing wakeup schedules inside the identity modal — not a text-edit-the-JSON situation"). Sixth atomic entry in the pinned-bounties multi-session work-through-all-pinned-bounties arc (after #215 copy-button + #216 overlay-behind-badge + #217 attach-inside-textarea + #218 alphabetical-row-ordering + #219 identity title+avatar). Design work-through: I threw together an interactive HTML mockup at `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/identity-modal-wakeup-form-editor/prototype.html` served over the tailnet — Ashley signed off "ship it" verbatim, so the prototype is the locked source of truth for form shape / field layout / per-type conditional swap / live JSON preview / silent Intl-based timezone detection. Two atomic commits: `1bfffc0` (backend widen — `WakeupUpdate` type + WS validator + wire payload extended to accept `name?` + `instruction?`) then `8b1989e` (frontend rewrite — WakeupsTab.tsx form editor + widened `OnUpdate` signature in IdentityModal.tsx + new WakeupsTab.test.tsx). Form fields (edit mode): editable `name` text input; schedule-type dropdown (interval/daily/weekly/one_shot) that swaps per-type fields underneath — interval: number + unit picker (s/m/h/d); daily: `<input type="time">`; weekly: day-of-week dropdown + time; one_shot: `<input type="datetime-local">` with inline red "fires this immediately" hint on past values — editable `instruction` textarea; secondary `enabled` checkbox (in addition to the always-visible header on/off chip which stays untouched from patch #154). Timezone: NO dropdown UI. Silently detected via `Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone` on mount, fallback `'America/New_York'` if Intl returns undefined/empty/'UTC'. Written into `schedule.timezone` on daily/weekly/one_shot saves; OMITTED from interval schedules (scheduler no-ops timezone on interval per tina.md § Scheduled wake-ups). Detected TZ shown as small muted hint under per-type fields on daily/weekly/one_shot only. Live JSON preview alongside the form (right column ≥620px, stacked below on mobile) — updates every keystroke via `useMemo`, matches the exact wire payload shape. Client-side shallow validation blocks Save on obvious errors (interval `every` regex, daily/weekly `at` regex, weekly `day` allowlist, one_shot `at` non-NaN parse) — server remains authoritative. `hydrateFormSchedule` round-trips existing schedules back into form fields; unrecognized shapes silently fall back to `{type: 'daily', at: '09:00'}` on entry — acceptable trade for a simple entry path since old hand-edited JSON is rare. Preserves patch #154's `useEffect` reset on server refresh (extended to also reset name + instruction drafts) and the "Scheduler reloads every ~30s" hint next to Save/Cancel. Backend widening was minimal: `identity-artifact-reader.ts` `WakeupUpdate` type + writer branch + remote-branch python one-liner already generic (`for k,v in u.items(): d[k]=v` merges any key); `claude-session-server.ts` WS handler validators added for the two new optional fields; `claude-session-api.ts` `IdentityUpdateWakeupPayload.updates` type widened. **Executor auto-fix (Rule 1 — Bug):** Test 4's `getByText` matcher for the past-datetime hint used regex `/fires this immediately/i` which failed to match because the hint has an inline `<b>immediately</b>` splitting the phrase across DOM children; fixed by asserting the two fragments separately (`/that datetime is in the past/i` for the text node + `"immediately"` for the `<b>` inner text). Same assertion, valid split. | 260731-1c8 | Add title + avatar editing to the IdentityModal Identity tab. Ashley bounty-driven (`identity-editing-modal` — pinned, verbatim "there are only like two things that you can customize about identity within the app which is the avatar and the title and so just having a place to adjust that felt like it would be a good addition and we could put it in the modal that comes up when you click on like the avatar when you're already in the session"). Fifth atomic entry in the pinned-bounties multi-session work-through-all-pinned-bounties arc (after #215 copy-button + #216 overlay-behind-badge + #217 attach-inside-textarea + #218 alphabetical-row-ordering). Scope locked to EXACTLY two editable fields: `title` (tagline) + `avatar` (image). `displayName` deliberately NOT exposed as editable — Ashley verbatim: "I was not thinking of editing display name um because it ties into like what that identity is called but the title and the avatar could be adjusted." `colorHue` exists in the backend but out of scope. Wires to existing `PUT /identities/:id` backend endpoint (`src/backend/database/routes/identities.ts:170` — multer FormData with JSON `data` field for `{title}` + optional `avatar` file, PNG/JPEG/WebP, 2MB cap, MD5 etag; auth-gated with JWT + userId scoping). Follows patch #154's write-path idiom (wakeup editor + bounty priority editor in the SAME modal — "first WRITE path on identity artifacts") — mutation callback threaded from modal, fresh-state applied on success. Deviates only in transport: HTTP FormData rather than WebSocket. **Fix (commit `80c0327`):** `src/ui/features/pretty-view/IdentityModal.tsx` — added imports (updateIdentity + applyIdentityChange), editor state hooks (titleDraft, committedTitle, avatarDraft, savingState, saveError, avatarPreviewUrl), reset effect (syncs draft state when identity prop changes or modal opens), cleanup effect (revokes createObjectURL on unmount / success / cancel), onAvatarPick handler (accepts File, generates optimistic preview via URL.createObjectURL), onSave handler (builds FormData with `data: JSON.stringify({title: titleDraft.trim()})` + optional avatar file, calls updateIdentity, on success calls applyIdentityChange from @/state/identities-store to broadcast the updated row to every useIdentities consumer — IdentityBadge / SessionRow / PrettyConversationRow / RelayInboundBubble all update in one call, no separate refetch needed), onCancel handler (resets drafts + revokes preview URL). Header `<img>` cache-busted via `?v=${avatarEtag}` — since `avatarUrl` is `/identities/${id}/avatar` (same string across avatar changes), the query param forces browser to re-fetch after a successful save. Editor block added to Identity tab with always-editable inputs + Save/Cancel footer (planner picked shape over Edit-toggle for lighter-weight interaction). Deliberately NO client-preflight of file size/mime — server is source of truth, and tests explicitly assert the server error string flows through inline (via saveError state, not modal close). On failure typed title + picked file survive so retry doesn't lose the user's work. **Executor auto-fix (Rule 1 — Bug):** Save button disabled predicate originally compared `titleDraft` against the `identity.title` prop; after a successful save the parent prop doesn't re-render immediately, so the button wouldn't re-disable. Fixed by introducing `committedTitle` state that tracks the last successfully saved title and updating on save success. Tests: new file `src/ui/features/pretty-view/IdentityModal.test.tsx` (5-case suite) — edit-title happy path, avatar-upload FormData shape assertion (verifies the File is in the FormData), oversized-file inline error (>2MB triggers server-side 400 → error text renders in tab without modal close), wrong-mime inline error (gif triggers server-side 400 → error text renders inline), cancel-discards (typed title + picked file both reset when Cancel clicked, preview URL revoked). Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; backend files touched = 0 (endpoint already exists, so `npm run build:backend` skipped per plan); targeted `npx vitest run src/ui/features/pretty-view/IdentityModal.test.tsx` = 5/5 passed; full `npx vitest run 2>&1 | tee /tmp/fullsuite.log` = 880 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 78 files (up from #218 baseline of 875, +5 = new IdentityModal test suite — checks out); independent grep of `/tmp/fullsuite.log` per tina.md L508 preference (#209→#211 arc) — 0 real failure markers (all `FAIL|failed|✗|Unhandled` matches inside passing test descriptions like `upload_failed`, `failed attempts`, `fetch failed`, or expected backend log lines from passing error-path tests). Single atomic commit `80c0327` on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`. Scope check: `git show --name-only 80c0327` shows exactly the 2 declared files (`IdentityModal.tsx` + `IdentityModal.test.tsx`), both under `src/ui/features/pretty-view/` — no backend, no identity-side (`~/.claude/identities/tina/` clean per L578 executor-scope-audit), no scope creep. NO push, NO docker build, NO deploy at commit time — orchestrator handles bare push after per Ashley 2026-07-30 refinement (downstream consumption via Stacy/ceo-skynet, near-zero-blast-radius; skynet-ec2 push→build→recreate motion still HELD per handoff rule + Standing Directive against reflex deploy-recommendation on code-complete through-lines). Ships as patch #219 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. Closes pinned bounty `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/identity-editing-modal/` (Tina archives after this row + patch entry + bounty flip — orchestrator bookkeeping split off from executor per h8f-vs-hn8 learned preference; pin left intact on archived record per only-user-pins symmetric rule). Deploy queue extends to #198→#219 (44 unpushed-to-container commits); still HELD until Ashley's shape-lock ship word. | 260730-wfy | Universal alphabetical row ordering in the conversation list — activeSet, pinned, per-host grouped buckets, and RDP sentinel bucket all sort rows by `row.label` with `localeCompare(..., { numeric: true, sensitivity: "base" })` (case-insensitive, locale-aware, numeric-natural). Host ORDER in Tier 3 unchanged (hostTree walk order). Ashley bounty-driven (`preserve-convo-list-order-especially-pins` — pinned, verbatim "keep ordering the same in convo list, especially in pins"). Widened mid-discussion to universal alphabetical verbatim: "what I was going to argue for is just always alphabetical, no matter where you're looking, like we're talking alphabetical under a certain host, if it's not pinned yet or alphabetical, if things are active or alphabetical, if they're pinned. So you can always have some stability and even finding things easier in a long list because you know what the alphabet is." Root cause the fix closes: `computeSnapshot`'s Tier 1 + Tier 2 iterated in TWO passes (openTabs first, fleetSyntheticRows second), so a row's position in a tier depended on WHICH SOURCE currently owned it — clicking a fleet-only row to make it an openTab teleported the row within its tier. Same latent bug shape in Tier 3's byHostId buckets. Universal alphabetical kills all three at once and gives Ashley alphabet-based muscle memory. **Fix (commits `332aed5` + `b508312`):** module-scoped `compareByLabel` helper in `src/ui/state/conversation-store.ts` (`(a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: "base" })`) referenced by all four sort sites: (a) `activeSetRows.sort(...)` after both source loops, (b) `pinned.sort(...)` after both source loops, (c) per-host `rows.sort(...)` inside the `orderedHosts` walk AND the orphan-host fallback loop over `byHostId` (both sorted BEFORE `grouped.push`), (d) `rdpRows.sort(...)` after both the hostTree pass + orphan `hostsFlat` pass, BEFORE the sentinel `grouped.push({ hostId: "__rdp__", ... })`. Header comment at `conversation-store.ts:6-7` rewritten to replace the outdated "No new sort rule, no recency shuffle, no alphabetical override" bullet with a bullet documenting the new alphabetical rule (locale-aware, case-insensitive, numeric-natural, applies within-bucket at every tier; host ORDER unchanged). Test file `conversation-store.test.ts`: hit-by-hit audit of all 28 `activeSet\[|pinned\[|grouped\[` grep hits documented in SUMMARY.md — 2 hits updated (lines 816, 820 — Test 25 union-rendering multi-row assertion where labels `terminal:hostA` vs `scratch` reordered from source-order `[t1, scratch]` to alphabetical `[scratch, t1]`); 26 hits left unchanged (single-row buckets, hostId/hostName/length assertions, or same-label ties). ONE new regression test `"pinned tier is alphabetically sorted by row.label regardless of source"` asserting two openTabs `["z","m"]` in source-order + two fleet sessions `["a","n"]` in source-order → `snap.pinned.map(r => r.label)` deep-equals `["a","m","n","z"]`; would fail against pre-change store which emitted `["z","m","a","n"]`. Panel test `PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx` audited but NOT modified — panel tests mock `useConversations` return directly, so the store sort logic doesn't reach them; mocks pass through unchanged. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 (per Tina L456 preference — frontend tsc alone doesn't catch backend TS errors); `npm run build` EXIT 0; targeted `npx vitest run` on `conversation-store.test.ts` + `PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx` = 101 passed; full `npx vitest run --reporter=verbose` = 875 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed across 77 files (up from #217 baseline of 874, +1 = new pinned-alphabetical regression test — checks out); independent grep of `/tmp/fullsuite.log` per tina.md L508 preference (#209→#211 arc) — 0 real failure markers (all `FAIL|failed|✗` matches are inside passing test descriptions like `upload_failed`, `failed attempts`, `fetch failed`, or expected backend log lines from passing error-path tests). Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `332aed5` (implementation + header comment) + `b508312` (test updates + regression test). Scope check: `git diff --name-only HEAD~2 HEAD` shows exactly the 2 declared files, both under `src/ui/state/` — no backend, no identity-side, no scope creep. Post-commit: bare push to `origin/feat/tab-title-from-tmux` = `bd0dc04..b508312` (authorized per Ashley 2026-07-30 refinement — downstream consumption via Stacy/ceo-skynet, near-zero-blast-radius; skynet-ec2 push→build→recreate motion still HELD). Ships as patch #218 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. So it's similar to the send button, you know, instead of trying to like put an actual button on the left side." — so the design shifted from "sibling row-2 button" to "inside-textarea absolute button" matching Send's #129 wrapper-specificity workaround pattern (bare `<button>` not shadcn Button; `absolute left-1 bottom-0.5` mirroring Send's `right-1 bottom-0.5`; `p-2` for 40×40 hit target around a `size-6` 24×24 Paperclip icon matching Send's paper-plane, NOT the removed aux-group's `size-4`). Four edits shipped atomically in commit `575baff`: (1) removed the `{showPaperclip && <Button ...>}` block from Row 1's `flex flex-row gap-1` aux group (was ~L1345-1378) and updated the group's leading comment to reflect the move + preserve Patch #83/#84 markers; (2) added a new bare `<button type="button">` inside the Row 2 `<div className="relative flex-1 self-stretch">` wrapper as a sibling of the Send button, with `aria-label="Attach file"` / `title="Attach file"` / `onClick={handleOpenFilePicker}` / `disabled={canSend===false || asideActive===true || recycleActive===true}` all preserved VERBATIM so existing paperclip tests (Tests 3/4/5 in `ComposeBox.test.tsx` checking `getByLabelText(/attach file/i)`) keep passing unchanged, plus subtle color `text-[rgba(240,235,224,0.3)] hover:text-[rgba(240,235,224,0.9)]` mirroring Send's non-morphed branch and matching disable/transition classes; (3) added conditional `showPaperclip && "pl-10"` to the Textarea className adjacent to the existing `"pr-10"` (patch #129) so the textarea's LEFT reserves 40px hit-target space for the new Paperclip only when it renders — desktop-without-paperclip loses no usable width; (4) added Test 4b regression guard in `ComposeBox.test.tsx` between Test 4 and Test 5 asserting Paperclip's closest `.relative.flex-1` ancestor also contains the Send button — fails if a future refactor silently moves Paperclip back to Row 1 or into a separate wrapper.
