| 260730-ujq | Copy-to-clipboard button on code blocks and blockquotes inside pretty-view chat messages. Ashley bounty-driven (`code-blocks-copy-button` — parked earlier session via `/bounty`, greenlit as first of the pinned-slate work-through-all-pinned-bounties arc she named this session). New file `src/ui/features/pretty-view/CopyableBlock.tsx` (~122 lines) — small React wrapper mounting a hover-visible copy button as an overlay on `<pre>` / `<blockquote>` renderers; `navigator.clipboard.writeText` on click with `window.electronClipboard` fallback (matches the three existing callsites at `HostKeyVerificationDialog.tsx:42`, `WarpgateDialog.tsx:34`, `ConnectionLog.tsx:69` — deliberately did NOT reach for `RobustClipboardProvider` which is xterm-specific and has focus-listener side effects); "Copy" ↔ "Copied" (with checkmark) label swap that reverts after 1500ms via a single `setTimeout` with a cleanup ref so unmount-during-copied doesn't leak. Styling via inline Tailwind arbitrary-value classes drawing from the pv-* palette (`bg-white/[0.06]`, `text-[color:var(--color-pv-fg)]`, `border-[color:var(--color-pv-border-quiet)]`) — deliberately did NOT touch `src/ui/index.css` (task-shape's mention was permissive; the tokens are consumed inline in the pretty-view Tailwind convention). Hover/touch discriminator uses pure CSS media queries (`@media (hover: hover)` for hover-visibility + `[@media(pointer:coarse)]:opacity-100` for always-visible on touch) instead of the `useIsTouchDevice` hook — plumbing a hook through a `ReactMarkdown` `components` override would require prop-drilling or context, CSS is simpler and matches the "SOLE mobile-vs-desktop discriminator" note at `ComposeBox.tsx:193`. Wiring in `src/ui/features/pretty-view/ChatMessage.tsx` extends the existing `components={{ ... }}` override map on the `<ReactMarkdown>` invocation (already the extension pattern in use at lines 168-179 for `a`, `p`, etc.) with two new slots: `pre: CopyableBlock` and `blockquote: CopyableBlock`. Import added at top. `<ReactMarkdown>` was already loaded with `remark-gfm` — no new deps, no new imports beyond the local `CopyableBlock`. **Executor auto-fixes (per SUMMARY.md decisions block):** (1) fixed `textContent` extraction to clone the wrapper and remove the copy button before reading — otherwise the "Copy"/"Copied" button label would bleed into the clipboard payload, defeating the feature. (2) used a `window.navigator` Proxy pattern in tests (not `Object.defineProperty`) because in vitest+jsdom, the component module sees the real jsdom `Clipboard [EventTarget]` through a different reference chain than the test module, so patching `navigator.clipboard` directly had no effect on what the component called. Tests: 8 new behavior tests (A-F) in `CopyableBlock.test.tsx` covering render, click-copy on both clipboard paths, 1500ms Copied-state revert, textContent extraction correctness, swallowed-rejection safety on clipboard error. 3 new regression-guard tests (G-I) in `ChatMessage.test.tsx` inside a new describe block confirming: `<pre>` renders wrapped with copy button, `<blockquote>` renders wrapped with copy button, injected-turn / quick-reply / ThumbsUp short-circuits remain untouched. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; CopyableBlock.test.tsx 8/8; ChatMessage.test.tsx 11/11 (8 pre-existing + 3 new); full `npx vitest run` = 872 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed (up from prior #214 baseline of 861 — +8 CopyableBlock + +3 ChatMessage = +11, checks out); log grep-gate `FAIL\|failed\|✗\|Unhandled Rejection` returned zero real failures per the just-promoted L508 learned preference (don't trust bare "0 failed" summary lines, especially on patches touching new async surface area). Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `0212822` (Task 1 — CopyableBlock component + tests) + `26cd804` (Task 2 — ChatMessage wiring + regression tests). Scope check: `git diff --name-only HEAD~2 HEAD` shows exactly the 4 declared files, all under `src/ui/features/pretty-view/` — no `src/backend/` touch, no `~/.claude/identities/tina/**` touch, no scope creep into `src/ui/index.css`. NO push, NO docker build, NO deploy — stopped at commit boundary per fleet rule (Ashley 2026-07-27). Ships as patch #215 in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md`. | 260730-sjf | Fixed the session-recycling overlay firing spuriously on host-reachability trouble — two categorical fixes shipped as one arc. Ashley bounty-driven (`recycling-overlay-fires-when-host-unreachable` — parked earlier this session via `/bounty`, greenlit after diagnosis). Root cause: Layer-2 discovery repoll's `.then()` callback in `src/backend/claude-session/claude-session-server.ts` conflated "we couldn't reach the host to ask" with "we asked and Claude isn't there" — both funneled into `transitionToHolding("discovery_diff")` and armed the SessionHoldingOverlay after its 350ms delay-arm. Once armed on a false signal, the next tick returned `active`+SAME sessionFile → fell into the "same file, still active — no state change here" branch that deliberately did NOT clear holding → overlay stuck for the full 5-min holdingTimeout (or until user hit Reset). Two categorical feeders of the false-arm: (i) `exec_error` from the three SSH round-trip try/catches inside `discoverClaudeSession` (walk / PID-file / JSONL test), (ii) `no_tmux_session` from `queryPanePid` returning null on ANY SSH throw at `src/backend/ssh/tmux-helper.ts:219`. **Fix A (commit `4d3ef07`) — narrow the arm at the backend.** `queryPanePid` split its single catch into a two-case contract: `execCommand` throw → RETHROW so the caller can classify; unparseable/empty pane_pid → return null (legitimate "no such pane"). JSDoc updated with the two-case contract. `queryNewestTmuxSession`'s catch left unchanged (backward-compat with its own callers). `discoverClaudeSession` wraps the `queryPanePid` call in try/catch — SSH throw → `{status:"inactive", reason:"exec_error"}` (same category as the three existing SSH-throw catches); genuine null → existing `no_tmux_session` return unchanged. `ClaudeSessionDiscoveryResult` JSDoc block updated to note `exec_error` is the categorical "we couldn't reliably ask" signal funneling all four SSH-throw sites. `claude-session-server.ts` repoll callback hoists `const isExecErrorTick = result.status === "inactive" && result.reason === "exec_error"` at top of `.then` body — inactive branch now switches on reason: `exec_error` = silent tick (NO transitionToHolding, early return); all other reasons (`not_claude`, `no_pid_session_file`, `no_open_session_file`, `no_tmux_session`) keep existing `transitionToHolding("discovery_diff")` behavior. `holdingTicks++` block guarded on `!isExecErrorTick` so SSH failures don't burn the 5-minute holding budget on ticks with no diagnostic signal. Two tests added to `session-file-discovery.test.ts` (Cases 13+14). **Fix B (commit `90e1d85`) — self-clear on same-file recovery.** New WS event variant `{type:"session_holding_cleared"}` added as `SessionHoldingClearedEvent` type + wired into `ClaudeSessionServerEvent` union in `src/ui/api/claude-session-api.ts`. New backend helper `transitionFromHoldingToActiveSameFile()` modeled on `transitionToHolding` (matching idempotency guard `if (changeoverState !== "holding") return;`) — flips `changeoverState="active"`, resets `holdingTicks=0`, emits the new WS frame (same try/catch shape as transitionToHolding), logs `claude_session_holding_cleared` at info level. Call site: repoll active-branch's same-file fall-through path now `else if (changeoverState === "holding")` on the same-file compare invokes the new helper. Real SIGTERM-fallback recycles unaffected because they take the different-file branch first. Frontend: new `case "session_holding_cleared"` in PrettyView's `ws.onmessage` switch calls `setIsHolding(false)` + `setHoldingTimeoutError(false)` ONLY — deliberately does NOT wipe messages / contextPct / harnessTasks / backgroundedAgents / plan_pending / asideText / setStatus (the point is the session did NOT actually change; message stream survives verbatim). Distinct from case `session_changed` which is heavy-reset for a real recycle. **Auto-fixes (per SUMMARY.md decisions block):** (1) rather than mock full WS server + SSH pair (7+ dependency mocks to reach `connectToPane`), the executor exported a module-scope `__applyRepollResultForTests` pure test seam that takes state + helpers as injectable parameters, mirroring the existing `__handleIdentityCountBountiesForTests` pattern — the codebase's established test-seam idiom. All 5 spec-required branch cases (a-e) covered plus 4 additional sub-cases in the new `claude-session-server.repoll.test.ts` (9 tests total). (2) PrettyView F3 contextPct-preservation test initially asserted `container.textContent.toContain('42')` and failed because contextPct renders via `aria-valuenow={contextPct}` on the context bar, not as visible text; fixed to `container.querySelector('[aria-valuenow="42"]')`. 3 new PrettyView tests total (F1: overlay unmounts on session_holding_cleared while isHolding=true; F2: messages preserved verbatim; F3: contextPct preserved via aria-valuenow). Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0, zero errors (critical because `tsc --noEmit` alone doesn't catch backend TS errors per patch #154 rule); `npm run build` EXIT 0, 4.39s; full `npm test` = 76 files / 861 passed / 6 skipped / 0 failed; log grep-gate `FAIL|failed|✗` returned 0 real failures (verified per the just-promoted L508 learned preference from #211 arc — don't trust summary lines without a spot-check). Note: the 5 unrelated ComposeBox.voice.test.tsx failures noted in the prior task's row (from patch #209's jsdom .catch bug) are already gone at this branch's tip, resolved by patch #211's `9213a5b`. Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `4d3ef07` (Fix A backend narrowing) + `90e1d85` (Fix B self-clear). Scope check: `git diff --name-only 9213a5b..HEAD` shows exactly the 8 declared files (tmux-helper.ts, session-file-discovery.ts, claude-session-server.ts, claude-session-api.ts, PrettyView.tsx, session-file-discovery.test.ts, claude-session-server.repoll.test.ts, PrettyView.test.tsx) — no scope creep, no /opt/ touch, no `~/.claude/identities/` touch. Mirrors patch #137's session-working-store pattern byte-for-byte: new module-scoped `src/ui/state/session-recycling-store.ts` with `publishSessionRecycling(key, isRecycling)`, `useSessionRecycling(key)` (useSyncExternalStore hook with null-key short-circuit), `getSessionRecyclingSnapshot()` test-only accessor, `__resetForTest()`. Consumer: `PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx` `PrettyConversationRowLive` reads `useSessionRecycling(sessionKey)` alongside existing `useSessionWorking(sessionKey)`; single key `${host.id}:${targetTmuxSession ?? ""}` resolves both stores. Coerces null→false at forward with `isRecycling === true` since row prop is strict boolean-optional. | 260730-ptd | Wired 4 Google Material Design audio-feedback sounds (start/stop/cancel/error) into the compose-box voice-recording flow at `src/ui/features/pretty-view/useVoiceRecording.ts`. Ashley bounty-driven ("sounds for mic functionality" — parked 2026-07-29 via `/bounty`, picked up this session). Sourced from Google's Material Design sound library (CC-BY-SA 4.0, unmodified from https://archive.org/details/material-design-sound-resources) after Ashley rejected two prior audition rounds (Mixkit legacy notification tones + uisfx modern packs). Chosen for the "big-tech app that actually gets it" aesthetic she was pointing at — short percussive taps, no melodic content, purpose-built for start-of-action feedback. Meta's Sound Kit was denied access on their Drive folder so not viable. Pairing: `state-change_confirm-down` → start (feels like purposeful press to arm recording); `state-change_confirm-up` → stop (Material's designed sonic pair to confirm-down, resolves the down-press cleanly); `navigation-cancel` → cancel (literally named for the purpose); `alert_error-02` → error (middle of the 3 error variants, shortest file, likely subtlest — easy swap to -01 or -03 if it feels wrong later). Task 1 (`be3c573`): copied 4 unmodified MP3s into new `src/ui/assets/sounds/mic/` dir + wrote `CREDITS.md` with attribution text covering source URL, license URL, unmodified-from-source note, and verbatim original→destination filename mapping. Source-tree attribution only for now; UI-visible credit surface (About page etc.) deferred to whenever About surface is next touched. Task 2 (`5c59743`): 4 Vite `?url` imports at hook top (built into Vite, no config change); 4 `useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null)` slots with inline lazy-init guards (hook mount is the only construction site, refs survive re-renders without reconstruction — cheaper than useMemo and no re-render triggers); `playSound(audio)` helper centralizes `currentTime = 0` reset (enables replay without seeking on rapid-fire transitions) + `.play().catch(() => {})` silent-failure (audio failure is UX polish, MUST NOT disrupt recording — Safari can reject if user hasn't yet interacted with the page, and a failed play() throwing would tank a working recording). Placements per plan spec (referenced by function name in `useVoiceRecording.ts`): start.mp3 in the `start()` `.then()` callback AFTER `recorder.start()` + `setState("recording")` AND ONLY on success path — permission-denied path (getUserMedia rejection) NEVER plays start.mp3 because it never enters the `.then` (per plan constraint: "so we don't play on permission-denied paths"; also explicitly regression-tested); stop.mp3 as the very FIRST statement in `endAppend()` AND `endSend()` after their state guards (BEFORE any `await`, giving user immediate feedback before the STT network round-trip latency); cancel.mp3 as the first statement in `cancel()` after its guard (BEFORE `stopRecording()` teardown); error.mp3 in all 3 failure branches of `transcribeBlob()` (fetch throw, !res.ok, invalid JSON) + the empty-transcript guard in endAppend/endSend. iOS silent-switch handling: ZERO code added — HTML5 `<audio>` on iOS Safari uses the ambient audio-session category which honors the physical silent switch by default; adding explicit AudioSession config would only be needed if we wanted sounds to bypass the switch (which we don't, per plan spec). Explicitly out of scope (deferred): mute toggle, transcript-ready chime (5th sound), UI-visible attribution surface. Test suite `useVoiceRecording.test.ts` grew 8→14 tests: `Audio` constructor mocked using a function-declaration `class`-shape (NOT `vi.fn().mockImplementation(() => ({}))` — vitest arrow mocks are not valid constructors, `new AudioMock()` on an arrow throws; this scarred the executor mid-test-write and is captured as a Rule-3-adjacent implementation decision in SUMMARY.md's decisions block); each new test uses `mock.invocationCallOrder` to prove ordering guarantees (start.mp3 AFTER recorder.start(); stop.mp3 BEFORE fetch call in both endAppend AND endSend; cancel.mp3 BEFORE recorder.stop()). Regression tests: getUserMedia rejection path never invokes start.mp3.play(); HTTP 500 response invokes error.mp3.play(); network error invokes error.mp3.play(); rejected-play() promise is silently swallowed AND state still transitions to "recording" (proving audio failure doesn't corrupt state). Verification: full frontend `npx vitest run` = 74 files / 841 passed (+6 vs baseline of 835) + 6 skipped / 0 failed; `npx tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; hook grep gates from plan's done-block all pass. Backend build NOT run (patch touches zero backend files — per patch #154 rule, frontend-only patches validated with `tsc --noEmit` + vitest). Scope check: `git diff --name-only HEAD~2 HEAD` shows only 7 declared files (4 MP3s + CREDITS.md + hook + test) — no `src/backend/` touched, no `~/.claude/identities/tina/**` touched by the executor. | 260730-mzj | Replaced the mtime-based JSONL discovery step in `src/backend/claude-session/session-file-discovery.ts` (`discoverClaudeSession` Step 5) with a per-PID session-file lookup, so pretty-view stops mis-serving one agent's transcript when another agent shares its cwd on the same box. Ashley reported clicking Aqua on Workstation → seeing Wilma's session bubbles; queried `/sessions/list` from tina's admin cookie and confirmed Workstation has 20 tmux sessions incl. both aqua and wilma, both launching claude from the same home cwd → JSONLs colliding in the shared `~/.claude/projects/-home-<user>/` slug directory → old `ls -t | head -n 1` picking whichever wrote most recently (Wilma). This is a recurrence of the archived bounty `pretty-view-shows-wrong-session-jsonl` (dropped 2026-07-26 when Nelly's trigger — an unattended backup-review timer sharing her cwd — was retired; its own timeline note predicted "re-open only if another unattended-claude-in-shared-cwd pattern reappears," and it did). Fix leverages Claude Code v2.1.150+'s per-PID note at `~/.claude/sessions/<PID>.json` — `{"pid":..., "sessionId":<UUID>, "cwd":<abs-path>, ...}` — where `sessionId` IS the JSONL filename. New Step 5 flow: `cat ~/.claude/sessions/<PID>.json` (with `---HOME---\n$HOME` delimiter appended in the same exec to avoid a second roundtrip), parse JSON, extract `sessionId` + `cwd`, slugify `cwd` via `replace(/[./]/g, "-")` (byte-identical semantics to old `sed 's|[./]|-|g'`), construct `$HOME/.claude/projects/<slug>/<sessionId>.jsonl`, verify existence with `test -f` in a second exec. Steps 1-4 (`queryPanePid` → BFS descendant-tree walk → parse `claude` PID) preserved byte-identical, including the load-bearing awk `;`-separator comment that scarred patch #170→#174 (fleet-wide outage from JS `+`-concat of awk statements without terminators). New Step 5 script block carries a matching JS-concat discipline comment that cross-references the walkScript's comment so a future maintainer doing a "cleanup" pass understands both blocks share the same assembly hazard. `ClaudeSessionDiscoveryResult` inactive `reason` union widened with `"no_pid_session_file"` (missing / unreadable / malformed / no `sessionId` field on the PID note); existing `"no_open_session_file"` reused for the narrower case where `sessionId` resolved but the `.jsonl` file doesn't exist on disk. Consumer at `claude-session-server.ts` uses generic `result.status` / `result.reason` branching (no exhaustive switch), so no consumer changes were required — the widened union stays a discriminated-union-safe extension. Old `ls -t $HOME/.claude/projects/<slug>/*.jsonl | head -n 1` dropped entirely — Ashley confirmed 2026-07-30 that no fleet box runs pre-2.1.150 Claude, so no backcompat fallback needed (single code path). Discovery-repoll ticker at `claude-session-server.ts:2201-2267` explicitly NOT retired — even with correct-first-time PID-file lookup, the ticker still serves the "pane's claude PID changed after session recycle, re-discover" case; out of scope for this task. Task 2: full rewrite of `src/backend/claude-session/session-file-discovery.test.ts` — 12 tests total (6 preserved walk-step cases + 6 new Step 5 branches). New tests: Test 7 = happy path with explicit slug-transform assertion (verifies `/home/foo` + `. ` in path both slugify correctly and land at the right JSONL path); Test 8 = PID-file missing / cat returned no delimiter → `no_pid_session_file`; Test 9 = PID-file malformed JSON → `no_pid_session_file`; Test 10 = PID-file present but missing `sessionId` field → `no_pid_session_file`; Test 11 = `sessionId` resolved but `test -f` says the JSONL is absent → `no_open_session_file`; Test 12 = PID-file exec rejects (SSH error) → `exec_error`. `mockExecCommand` now dispatches on `".claude/sessions/"` and `'if [ -f "'` substrings; the old `"readlink -f"` / `"ls -t"` / `"/proc/"` dispatch keys deleted along with the source code they exercised. Verification: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0, `npm run build` EXIT 0 (backend + frontend both — strict per patch #154 rule; frontend `tsc --noEmit` alone doesn't catch backend TS errors), `npx vitest run src/backend/claude-session/session-file-discovery.test.ts` 12/12 pass, sibling suites (`claude-session-server.count-bounties.test.ts`, `claude-session-server.aside.test.ts`, `claude-session-server.aside.integration.test.ts`, `identity-artifact-reader.test.ts`) 41/41 pass — no consumer regressions. Two new lucide-react buttons (Target = `/bounty`, ListPlus = `/queue`) inserted between the existing Lightbulb "explain" quick-reply and the Hourglass queue-until-idle button in `src/ui/features/pretty-view/ComposeBox.tsx` (import extended L2 with `Target, ListPlus` alphabetized into the named import; button pair added L~1471-1541 between Lightbulb's closing `</Button>` and the leading `{/* Patch #84: Queue button …` comment; order is Target FIRST then ListPlus per spec). Both buttons mirror the Lightbulb button's structure verbatim: `size="icon-sm"`, `variant="outline"`, identical `className` block (warm-neutral Glass gradient + hover variants + `max-md:size-12 [&_svg]:max-md:size-6` mobile bump). `onClick` handlers wire straight into the existing `handleSend(overridePayload?: string)` seam at `ComposeBox.tsx:795` — Target calls `handleSend("/bounty " + text)` and ListPlus calls `handleSend("/queue " + text)` with a single space between prefix and text. Using the overridePayload seam means D-50 newline collapse (Ink safety), COMPOSE-04 hard-lock (no optimistic bubble on send), attachment branching, and textarea-clear-on-success all apply automatically — no new state, no new function, no duplication of send-path plumbing. Disable predicate: `text.trim() === "" || canSend === false || asideActive === true || recycleActive === true` — spec only required the first two clauses (need content to prepend + match aside-morph disable pattern), but planner widened it to include the last two clauses to match every other WS-side-effecting aux-row button (ThumbsUp/Lightbulb/Hourglass all include `canSend` and `recycleActive`); planner's discretion, documented in PLAN's `<context>`. `aria-label` + `title`: `"Send with /bounty prefix"` for Target, `"Send with /queue prefix"` for ListPlus — mirrors the existing `Send with /id reset prefix` convention at line 1203, clustering the new buttons semantically with the Reset cell as the "prefix modifier" family of affordances (distinct from ThumbsUp/Lightbulb which send canned text ignoring textarea contents). Leading block comment above Target explains prefix-send affordance category. Leading block comment above ListPlus acknowledges naming collision — the existing Hourglass button (patch #84) is ALSO internally called "queue" but is a LOCAL "queue-until-idle" mechanic (single-slot pending message that fires when target session goes idle for 3s), semantically completely different from the new `/queue` slash-command which is a fleet-wide agent-side skill that files a task in the target agent's harness task list. Rename of Hourglass is explicitly out of scope; disambiguation via distinct icons + tooltips + inline naming-collision comment. | 260730-jes | Made deactivating an active conversation feel instant. Root cause: `doCloseTab(id)` in `src/ui/AppShell.tsx:1144` batched four state mutations (`setActiveTabId` + `selectConversation` + `setPaneTabIds` + `setTabs`) into a single React 18 commit alongside the Zustand `removeFromActiveSet(row.id)` that `handleRowDeactivate` at `PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx:453` had already fired. React rendered the whole batch as one commit — unmounting the deactivated PrettyView AND mounting the newly-activated conversation's PrettyView (fresh WS setup, backfill dispatch, hundreds of message bubbles, ResizeObserver spin-up, aside probe). The list paint was blocked behind all that mount work → ~1s frozen UI on Deactivate tap. Backend `deleteOpenTab(instanceId)` was already fire-and-forget async — NOT the bottleneck. Fix: added `startTransition` to the React named import (first `startTransition` use in the codebase — verified via `grep -rn "startTransition\|useTransition" src/` returned zero pre-change) at `AppShell.tsx:7`. Reshaped `doCloseTab` so the synchronous prelude (`tabs.find(...)`, `deleteOpenTab(...).catch(() => {})`, `terminalRefs.current.delete(id)` — read-only or Map-mutation, no React state) runs first, then a single `startTransition(() => { ... })` callback wraps all four state mutations plus the `remaining` / `nextId` computation that reads render-time closure. React now commits the urgent Zustand active-set removal (driving the list paint) as an urgent update FIRST, then commits the tab switch (triggering the expensive pane unmount+mount) as a transition. List settles + paints immediately; new pane fills in async. Accepted trade-off: for a fraction of a second between "list settled" and "new pane mounted," the right pane briefly shows the just-deactivated pretty view — acknowledged by Ashley ("the user isn't actually waiting on the session to be sort of unloaded"). 6-line WHY/WHAT/TRADE-OFF block comment above the `startTransition(...)` call names the intent + the "DO NOT REVERT to synchronous" warning + references bounty #5 so a future reader recognizes what's load-bearing. Task 2 test: Test 20H appended at the tail of the existing `describe("PrettyConversationsPanel: deactivate action (quick-260727-gm3)", ...)` block in `src/ui/features/pretty-conversations/PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx`. Uses fleet-derived fixture row (host id `"7"` numeric-parseable to match `fleetRowId(parseInt(row.host.id, 10), ...)` shape after a planner-caught NaN fix from initial `"h1"` fixture; matches Test 20F's pattern; tmuxSession `"ordering-session"`) exercising BOTH `removeFromActiveSet` calls (row.id purge AND fleet::7::ordering-session purge). Asserts via `vi.spyOn` + `mock.invocationCallOrder`: `removeFromActiveSetSpy` called twice with `toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, "active-h")` and `(2, "fleet::7::ordering-session")`, `onDeactivateRow` called with the full row object, and the ordering contract `removeFromActiveSetSpy.mock.invocationCallOrder[0] < onDeactivateRow.mock.invocationCallOrder[0]` — the CALLER-side ordering guarantee that keeps the two-commit React split working end-to-end. This test defends against a future refactor swapping the order in `handleRowDeactivate` and collapsing the two commits back into one. Test path chosen (extend existing 1866-line PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx) over creating a new AppShell.test.tsx from scratch, which would have required ~50 lines of new scaffolding for AppShell's ~30 imports — disproportionate to the ordering assertion size. Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `8ceffca` (feat: impl — AppShell.tsx +42/-27 across L7 import + L~1153-1194 body reshape, net +15) and `7ed9fe5` (test: Test 20H — PrettyConversationsPanel.test.tsx +55/-0 across L~957-1011). | 260729-ig7 | Bar-anchored Skynet's pretty-view contextPct scraper regex to close the weekly-limit false-positive that mirrors nelly's vms-apps context-watch pre-fix bug (f88c928, relayed 2026-07-25). Root cause: `src/backend/claude-session/claude-session-server.ts` L2660-2703 scraped Claude Code's tmux status-line context% every ~3s via `tmux capture-pane`, taking the rightmost `(\d{1,3})%` on lines containing `context)`. When Claude Code appends a weekly-limit warning to that same line (e.g. `Opus 4.7 (1M context) │ skynet █████░ 17% ┃ youve used 95% of your weekly limit`), rightmost-wins picks 95 (the warning) instead of 17 (the meter). Rare in practice (weekly warnings uncommon) but the meter is a load-bearing UX signal — a lie in it costs trust in the whole indicator. Fix: extracted the parser to a pure helper `parseContextPct(paneText: string): number | null` in new file `src/backend/claude-session/context-pct-parser.ts` (64 lines, mirrors the `session-file-parser.ts` extract-to-helper pattern in the same directory), with bar-anchored regex `[█▉▊▋▌▍▎▏░]\s*(\d{1,3})%` — the % must be immediately preceded (with optional whitespace) by a Claude Code meter glyph. Live Claude Code only renders `█` and `░` today; the wider partial-fill glyph set matches nelly's regex and is defensive against future variants. Fallback loop's `if (!/[░█]/.test(line)) continue;` line filter dropped as redundant (bar-anchored regex can't match a line without any bar glyph). Range guard (null for <0 / >100 / non-finite) folded into the helper. Semantics preserved: 8-line tail (patch #56), primary `context)`-label pass then bar-glyph fallback, rightmost-per-line + last-matching-line wins across the 8-line window (patch #59's GSD milestone-bar collision fix still discriminates — both meters bar-anchored, rightmost still wins). `claude-session-server.ts` setInterval callback (L2660-2703) now delegates to the helper — 4-condition null/finite/range chain collapsed to `if (pct === null) return;`; interval (3s), `contextPctInFlight` guard, `ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN` guard, `connSnapshot` capture, `execCommand` shape, `ws.send({type:"context_pct", pct})` emit, silent `.catch()`, `.finally()` all unchanged. Wire shape unchanged — PrettyView + ComposeBox untouched. Tests: new `context-pct-parser.test.ts` (89 lines, 10 vitest cases) — (1) primary path single-line bar-anchored → returns meter %, (2) **bug repro** meter + weekly-warning appended → 29 not 95, (3) patch #59 collision case → rightmost bar-anchored wins, (4) `context)` line with warning-only text no bar-adjacent % → null (no fabricated reading), (5) no `context)` no bar glyph unrelated % → null, (6) 100% upper edge → 100, (7) primary/fallback split (bar-anchored NN% but no `context)`) → matched by fallback, (8) 8-line tail (10-line pane, line 1 distractor + line 10 real) → returns line 10, (9) last-matching-line wins semantic, (10) range guard 250% → null. | 260729-hn8 | Fixed the pretty-view screenshot-upload path collision when multiple files with the same original name land in a single batch (bounty `pretty-view-upload-path-collision`, reported 2026-07-24 after Ashley pasted 2 Chrome-clipboard `image.png` files in one paste and the receiving agent saw both message attachments pointing at the SAME finalPath `112220-image.png`; second file silently overwrote the first). Root cause: `src/backend/ssh/pretty-view-upload.ts` `resolveNonCollidingFinal` at L290-302 only stat-checks disk to decide whether to add `-2/-3/…-10` suffixes, and inside `handleUploadStart`'s per-file loop at L465-479, each file `sftpStat`s the SAME candidate path — File 2 sees "not exists" because File 1 has only claimed a `tempPath` (which is uniquely disambiguated by `randSuffix` at L483) but has not yet been renamed to its `finalPath` (that happens at completion via `sftpRename` at L671). Both files then rename to the same final target at completion; second write wins. Fix (batch-local reservation): threaded a `reservedFinalPaths: Set<string>` third argument through `resolveNonCollidingFinal`; every candidate check (primary + `n=2..10` suffix loop) now gates on BOTH `!sftpStat.exists` AND `!reserved.has(candidate)`; declared `const reservedFinalPaths = new Set<string>()` before the per-file loop and `reservedFinalPaths.add(finalPath)` immediately after each successful resolve. Reuses the existing `-2/-3/…` suffix strategy — behaviorally consistent with the on-disk collision path that Test 8 already documents (`143211-log.txt` → `143211-log-2.txt`). Tests added: Test 8b — two `image.png` files in one batch land at `143211-image.png` and `143211-image-2.png` (distinct, order-independent), both temp files write successfully, both rename to distinct final paths at completion, message frame shows two distinct `landingPath` values. Test 8c — three-way collision → `X`, `X-2`, `X-3` (covers the suffix loop iteration). Existing Test 8 disk-collision case still passes — fix is additive to the existing suffix logic, not a replacement. Verification: `npm run build:backend` clean, `npm run build` clean (patch #154 rule: backend files touched → run BOTH), `npx vitest run src/backend/ssh/pretty-view-upload.test.ts` = 15/15 pass. | 260729-1vd | Weekly usage meter — dual-race split-bar (5h + Week) inside `.pv-panel-header` below title row. Design iterated live with Ashley via `?metertry=1` container-injected tuner across variants A-G and positions 1-4, locked on F+4. Each bar: upper half warm-coral usage fill (`linear-gradient(90deg, hsla(38,60%,72%,0.85), hsla(20,72%,66%,0.95))` + `0 0 10px hsla(20,70%,60%,0.45)` glow), lower half cool-cyan elapsed-time fill (`linear-gradient(90deg, hsla(200,50%,60%,0.75), hsla(190,60%,62%,0.85))` + `0 0 8px hsla(190,60%,55%,0.35)` glow), 1px `rgba(0,0,0,0.25)` hairline divider at 50% — "racing" visual so pace-vs-elapsed reads at a glance (usage bar leading time bar = burning fast; lagging = on track). Ashley's original ask verbatim: "we basically have two bars where one is for the five-hour limit and one is for the weekly limit because both are important... each bar is actually split in half long ways so that the top inner bar represents actually how much of the given limit you've used, and the bottom inner bar represents how far along you are in that period of time. So you can kind of see them racing a little bit." Backend adds `/api/usage` Express proxy to Nelly's collector at `http://100.113.23.63:9421/` (source-IP gated to tailnet, no auth; response shape `{five_hour:{used_percentage,resets_at}, seven_day:{...}, updated_at, source_box}`; one Anthropic account = one global bucket that the whole fleet drains from — hence the visible meter). 5s AbortController timeout, 503 `{error:"usage collector unreachable"}` fallback on failure. Nginx routing added in BOTH `docker/nginx.conf` AND `docker/nginx-https.conf` (`location = /api/usage` proxying to `:30001`) per CLAUDE.md hard constraint that missing either causes the 200-with-index.html-and-frontend-crash-on-.map trap. Frontend polls `/api/usage` every 15s via `useEffect` + `setInterval` + AbortController cleanup, computes `elapsedPct = (now_s - (resets_at - window_s)) / window_s * 100` clamped `[0,100]` (window_s = 18000 for 5h, 604800 for 7d — rolling windows, not calendar-anchored; Anthropic-side `resets_at` is authoritative). On fetch failure retains last-known values, renders aria-busy empty container on first-load failure (no crash, no infinite spinner). `.pv-panel-header` restructured from single-row `flex` to `flex-direction:column; align-items:stretch; gap:8px` with new inner `.pv-panel-header-row` wrapper holding the previous `.pv-title` + `.pv-header-actions` content — preserves patch #142 `data-sidebar-toggle-overlaps` conditional padding-left behavior. 4 atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `761b9e7` (backend proxy route + BOTH nginx configs), `fe6da43` (CSS — header restructure + `.pv-usage-meter*` styles verbatim from tuner spec), `9b55766` (`WeeklyUsageMeter.tsx` component + panel wire-up), `91d6eb3` (4 new tests). Test suite green: `npx vitest run src/ui/features/pretty-conversations/` = 4 files / **67 passed** (36 pre-existing panel tests unchanged + 4 new WeeklyUsageMeter tests covering render / elapsed% math (80% ±1% given known now+resets_at) / fetch-failure C1 (first-load → aria-busy empty state) / fetch-failure C2 (subsequent → last-known retained)). Pre-push typecheck per patch #154 fixup lesson: `npm run build:backend` EXIT 0 + `npm run build` EXIT 0 (backend edits touched `src/backend/` so both build gates required). Design source of truth: `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/weekly-usage-meter/metertry-inject.html` `variantDualRace` function — CSS values pulled 1:1. Data source confirmed via Matrix relay DM with Nelly (owner of the collector on thenasty), 4-question audit answered end-to-end (endpoint / semantics / scope / stand-up-vs-consume) before any code touched. Reference bounty and thread: `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/weekly-usage-meter/`. NO push, NO docker build, NO deploy — stopped at commit boundary per fleet rule (Ashley 2026-07-27, "deploy pre-work is not authorized by a code-work ask"). Bundles with sibling commit `9f1fdd4` (selection-indicator brighter D variant — 3px hue ring + 72px glow on `.pv-row.selected`, iterated in the same session via `?seltune=1` tuner) for a single `feat/tab-title-from-tmux` push + full canonicalizing docker build + `docker compose up -d --force-recreate skynet` on Ashley's ship signal. Both patches ship as consecutive numbered entries in `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md` inline at deploy time (fleet rule: docs inline with ship, never batched). | 260728-0uo | Surgical mobile `.prose-sm` font bump for Telegram parity. Ashley's ask verbatim: "make the font bigger across the board on mobile, because Telegram's is bigger, and I like the size of that, if you can find out what it is." Five prototype revs (v1→v5, all served on tailnet 8899 from `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/skynet-transformation/`) drove the design: v1/v2/v3 ruled out font swap (Inter Variable vs system-ui/SF Pro looked identical to Ashley at every candidate size); v4 gave measurements (Skynet coded 14px `text-sm`, Telegram = iOS body 17px via `font: -apple-system-body`, ratio 1.214× at prototype viewport 440×796); v5 loaded Skynet's actual compiled CSS bundle (docker-cp'd out of running container at `/app/html/assets/index-3F5IQHC5.css`) so prototype bubble A was byte-identical to a deployed ChatMessage — Ashley still saw A as MUCH bigger than her real Skynet, ruling out class-list divergence. Final diagnostic: injected `?fontdiag=1` conditional script into container's `/app/html/index.html` (backed up as `index.html.pre-fontdiag.bak`) that measured her real bubble live and displayed on-page. Result: html `fs-lg dark`, html fs 16px, bubble fs 14px, p rect h 25px, viewport **587×1061** (1.334× BIGGER than my prototype's 440×796 on the same phone — explains why identical CSS px looked physically smaller in her Skynet render; still unexplained viewport delta, not the same as PWA-standalone-vs-Safari — she confirmed both look identical to her). Then evolved to `?fonttune=1` live tuner (peach panel with html font-size buttons 16/18/20/22/24/26/28/30/32 that mutated `document.documentElement.style.fontSize` in real-time on the running app) — she picked **html=24** as her Telegram-parity match (which cascades `.prose-sm` from 0.875rem to 21px). | 260727-s8g | Patch #157 — activeSet id-shape purge on deactivate. Ashley UAT'd Phase 15/#149 aftermath and reported: ambient fleet-row tap (e.g. "shrok" on thenasty, hostId=3) promotes the row to Tier 1 with `.active-set` glow (expected); tapping the row's DeactivateAction (X-in-circle) should send it back to its Tier 3 host group as ambient but instead leaves it stuck in Tier 1 with the glow still on — visually indistinguishable from a still-active row. Confirmed via live-DOM diag under `~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/skynet-transformation/deactivate-diag.js`. Root cause: id-shape mismatch in `state.activeSet`. Sequence: (1) ambient fleet-row tap → `addToActiveSet("fleet::HOSTID::SESSIONNAME")` via `fleetRowId()` at `conversation-store.ts:279`; (2) AppShell opens an openTab with a different id shape (`tab-xxx`); Panel's `useEffect([selectedId])` at ~L197 adds `tab-xxx` to activeSet when selectedId flips; (3) activeSet now holds BOTH ids — Tier 1 renders the openTab (fleet-synthetic suppressed by openTabs-wins dedup at store L316), looks fine; (4) `handleRowDeactivate` removed only `row.id` (= `tab-xxx`) and closed the tab; (5) next `computeSnapshot` un-suppresses the fleet-synthetic entry (openTab is gone) and Tier 1 re-promotes it because `state.activeSet.has("fleet::HOSTID::SESSIONNAME")` is still true. Same class of bug as the queued #149 followup-1 pin-nuke (id-shape mismatch scoped to `pinnedIds`), separately queued. **Fix**: exported the existing `fleetRowId` helper as a single source of truth (comment above it at L275-278 already anticipated external callers); Panel's `handleRowDeactivate` now purges BOTH id shapes atomically — `removeFromActiveSet(row.id)` FIRST, then IF (`row.host && row.targetTmuxSession`) `removeFromActiveSet(fleetRowId(parseInt(row.host.id, 10), row.targetTmuxSession))`, then `onDeactivateRow(row)`. Guard skips fleet-id purge for rows without host/session so we never construct a bogus `fleet::null::` string. `removeFromActiveSet` is idempotent so the second call is a safe no-op when only one id shape is present. `parseInt(host.id, 10)` bridges the string→number seam (Host.id: string; fleetRowId(hostId: number)) — matches the store's own bridge at computeSnapshot L296 (`dedupKey(String(parseInt(tab.host.id)), tmux)`). Panel comment block extended with a quick-260727-s8g paragraph documenting the id-shape-mismatch class of bug and referencing the queued #149 followup-1 pin variant. Test 20G (row without targetTmuxSession): fixture `host=valid, targetTmuxSession:null` → click Deactivate → `removeFromActiveSetSpy` called EXACTLY ONCE with `"active-2"`, no call contains `"fleet::"`, no crash. Test 20E untouched. | 260727-r09 | Patch #156 — Gate patches #143 v2 (Terminal) and #148 (PrettyView) visibilitychange handlers on iOS-PWA detection. Full root-cause diagnosis done live with Ashley using DevTools-console diag snippet served on tailnet 8899 (`~/.claude/identities/tina/bounties/chrome-tab-switch-away-forces-manual-reconnect/tab-diag.js`, two iterations to capture message-type strings). Ashley reported Chrome-tab-switch away+back leaves some sessions showing manual "Reconnect / Close" overlay instead of staying connected. First trace (v1 diag) proved close code=1005 wasClean=true (client-side `ws.close()`, not server termination). Second trace (v2 diag) captured message sequence: on visibility→visible, WS #1 receives `sessionCreated → sessionAttached → connected("Session reattached") → disconnected("Connection closed")` in the same millisecond, causing `wasDisconnectedBySSH=true` and the manual overlay. Root chain: patch #143 v2 fires `connectToHost` unconditionally on visibility→visible (was added for iOS PWA where WSes silently die + `readyState` lies). On Chrome desktop, WSes actually stay alive, so force-reconnect closes the OLD WS and opens a NEW one; NEW attaches via `sessionManager.attachWs` (takes over via `sessionTakenOver`), OLD's `ws.on("close")` handler at `terminal.ts:259` then destroys the session via `destroySession` (either directly if isConnected=false, or via `detachWs` timeout), which calls `session.sshConn.end()` — the SSH connection is SHARED (reused at `terminal.ts:403` for the NEW WS), so its `close` event fires on the NEW WS's closure too, which sends `{type:"disconnected",message:"Connection closed"}` at `terminal.ts:2496`. Ashley's insight ("no way this is a Chrome thing") reframed the fix — hiding a tab should be inert, the visibility handler is over-reaching to a class of platforms where its behavior is actively harmful. Fix: keep current force-reconnect on iOS PWA, no-op the handler everywhere else via `isIosPwa()` check (`window.navigator.standalone === true && /iP(hone\|ad\|od)/.test(navigator.userAgent)`). Frontend-only. **Files**: NEW `src/ui/lib/is-ios-pwa.ts` (isIosPwa + SSR guard) + NEW `src/ui/lib/is-ios-pwa.test.ts` (7 tests: 4 combinations of standalone×UA + SSR guard + type-cast round-trip). | 260727-lbr | Aside dismiss clears Claude Code's /btw overlay history before closing it — root cause of Ashley's aside-answer-pollution complaint: aside history within Claude Code's own /btw overlay (not Skynet's state) carried across BTW invocations in the same session, so the model self-referenced prior "please explain" turns and answered as if being asked to re-explain something it had already explained. Fix: change the tmux keystroke sequence sent by the aside-dismiss WS handler from just `Escape` (which closes the overlay but leaves its history intact) to `x` (clears the /btw overlay's in-session history) → 100ms gap → `Escape` (closes overlay). Frontend dismiss path 100% unchanged — the X (Resume) button in ComposeBox.tsx:1506 still fires handleAsideDismiss → WS `{type:'aside_dismissed'}` → backend handler; only the backend's tmux keystroke sequence changed. Change is contained in three code touches: (1) new named exported constant `BTW_CLEAR_HISTORY_KEY = "x"` at claude-session-server.ts near BTW_PROMPT/ASIDE_END_MARKER — lowercase `x` per Ashley's "X button" phrasing (single-key like `f` for fork / `Esc` for close per the ASIDE_END_MARKER footer pattern); if UAT reveals a different key (e.g. `c`, `Ctrl+L`), the constant is the one-line fix so a wrong-key UAT doesn't require a full replan; (2) `sendEscapeToBtw` renamed to `dismissBtw` at claude-session-server.ts:194 — new shape uses TWO `execCommand` calls separated by `await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100))`, mirroring patch #152's `injectBtw` two-call pattern (a batched key burst risks being absorbed as a paste by Claude Code's Ink REPL, so the 100ms gap guarantees the clear-history keystroke and the Escape keystroke arrive as distinct events); 100ms chosen (not 200ms like injectBtw) because dismiss is two single-key presses vs. injectBtw's ~300-char paste — no paste-detection risk on the shorter keys, so the shortest known-good gap suffices; rationale recorded in the constant's docstring so future refactors don't blindly copy 200ms; (3) the ONE production caller at claude-session-server.ts (former line 1705, inside the `aside_dismissed` WS handler) switched from `sendEscapeToBtw(sshConn, currentTmuxSession)` to `dismissBtw(sshConn, currentTmuxSession)`. Test side: 5 new unit tests locking the two-call ordered contract (BTW_CLEAR_HISTORY_KEY sent first, `Escape` sent second, ~100ms gap between with fake timers, log-and-swallow on either-call failure); existing integration test at claude-session-server.aside.integration.test.ts updated to assert the mock execCommand receives BOTH keystrokes in order (not just Escape); PrettyView.tsx doc-comments at L231/305/633 sweep-updated `sendEscapeToBtw → dismissBtw` so `grep -rn "sendEscapeToBtw" src/` is a zero-match tripwire (planner's discretion — cheap insurance against name drift). New `__dismissBtwForTests` test seam mirrors the existing `__injectBtwForTests` convention. Verification all green: `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 at both the mid-task gate and after Task 2; `npx vitest run` → **653/653 across 50 test files** (baseline 648 + exactly 5 new = 4 dismissBtw shape tests + 1 clear-history-key constant assertion; integration test count unchanged — assertion added inside existing test). All grep checkpoints pass: `BTW_CLEAR_HISTORY_KEY` present in server + tests; `dismissBtw` present in server + WS handler; `sendEscapeToBtw` 0 matches in src/ (tripwire holds). WS frame shape `{type:'aside_dismissed', hostId, tmuxSession}` and frontend `handleAsideDismiss` UNTOUCHED (per plan non-negotiable — only the tmux keystroke sequence changed). Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`: `60c1ded` (feat: dismissBtw two-keystroke shape) + `5adad8a` (test: integration + PrettyView doc-comment sweep). Zero other files touched. NO Co-Authored-By trailer (fork convention). No push. No deploy this quick — batched pending Ashley greenlight per "every build → deploy is a new may-I" rule from patch #35, though this is trigger 3 territory (aside-answer-pollution is actively degrading her live use every time she stacks asides in a session) so SOLO-DEPLOY carveout is available on her nod. `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md` catalog write-up deferred to deploy time per batch-writeups-until-deploy rule (catalog is ~19-22 patches deep now including this pair). | 260727-kbw | Phase 15 UAT followup — fleet-pin load-order race fix. Ashley UAT'd Phase 15 (server-side pinned-conversation persistence, shipped 14:20 UTC) with "pins are not working, they last only until I close the app." Tina verified server side was working (curl round-trip via `/user-preferences` GET+PUT confirmed her `fleet::7::aqua` pin IS persisted; deployed AppShell chunk has correct mount-effect wiring: `getPinnedIds()` [minified `Qe`] then `hydratePinnedIdsFromServer` [minified `Ct`]). Root cause: race in `updateOpenTabs` pruner at `src/ui/state/conversation-store.ts:536-547`. On page load: `state.pinnedIds`, `state.fleetSessions` both empty (fleet loads independently async). Panel mount effect fires `getPinnedIds()` microtask → resolves `["fleet::7::aqua"]` → `hydratePinnedIdsFromServer` sets `pinnedIds = {fleet::7::aqua}`. Any subsequent `updateOpenTabs` re-emit (routine WS tab-list update) triggers pruner (now `pinnedIds.size > 0`). If `state.fleetSessions` still hasn't loaded yet, `fleetPinKeepSet` is empty → fleet pin isn't in `nextIds` (never in openTabs — it's fleet), isn't in (empty) `fleetPinKeepSet` → PRUNED. Pin disappears from UI; server still has it; next refresh rehydrates; race repeats. Ashley confirmed the caveat about later empty re-emits doesn't apply — fleet list is loaded ONCE per page and stays that reference for the rest of the session. **Fix (Ashley-designed):** defer the panel's `getPinnedIds()` + `hydratePinnedIdsFromServer()` hydration until fleet sessions have loaded at least once. When fleet has loaded, `fleetPinKeepSet` in any subsequent `updateOpenTabs` will include the fleet pin, and the pruner won't nuke it. **Slice 1** (`e6aede4`): store — added `fleetSessionsLoaded: boolean` to `ConversationState` (default false); `updateFleetSessions()` unconditionally sets `fleetSessionsLoaded: true` on state update (empty array counts as "loaded" — user may genuinely have zero fleet sessions), preserving the existing same-sessions no-op short-circuit for the array itself but forcing `notify()` on false→true flag flip; new `getFleetSessionsLoadedSnapshot()` + exported `useFleetSessionsLoaded(): boolean` via `useSyncExternalStore` mirroring `usePinnedIds()` pattern; new test helper `__resetFleetSessionsForTest()` mirroring existing `__resetPinnedIdsForTest`/`__resetActiveSetForTest` pattern for clean test starting points. 7 new store tests: `fleetSessionsLoaded starts false`; non-empty array flips to true; empty array ALSO flips to true (empty-is-loaded); subsequent identical calls don't re-notify solely on flag; `useFleetSessionsLoaded` returns current value and re-renders on flip; **regression test locking the exact bug**: `updateFleetSessions([{hostId:7, sessionName:"aqua"}])` → `hydratePinnedIdsFromServer(["fleet::7::aqua"])` → `updateOpenTabs([])` with empty tab list → assert `fleet::7::aqua` STILL in `pinnedIds` (would have been nuked pre-fix). **Slice 2** (`fd3efbb`): panel — imported `useFleetSessionsLoaded` + added `useRef` import; replaced the mount effect at PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx:204-218 with fleet-loaded-gated version — subscribes to `fleetSessionsLoaded`, adds `hydratedRef = useRef(false)` guard to prevent double-hydration on any spurious flag re-flip (defense-in-depth even though Ashley confirms it stays true after first flip). Effect gates on `!fleetSessionsLoaded → return`, then `hydratedRef.current → return`, then flips ref true and fires the same `getPinnedIds()` → `hydratePinnedIdsFromServer(ids)` async IIFE with cancelled-guard. Preserved existing Phase 15 (a)/(b)/(c) block comment; added a new (d) paragraph explaining the fleet-loaded gate + the race it closes (tagged with quick-task slug `quick-260727-kbw` for future greppability). New Test 22 mirroring Test 21 mock pattern: mount does NOT call `getPinnedIds` when `fleetSessionsLoaded=false`; when flag flips to true, `getPinnedIds` called exactly once + `hydratePinnedIdsFromServer` called exactly once with resolved ids. Verification all green: `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npx vitest run` → **648/648 across 50 test files** (baseline 647 + 1 new panel test; store file grew 55 → 62 tests including 7 new = +7 store, +1 panel, 0 regressions). All grep checkpoints pass: `fleetSessionsLoaded` 8 hits in store, `useFleetSessionsLoaded` export 1 hit, panel gate + `hydratedRef` 4 hits each. Two atomic commits on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`, NO Co-Authored-By trailer (fork convention). No push, no deploy — Ashley calls the deploy separately per the "every build → deploy is a new may-I" rule from patch #35; this is trigger 3 (actively broken in production Ashley is currently hitting), so SOLO-DEPLOY carveout applies pending her nod. Pre-warn on first-hard-refresh `HTTP2_PROTOCOL_ERROR` (Caddy H2 stream fallout during container recreate — close+reopen tab spawns fresh H2 connection; not a real deploy failure). `~/.claude/identities/tina/skynet-patches.md` catalog write-up deferred per batch-writeups-until-deploy rule (catalog is ~15-18 patches behind now including Phase 15 waves + this fix; all pinned together at deploy-recommendation time). Anti-scope-creep held: no forbidden surface touched. Related bounties: `skynet-transformation` master (Phase 15 UAT followup folds into its timeline per one-project rule — do NOT create sibling bounty). | 2026-07-27 | fd3efbb | [260727-kbw-fleet-pin-load-race](./quick/260727-kbw-fleet-pin-load-race/) |

Root cause: Phase 14's arm-emitter useEffect at `src/ui/features/pretty-view/PrettyView.tsx:909` short-circuited `aside_arm` only when the last user turn's `content.trimStart().startsWith("/id ")` — a check that catches raw-text `/id save` typed into SSH-attached tmux panes (round-trips through JSONL as literal string), but MISSES the harness slash-UI invocation path where `/id tina` lands in JSONL as XML wrappers `<command-message>id</command-message>\n<command-name>/id</command-name>\n<command-args>tina</command-args>` (content starts with `<`, not `/id `). Harness slash-UI is Ashley's PRIMARY /id invocation path from pretty-view, so every /id from that surface armed a phantom aside. Fix: extract module-local const arrow `isIdCommand = (content: string): boolean => content.trimStart().startsWith("/id ") \|\| content.includes("<command-name>/id</command-name>")` above the useEffect + swap the inline check for `if (isIdCommand(lastUserContent)) return;` at the callsite. XML substring `<command-name>/id</command-name>` chosen as the marker because it is not a legal substring of prose — it only appears when the harness itself wraps the command, so false-positive risk is zero. Helper stays module-local (no new shared util file — Phase 14 no-new-deps posture preserved). Test 9 mirrors Test 7 shape with realistic UAT payload (`<command-args>tina</command-args>` sibling) → asserts `aside_arm` NOT called. Tests 1-8 explicitly locked unchanged; Tests 7 (/id-then-real-msg still arms) + 8 (only last user turn matters) remain valid regression guards. TDD gate followed: RED (Test 9 isolated → `expected […aside_arm…] to be undefined`) → GREEN (helper + callsite swap → Test 9 passes) → REFACTOR skipped (helper landed final on first pass) — RED+GREEN combined into single atomic commit matching Phase 14 commit style precedent (`f029e44` also bundles impl+tests). Verification all green: `npm run type-check` clean; `npx vitest run PrettyView.aside.test.tsx` 9/9 (was 8/8 pre-plan); full sweep `npm test` 604/604 across 49 files (was 603/603 — net +1). Grep gates: `isIdCommand` declaration+callsite both present; `<command-name>/id<` XML substring present; Test 9 title present. Frontmatter note (non-blocking): plan `<truths>` said baseline "33 → 34" tests but aside test file's actual baseline was 8 → 9 (33/34 figure appears to have referenced aggregate PrettyView scope) — behavioral truth-set fully satisfied, documented in SUMMARY.md for traceability, no action. Two files, +73/-1 (`src/ui/features/pretty-view/PrettyView.tsx` +17/-1, `src/ui/features/pretty-view/PrettyView.aside.test.tsx` +56/0). Single atomic commit on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`. | 260724-bwi | Patch #148 — PrettyView WebSocket auto-reconnect. Root cause diagnosed 2026-07-24 with Ashley via #147 SkynetLog telemetry: `PrettyView.tsx:447-457` ws.onclose handler had explicit "Do NOT auto-reopen" comment, just set `errorMessage = "Connection closed"` and gave up. When claude-session WS closed for ANY reason (iOS PWA background, deploy container recreate, network flap), no reopen path existed. Terminal.tsx's ssh WS reconnects fine (#143v2 works, proven by 3 clean end-to-end reconnect cycles in the SkynetLog file with ZERO failure entries), but PrettyView's SEPARATE WebSocket to /claude-session/websocket/ was single-shot. Ashley's confirmed workaround: toggle pretty view off + on via key combo = unmount+remount = useEffect re-runs = fresh WS. Fix: mirror Terminal.tsx's proven retry pattern into PrettyView. Concrete changes: (a) new module-scope `MAX_RECONNECT_ATTEMPTS = 5` const, (b) refs `reconnectAttemptsRef`/`reconnectTimeoutRef`/`paneKeyRef`/`statusRef`, (c) `retryKey` state added to the WS-open useEffect deps so bumping it re-runs the effect with cleanup+fresh WS, (d) `paneKeyRef` distinguishes pane-change (full state reset preserved) from retry re-run (state preserved), (e) `statusRef` mirror avoids stale-status closure issues in onclose handler, (f) replaced onclose "Do NOT auto-reopen" body with retry-scheduling logic gated on `statusRef.current !== 'inactive'` and `reconnectAttemptsRef.current < MAX_RECONNECT_ATTEMPTS` with `Math.min(2000 * attempt, 8000)` backoff (2/4/6/8/8s = ~28s total window), (g) ws.onopen clears `errorMessage(null)` and increments cleared attempts (so retries never show transient empty state — errorMessage stays "Connection closed" through backoff until a NEW WS actually opens), (h) new mount-once visibilitychange useEffect: on `document.hidden` clears any pending timer, on visible resets counter to 0 and bumps retryKey if WS not OPEN and status not inactive (direct fix for Ashley's iOS PWA long-background case where retries might burn through while suspended), (i) cleanup fn clears pending reconnectTimeoutRef before ws.close() so unmount never fires stale retries. "inactive" state semantics preserved throughout — no retry, no reopen, no errorMessage clear when server sent an authoritative terminal frame. 4 new tests (retry-on-close fires new WS after 2s and clears errorMessage on onopen; max-5-attempt cap holds; visibilitychange:visible resets counter and reopens even after cap exhaust; inactive state short-circuits ALL retry paths) + 3 existing Phase 05 tests all pass = 7/7 green. | 260723-9nv | Compose-box textarea auto-grow with contents (patch #135) — replaces the newline-count `rows = Math.min(6, text.split("\n").length)` heuristic at ComposeBox.tsx:517 (which left long single-line messages clipped because wrapped visual lines never add a `\n`) with a `useLayoutEffect` on `[text]` that drives `el.style.height` imperatively off `scrollHeight`, capped at 6 line-heights. Mechanic: `el.style.height='auto'` first (mandatory — without it, scrollHeight only grows, never shrinks on delete), then `Math.min(el.scrollHeight, maxHeightPxRef.current)`, then set `overflowY='auto'` only at the cap so the scrollbar appears only when the 6-row ceiling is hit. `maxHeightPxRef` (new `useRef<number|null>(null)`) is computed once on first pass from `parseFloat(getComputedStyle(el).lineHeight) × 6` with 144px fallback for JSDOM `normal`-keyword branch where parseFloat resolves to NaN. `useLayoutEffect` imported alongside existing React hooks. `<Textarea rows={rows}>` → `<Textarea rows={1}>` (SSR/initial-paint fallback; effect immediately overrides on mount). All other styling untouched: `min-h-8!` (32px CSS floor for empty state via the Tailwind v4 important-suffix cascade beats shadcn's `min-h-[80px]` base), `resize-none`, `bg-[rgba(10,12,20,0.5)]!`, `pr-10` (patch #129 inside-Send padding), border, focus ring, warm-black well, aux buttons, meter well, queueArmed overlay, inside-textarea Send button (position anchored to wrapper, not textarea, so no adjustment needed). Both were rendering simultaneously on mobile-in-conversation per Ashley's diag (fixed at 8,8 z-30 + legacy at 0,0). Identity badge already surfaces conversation identity so the label was dead weight too. Bundled cleanup: removed now-unused `activeConversationLabel` derivation, `Separator` + `Button` imports, and dead `isMobileViewScreen` const (Rule 1 dead-code cleanup, executor deviation documented in SUMMARY). Fix ONLY the color; separate follow-up will reclaim ~5/6 of the vertical space per Ashley's ask (trim compose `py-2` + adjust safe-area padding). Fix (d) `PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx`: added `useEffect(() => { if (selectedId) addToActiveSet(selectedId); }, [selectedId])` right after the hoisted `useActiveSet()` call — root cause of Ashley's "active conversations not lighting up / not glowing like the mock" (diag showed ALL 32 rendered rows had `pv-row pv-row--mobile ambient` class, i.e. NONE in activeSet, despite one being her currently-open session): `addToActiveSet` only fired from `handleRowSelect` (click path), so URL-fragment restore into `#tab=tmux:host:session&active=0` bypassed handleRowSelect entirely, left activeSet empty, and rows stayed `ambient` (flat quiet treatment) instead of the full pretty-view bubble glow. New useEffect catches every selectedId change regardless of path (click, URL restore, tab switch, programmatic) because they all funnel through `selectedId`. `addToActiveSet` idempotent so double-fires from click-that-also-changes-selectedId are harmless. Fix (e) `pretty-conversations.css` + `PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx`: added `.pv-panel-group { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }` and applied `pv-panel-group` class to all 3 group wrappers (pinned rows now wrapped in an explicit group div; regular hosts group + RDP-sentinel group converted from bare `flex flex-col`). Root cause of Ashley's "no spacing between list items" was that my patch #142 `.pv-panel-scroll gap:8px` IS applied but only between GROUPS — individual rows within each group's `<div class="flex flex-col">` wrapper were edge-to-edge because the wrappers had no gap. 8px within-group matches 8px between-groups for consistent visual rhythm. Fix (f) `PrettyConversationsPanel.tsx`: removed the mobile-conditional gate on `pv-panel-header` so "Conversations" title renders on mobile too (was suppressed by a `showDesktopTitle`-style gate — Ashley confirmed the prior handoff note "deliberately left off per Phase 10 design" was WRONG and made up in a prior session). | 260724-7ow | SSH terminal auto-reconnect on PWA visibilitychange (patch #143) — fixes iOS PWA backgrounding failure mode where the 8-attempt exponential-backoff reconnect budget (2s→4s→8s cap, ~50s) burns through against a throttled iOS tab while Ashley is on another app, leaving `shouldNotReconnectRef=true` and the manual "Connection lost" overlay stuck even though the target's tmux was up the entire time. New sibling `useEffect` in `Terminal.tsx` placed immediately after the existing lifecycle-reset effect at line 328 (co-located with the reconnect refs it manipulates): (a) on `document.hidden===true`, clears any scheduled `reconnectTimeoutRef` timeout and resets `reconnectAttempts.current=0` so the budget isn't burned against a throttled tab; (b) on `document.hidden===false`, if `ws.readyState !== OPEN` AND `wasDisconnectedBySSH.current===false` AND `isUnmountingRef.current===false`, resets all reconnect state flags (`shouldNotReconnectRef=false`, `isReconnectingRef=false`, `isConnectingRef=false`, `reconnectAttempts=0`, `wasConnectedRef=false`, `wasDisconnectedBySSH=false`), clears `updateConnectionError(null)`, hides overlay via `setShowDisconnectedOverlay(false)`, and calls `connectToHost(terminal.cols, terminal.rows)`. Boundary preserved: `wasDisconnectedBySSH.current===true` (auth fail, target-side ssh session closed, `~.` typed) does NOT auto-reconnect — manual overlay Reconnect stays the affordance. Deliberate divergence from the manual Reconnect handler (Terminal.tsx:3024-3037): NO `terminal.clear()` on the visibility path — tmux repaint on reattach handles restoration, and the clear was the visible-flicker cause in the manual overlay path. Deps: `[terminal, updateConnectionError]` matching file convention (file-wide `eslint-disable react-hooks/exhaustive-deps` at line 1, refs + setState omitted, connectToHost is a stable function declaration). Tests: 1 new structural (Test 6 — regex-pinned baseline of `visibilitychange`/`document.hidden`/`terminal.clear()`/`wasDisconnectedBySSH.current` occurrence counts to catch drift; JSDoc mid-execution rephrased to avoid literal `terminal.clear()` token that would have inflated count from 8→9) + 4 new behavioral in a new `describe('Terminal - patch #143 visibilitychange auto-reconnect', ...)` block using the file's established byte-for-byte helper-mirror pattern (Tests 7/8/9/10 covering hidden-branch cancel+reset, visible-branch full reset+connectToHost fire, target-terminated boundary NO-fire, and WS.OPEN no-op — Test 10 is one of the plan's must_haves.truths so earned its own test). | 260728-r1h | Fix Kiro CLI wrapper defeating claude-session detection — walk pane_pid descendants for `claude` comm. Root cause verified live on thenasty via SSH: AWS Kiro CLI's `kiro-cli-term` pty wrapper sets `argv[0]='bash'` on the shell it launches, so `/proc/<pane_pid>/cmdline` reads `bash (kiro-cli-term)` and tmux's `pane_current_command` returns `bash` even though `/proc/<pane_pid>/comm` is `kiro-cli-term`. Detection at `session-file-discovery.ts:47-51` did strict `trimmedCommand === "claude"` exact-match → returned `not_claude` for every thenasty pane, meanwhile `claude` was running as a grandchild (kiro-cli-term → bash --login → claude). All 5 thenasty identities (beatrice, nelly, shrok, vicky, yolanda) rendered "no active Claude session" fallback in pretty-view. Fix: replaced strict pane_current_command match with a POSIX `ps -eo pid=,ppid=,comm=` + awk fixed-point BFS descendant-tree walk seeded from `pane_pid`. Emits the first PID with `comm='claude'` anywhere in the tree (including `pane_pid` itself as the backcompat direct-claude case). Uses that PID for the existing CWD/JSONL discovery script (unchanged byte-for-byte except `${pid}` → `${claudePid}`). Handles wrappers of arbitrary depth without hardcoding. `queryPaneCurrentCommand` import removed; `pid_unavailable` kept in the type union for backcompat but no longer emitted (folded into `no_tmux_session`). Wire-type unaffected — frontend types `reason` as bare `string` and only switches on `holding_timeout`. Tests: 8 new vitest cases (Kiro wrapper 3-level, deeper 4-level wrapper, direct-claude backcompat, no-claude tree, null pane_pid, walk timeout, CWD reject, CWD empty). Backend typecheck via `npm run build:backend && npm run build` clean (patch #154 lesson honored); 766/766 tests green. Single atomic commit `67f94a4` on `feat/tab-title-from-tmux`.
